1 upanayanādhyāyaḥ



1.01  jayati jagataḥ prasūtirviśvātmā sahajabhūṣaṇaṃ nabhasaḥ|

1.01  drutakanakasadṛśadaśaśatamayūkhamālārcitaḥ savitā||

1.02  prathamamunikathitamavitathamavalokya granthavistarasya artham|

1.02  nātilaghuvipularacanābhirudyataḥ spaṣṭamabhidhātum||

1.03  muniviracitamidamiti yaccirantanaṃ sādhu na manujagrathitam|

1.03  tulye +arthe +akṣarabhedādamantrake kā viśeṣoktiḥ||

1.04  kṣititanayadivasavāro na śubhakṛditi yadi pitāmahaprokt

1.04  kujadinamaniṣṭamiti vā ko +atra viśeṣo nṛdivya*kṛteḥ[K.kṛte]||

1.05  ābrahmādiviniḥsṛtamālokya granthavistaraṃ kramaśaḥ|

1.05  kriyamāṇakameva +etatsamāsato +ato mamotsāhaḥ||

1.06  āsīttamaḥ kiledaṃ tatrāpāṃ taijase +abhavaddhaim

1.06  svarbhūśakale brahmā viśvakṛdaṇḍe +arkaśaśinayanaḥ||

1.07  kapilaḥ pradhānamāha dravyādīn kaṇakabhugasya viśvasy

1.07  kālaṃ kāraṇameke svabhāvamapare jaguḥ karm |

1.08  tadalamativistareṇa prasaṅgavādārthanirṇayo +atimahān|

1.08  jyotiḥśāstrāṅgānāṃ vaktavyo nirṇayo +atra mayā||

1.09   jyotiḥśāstramanekabhedaviṣayaṃ skandhatrayādhiṣṭhitaṃ

1.09  tatkārtsnyopanayasya nāma munibhiḥ saṃkīrtyate saṃhitā|

1.09  skandhe +asmin gaṇitena yā grahagatistantrābhidhānastvasau

1.09  horānyo +aṅgaviniścayaśca kathitaḥ skandhastṛtīyo +aparaḥ||

1.10  vakrānuvakrāstamayodayādyāstārāgrahāṇāṃ karaṇe mayoktāḥ|

1.10  horāgataṃ vistaraśaśca janmayātrāvivāhaiḥ saha pūrvamuktam||

1.11  praśnapratipraśnakathāprasaṅgān svalpopayogān grahasambhavāmśc

1.11  santyajya phalgūni ca sārabhūtaṃ bhūtārthamarthaiḥ sakalaiḥ pravakṣy |



2  sāṃvatsarasūtrādhyāyaḥ



athātaḥ sāṃvatsarasūtraṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ

2.(1) tatra sāṃvatsaro +abhijātaḥ priyadarśano vinītaveṣaḥ satyavāganasūyakaḥ samaḥ susaṃhitopacitagātrasandhiravikalaścārukaracaraṇanakhanayanacibukadaśanaśravaṇalalāṭabhrūttamāṅgo vapuṣmān gambhīrodāttaghoṣaḥ| prāyaḥ śarīrākāra*anuvarttino[K.anuvartino] hi guṇā doṣāśca bhavanti|

2.(2) tatra guṇāḥ---śucirdakṣaḥ pragalbho *vāggmī[K.vāgmī] pratibhānavān deśakālavitsāttviko na parṣadbhīruḥ sahādhyāyibhiranabhibhavanīyaḥ kuśalo +avyasanī śāntikapauṣṭikābhicārasnānavidyābhijño vibudhārcanavratopavāsanirataḥ svatantrāścaryotpāditaprabhāvaḥ pṛṣṭābhidhāyyanyatra daivātyayādgrahagaṇitasaṃhitāhorāgranthārthavetteti|

2.(3) tatra grahagaṇite pauliśaromakavāsiṣṭhasaurapaitāmaheṣu pañcasveteṣu siddhānteṣu yugavarṣāyanartumāsapakṣāhorātrayāmamuhūrta*nāḍī[ū.nāḍīvināḍī]prāṇatruṭitruṭyādyavayava*ādikasya[K.ādyasya] kālasya kṣetrasya ca vettā|

2.(4) caturṇāṃ ca mānānāṃ saurasāvananākṣatracāndrāṇāmadhimāsakāvamasambhavasya ca kāraṇābhijñaḥ|

2.(5) ṣaṣṭyabdayugavarṣamāsadinahorādhipatīnāṃ *pratipatti[K.pratipattivi]cchedavit |

2.(6) saurādīnāṃ ca mānānām *asadṛśasadṛśa[K.sadṛśāsadṛśa]yogyāyogyatvapratipādanapaṭuḥ|

2.(7) siddhāntabhede +apyayananivṛttau pratyakṣaṃ samamaṇḍalalekhāsamprayogābhyuditāṃśakānāṃ [K.ca] chāyājalayantradṛggaṇitasāmyena pratipādanakuśalaḥ|

2.(8) sūryādīnāṃ ca grahāṇāṃ śīghramandayāmyottaranīcoccagatikāraṇābhijñaḥ|

2.(9) sūryacandramasośca grahaṇe grahaṇādimokṣakāladikpramāṇasthitivimardavarṇa*ādeśānām[K.deśānām] anāgatagrahasamāgamayuddhānāmādeṣṭā|

2.(10) pratyekagrahabhramaṇayojanakakṣyāpramāṇaprativiṣayayojanaparicchedakuśalaḥ[K.kuśalo]|

2.(11) bhūbhagaṇabhramaṇasaṃsthānādyakṣāvalambakāharvyāsacaradalakālarāśyudayacchāyānāḍīkaraṇaprabhṛtiṣu kṣetrakālakaraṇeṣvabhijñaḥ|

2.(12) nānācodyapraśnabhedopalabdhijanitavāksāro nikaṣasantāpābhiniveśaiḥ [K.viśuddhasya] kanakasyevādhikataramamalīkṛtasya [K.śāstrasya]vaktā tantrajño bhavati[K.uktañca]|

2.01  na pratibaddhaṃ gamayati vakti na ca praśnamekamapi pṛṣṭaḥ|

2.01  nigadati na ca śiṣyebhyaḥ sa kathaṃ śāstrārthavijjñeyaḥ||

2.02  grantho +anyathānyathā*arthaṃ[K.arthaḥ] karaṇaṃ yaścānyathā karotyabudhaḥ|

2.02  sa pitāmahamupagamya stauti naro vaiśikenāryām||

2.03  tantre suparijñāte lagne chāyāmbuyantrasaṃvidit

2.03  horārthe ca surūḍhe nādeṣṭurbhāratī vandhyā||

<uktan cāryaviṣṇuguptena>

2.04  apyarṇavasya puruṣaḥ prataran kadā cid

2.04 āsādayedanilavegavaśena pāram|

2.04 na tvasya kālapuruṣākhyamahārṇavasya

2.04 gacchetkadā cidanṛṣirmanasāpi pāram||

2.(13) horāśāstre +api [K.ca] rāśihorādreṣkāṇanavāṃśakadvādaśabhāgatriṃśadbhāgabalābalaparigraho grahāṇāṃ diksthānakālaceṣṭābhiranekaprakārabalanirdhāraṇaṃ prakṛtidhātudravyajāticeṣṭādiparigraho niṣekajanmakālavismāpanapratyayādeśasadyomaraṇāyurdāyadaśāntardaśāṣṭakavargarājayogacandrayogadvigrahādiyogānāṃ nābhasādīnām
ca yogānāṃ phalānyāśrayabhāvāvalokananiryāṇagatyanūkāni *tatkāla[K.tātkālika]praśnaśubhāśubhanimittāni vivāhādīnāṃ ca karmanāṃ karaṇam|

2.(14) yātrāyāṃ tu [K.ca] tithidivasakaraṇanakṣatramuhūrtavilagnayogadehaspandanasvapnavijayasnānagrahayajñagaṇayāgāgniliṅgahastyaśveṅgitasenāpravādaceṣṭādigrahaṣāḍguṇyopāyamaṅgalāmaṅgalaśakunasainyaniveśabhūmayo +agnivarṇā mantricaradūtāṭavikānāṃ yathākālaṃ prayogāḥ paradurgopalambhopāyaścetyuktaṃ cācāryaiḥ|

2.05  jagati prasāritamivālikhitamiva matau niṣiktamiva hṛday

2.05  śāstraṃ yasya sabhagaṇaṃ nādeśā *niṣphalās[K.niḥphalās] tasy |

2.(15) saṃhitāpāragaśca daivacintako bhavati|

2.(16) yatra +ete saṃhitāpadārthāḥ|

2.(17.1) dinakarādīnāṃ grahāṇāṃ cārāsteṣu ca teṣāṃ prakṛtivikṛtipramāṇavarṇakiraṇadyutisaṃsthānāstamanodayamārgamārgāntaravakrānuvakrarkṣagrahasamāgamacārādibhiḥ phalāni nakṣatrakūrmavibhāgena deśeṣv*agastyacāraḥ[K.agasticāraḥ]| saptarṣicāraḥ |grahabhaktayo
nakṣatravyūhagrahaśṛṅgāṭakagrahayuddhagrahasamāgamagrahavarṣaphalagarbhalakṣaṇarohiṇīsvātyāṣāḍhīyogāḥ
sadyovarṣakusumalatāparidhipariveṣaparighapavanolkādigdāhakṣiticalanasandhyārāgagandharvanagararajonirghātārghakāṇḍasasyajanmendradhvajendracāpavāstuvidyāṅgavidyāvāyasavidyāntaracakramṛgacakraśvacakravātacakraprāsādalakṣaṇapratimālakṣaṇapratiṣṭhāpanavṛkṣāyurvedodagārgalanīrājana*khañjanaka[K.khañjana]utpātaśānti
mayūracitrakaghṛtakambalakhaḍgapaṭṭakṛkavākukūrmagoajāśvebhapurūṣa[K.ū.puruṣa]strīlakṣaṇāny

2.(17.2) antaḥpuracintāpiṭakalakṣaṇopānacchedavastracchedacāmaradaṇḍa*śayana[K.śayyā]āsanalakṣaṇaratnaparīkṣā dīpalakṣaṇaṃ dantakāṣṭhādyāśritāni śubhāśubhāni nimittāni sāmānyāni ca jagataḥ pratipuruṣaṃ pārthive ca pratikṣaṇamananyakarmābhiyuktena daivajñena cintayitavyāni| na caikākinā śakyante +aharniśamavadhārayituṃ nimittāni| tasmāt
subhṛtenaiva daivajñena anye *+api[K.omitted] tadvidaścatvāraḥ *kartavyāḥ[K.bhartavyāḥ]|tatraikenāindrī cāgneyī ca digavalokayitavyā| yāmyā nairṛtī cānyenaivaṃ vāruṇī vāyavyā cottarā cāiśāni ceti| yasmād*ulkāpātādīni[K.nimittāni] śīghram *apagacchati[K.upagacchati]iti| *tasyāś[K.teṣāṃ]
cākāravarṇasnehapramānādigraharkṣopaghātādibhiḥ phalāni bhavanti|

<uktaṃ ca gargeṇa maharṣiṇā>

2.06  kṛtsnāṅgopāṅgakuśalaṃ horāgaṇitanaiṣṭhikam|

2.06  yo na pūjayate rājā sa nāśamupāgacchati||

2.07  vanaṃ samāśritā ye +api nirmamā niṣparigrahāḥ|

2.07  api te paripṛcchanti jyotiṣāṃ gatikovidam||

2.08  apradīpā yathā rātriranādityaṃ yathā nabhaḥ|

2.08  tathāsāṃvatsaro rājā bhramyatyandha ivādhvani||

2.09  *muhūrta[K.muhūrtaṃ]tithinakṣatramṛtavaścāyane tathā|

2.09  sarvāṇyevākulāni syurna syātsāṃvatsaro yadi||

2.10  tasmādrājñādhigantavyo vidvān sāṃvatsaro +agraṇīḥ|

2.10  jayaṃ yaśaḥ śriyaṃ bhogān śreyaśca samabhīpsatā||

2.11  nāsāṃvatsarike deśe vastavyaṃ bhūtimicchatā|

2.11  cakṣurbhūto hi yatraiṣa pāpaṃ tatra na vidyat |

2.12  na sāṃvatsarapāṭhī ca narakeṣūpapadyat

2.12  brahmalokapratiṣṭhāṃ ca labhate daivacintakaḥ||

2.13  granthataścārthataścaitatkṛtsnaṃ jānati yo dvijaḥ|

2.13  agrabhuksa bhavetśrāddhe pūjitaḥ paṅktipāvanaḥ|

2.14  mlecchā hi yavanāsteṣu samyakśāstramidaṃ sthitam|

2.14  ṛṣivatte +api pūjyante kiṃ punardaivaviddvijaḥ||

2.15  kuhakāveśapihitaiḥ karṇopaśrutihetubhiḥ|

2.15  kṛtādeśo na sarvatra praṣṭavyo na sa daivavit ||

2.16  aviditvaiva *yat[K.yaḥ]śāstraṃ daivajñatvaṃ prapadyat

2.16  sa paṅktidūṣakaḥ pāpo jñeyo nakṣatrasūcakaḥ||

[K.2.18  nakṣatrasūcakoddiṣṭamupahāsaṃ karoti yaḥ|

K.2.18  sa vrajatyandhatāmisraṃ sārdhamṛkṣaviḍambinā||]

2.17  nagaradvāraloṣṭasya yadvatsyādupayācitam|

2.17  ādeśastadvadajñānāṃ yaḥ satyaḥ sa vibhāvyat |

2.18  sampattyā yojitādeśastadvicchinnakathāpriyaḥ|

2.18  mattaḥ śāstraikadeśena tyājyastādṛgmahīkṣitā||

2.19  yastu samyagvijānāti horāgaṇitasaṃhitāḥ|

2.19  abhyarcyaḥ sa narendreṇa svīkartavyo jayaiṣiṇā||

2.20  na tatsahasraṃ kariṇāṃ vājināṃ ca caturguṇam|

2.20  karoti deśakālajño *yathaiko[K.yadeko]daivacintakaḥ||

2.21  duḥsvapnadurvicintitaduṣprekṣitaduṣkṛtāni karmāṇi|

2.21  kṣipraṃ prayānti nāśaṃ śaśinaḥ śrutvā bhasaṃvādam||

2.22  na tathecchati bhūpateḥ pitā jananī vā svajano +atha vā suhṛt |

2.22  svayaśoabhivivṛddhaye yathā hitamāptaḥ sabalasya daivavit ||



3 ādityacārādhyāyaḥ



3.01  āśleṣārdhāddakṣiṇamuttaramayanaṃ *raver[K.omitted] dhaniṣṭhādyam|

3.01  nūnaṃ kadā cidāsīdyenoktaṃ pūrvaśāstreṣu||

3.02  sāmpratamayanaṃ savituḥ karkaṭakādyaṃ mṛgāditaścānyat |

3.02  uktābhāvo vikṛtiḥ pratyakṣaparīkṣaṇairvyaktiḥ||

3.03  dūrasthacihnavedhādudaye +astamaye +api vā sahasrāṃśoḥ|

3.03  chāyāpraveśanirgamacihnairvā maṇḍale mahati||

3.04  aprāpya makaramarko vinivṛtto hanti sāparāṃ yāmyām|

3.04  karkaṭakamasamprāpto vinivṛttaścottarāṃ sa +aindrīm||

3.05  uttaramayanamatītya vyāvṛttaḥ kṣemasasyavṛddhikaraḥ|

3.05  prakṛtisthaścāpyevaṃ vikṛtagatirbhayakṛduṣṇāṃśuḥ||

3.06  satamaskaṃ parva vinā tvaṣṭā nāmārkamaṇḍalaṃ kurut

3.06  sa nihanti sapta bhūpān janāṃśca śastrāgnidurbhikṣaiḥ||

3.07  tāmasakīlakasaṃjñā rāhusutāḥ ketavastrayastriṃśat |

3.07  varṇasthānākāraistān dṛṣṭvā +arke phalaṃ brūyāt ||

3.08  te cārkamaṇḍalagatāḥ pāpaphalāścandramaṇḍale saumyāḥ|

3.08  dhvāṅkṣakabandhapraharaṇarūpāḥ pāpāḥ śaśāṅke +api||

3.09  teṣāmudaye rūpāṇyambhaḥ kaluṣaṃ rajovṛtaṃ vyom

3.09  nagataruśikhara*āmardī[K.vimardī] saśarkaro mārutaścaṇḍaḥ||

3.10  ṛtuviparītāstaravo dīptā mṛgapakṣiṇo diśāṃ dāhāḥ|

3.10  nirghātamahīkampādayo bhavantyatra cotpātāḥ||

3.11  na pṛthakphalāni teṣāṃ śikhikīlakarāhudarśanāni yadi|

3.11  tadudayakāraṇameṣāṃ ketvādīnāṃ phalaṃ brūyāt ||

3.12  yasmin yasmin deśe darśanamāyānti sūryabimbasthā|

3.12  tasmiṃstasmin vyasanaṃ mahīpatīnāṃ parijñeyam||

3.13  kṣutpramlānaśarīrā munayo +apyutsṛṣṭadharmasaccaritāḥ|

3.13  nirmāṃsabālahastāḥ kṛcchreṇa +āyānti *paradeśam[K.paradeśān]||

3.14  taskaraviluptavittāḥ pradīrghaniḥśvāsamukulitākṣipuṭāḥ|

3.14  santaḥ sannaśarīrāḥ śokodbhava*vāṣpa[K'str. bāṣpa]ruddhadṛśaḥ||

3.15  kṣāmā jugupsamānāḥ svanṛpatiparacakrapīḍitā manujāḥ|

3.15  svanṛpaticaritaṃ karma *na[K.ca] *purā kṛtaṃ[K.parākṛtaṃ, K'str. purākṛtaṃ] prabruvantyany |

3.16  garbheṣvapi niṣpannā vārimuco na prabhūtavārimucaḥ|

3.16  sarito yānti tanutvaṃ kva citkvacijjāyate sasyam||

3.17  daṇḍe narendramṛtyurvyādhibhayaṃ syāt*kabandhasaṃsthāne[K.kavandhasaṃsthāne]|

3.17  dhvāṅkṣe ca taskarabhayaṃ durbhikṣaṃ kīlake +arkasth |

3.18  rājopakaraṇarūpaiśchatradhvajacāmarādibhirviddhaḥ|

3.18  rājānyatvakṛdarkaḥ sphuliṅgadhūmādibhirjanahā||

3.19  eko durbhikṣakaro dvyādyāḥ syurnarapatervināśāy

3.19  sitaraktapītakṛṣṇaistairviddho +arko +anuvarṇaghnaḥ||

3.20  *dvaśyante[K.& ū.dṛśyante] ca yataste ravibimbasyotthitā mahotpātāḥ|

3.20  āgacchati lokānāṃ tenaiva bhayaṃ pradeśen |

3.21  ūrdhvakaro divasakarastāmraḥ senāpatiṃ vināśayati|

3.21  pīto narendraputraṃ śvetastu purohitaṃ hanti||

3.22  citro +atha vāpi dhūmro raviraśmir*vyākulām[K.vyākulāṃ] karoty*ūrdham[K.mahīm]|

3.22  taskaraśastranipātairyadi salilaṃ nāśu pātayati||

3.23  tāmraḥ kapilo vārkaḥ śiśire harikuṅkumacchaviśca madhau|

3.23  āpāṇḍukanakavarṇo grīṣme varṣāsu śuklaśc |

3.24  śaradi kamalodarābho hemante rudhirasannibhaḥ śastaḥ|

3.24  prāvṛṭkāle snigdhaḥ sarvartunibho +api śubhadāyī||

3.25  rūkṣaḥ śveto viprān raktābhaḥ kṣatriyān vināśayati|

3.25  pīto vaiśyān kṛṣṇastato aparān śubhakaraḥ snigdhaḥ||

3.26  grīṣme rakto bhayakṛdvarṣāsvasitaḥ karotyanāvṛṣṭim|

3.26  hemante pīto +arkaḥ karoti *na cireṇa[K.acireṇa] rogabhayam||

3.27  suracāpapāṭitatanurnṛpativirodhapradaḥ sahasrāṃśuḥ|

3.27  prāvṛṭkāle sadyaḥ karoti vimaladyutirvṛṣṭim||

3.28  varṣākāle vṛṣṭiṃ karoti sadyaḥ śirīṣapuṣpābhaḥ|

3.28  śikhipatranibhaḥ salilaṃ na karoti dvādaśābdāni||

3.29  śyāme +arke kīṭabhayaṃ bhasmanibhe bhayamuśanti paracakrāt |

3.29  yasyarkṣe sacchidrastasya vināśaḥ kṣitīśasy |

3.30  śaśarudhiranibhe bhānau nabhastalasthe bhavanti saṅgrāmāḥ|

3.30  śaśisadṛśe *nṛpatibadhaḥ[K.nṛpativaddhaḥ] kṣipraṃ cānyo nṛpo bhavati||

3.31  kṣutmārakṛtghaṭanibhaḥ khaṇḍo *janahā[K.nṛpahā] vidīdhitirbhayadaḥ|

3.31  toraṇarūpaḥ purahā chatranibho deśanāśāy |

3.32  dhvajacāpanibhe yuddhāni bhāskare vepane ca rūkṣe c

3.32  kṛṣṇā rekhā savitari yadi hanti tato *nṛpaṃ[K.nṛpaṃ tataḥ] sacivaḥ||

3.33  *dinakaram[K.divasakaram] *udayāstasaṃsthitam[K.udayasaṃsthitam] ulkāśanividyuto yadā hanyuḥ|

3.33  narapatimaraṇaṃ vindyāttadānyarāja*pratiṣṭhā[K.pratiṣṭhāṃ] c |

3.34  pratidivasamahimakiraṇaḥ pariveṣī sandhyayordvayoratha vā|

8.24  prajāpatiścāpyanuvatsaraḥ syādidvatsaraḥ śailasutāpatiśc |

8.25  vṛṣṭiḥ samādye pramukhe dvitīye prabhūtatoyā kathitā tṛtīy

8.25  paścājjalaṃ muñcati yaccaturthaṃ svalpodakaṃ pañcamamabdamuktam||

8.26  catvāri mukhyāni yugānyathaiṣāṃ viṣṇvindrajīvānaladaivatāni|

8.26  catvāri madhyāni ca madhyamāni catvāri cāntyānyadhamāni

vindyāt ||

8.27  ādyaṃ dhaniṣṭāṃśamabhiprapanno māghe yadā yātyudayaṃ surejyaḥ|

8.27  ṣaṣṭyabdapūrvaḥ prabhavaḥ sa nāmnā *prapadyate[K.pravartate] bhūtahitastadābdaḥ||

8.28  kva cittvavṛṣṭiḥ pavanāgnikopaḥ santītayaḥ śleṣmakṛtāśca rogāḥ|

8.28  saṃvatsare +asmin prabhave pravṛtte na duḥkhamāpnoti janastathāpi||

8.29  tasmāddvitīyo vibhavaḥ pradiṣṭaḥ śuklastṛtīyaḥ parataḥ pramodaḥ|

8.29  prajāpatiśceti yathottarāṇi śastāni varṣāṇi phalāny*athaiṣām[K.caiṣām]||

8.30  niṣpannaśālīkṣuyavādisasyāṃ bhayairvimuktāmupaśāntavairām||

8.30  saṃhṛṣṭalokāṃ kalidoṣamuktāṃ kṣatraṃ tadā śāsti ca bhūtadhātrīm||

8.31  ādyo +aṅgirāḥ śrīmukhabhāvasāhvau *yuvā sudhāteti[K.yuvātha dhāteti] yuge dvitīy

8.31  varṣāṇi pañcaiva yathākrameṇa trīṇyatra śastāni same pare dv |

8.32  triṣv*ādyavarṣeṣu[K.aṅgirādyeṣu, K'str. ādyavarṣeṣu] nikāmavarṣī devī nirātaṅka*bhayaś[K.bhayāś] ca lokaḥ|

8.32  abdadvaye +antye +api samā suvṛṣṭiḥ kintvatra rogāḥ samarāgamaśc |

8.33  śākre yuge pūrvamatheśvarākhyaṃ varṣaṃ dvitīyaṃ bahudhānyamāhuḥ|

8.33  pramāthinaṃ vikramamapy*athānyad[K.ato +anyad] vṛṣaṃ ca vindyādgurucārayogāt ||

8.34  ādyaṃ dvitīyaṃ ca śubhe tu varṣe kṛtānukāraṃ kurutaḥ prajānām|

8.34  pāpaḥ pramāthī vṛṣavikramau tu subhikṣadau rogabhayapradau c |

8.35  śreṣṭhaṃ ca caturthasya yugasya pūrvaṃ yaccitrabhānuṃ kathayanti varṣam|

8.35  madhyaṃ dvitīyaṃ tu subhānusañjñaṃ rogapradaṃ mṛtyukaraṃ *na taṃ ca[K.na tacca]||

8.36  tāraṇaṃ tadanu bhūrivāridaṃ sasyavṛddhi*muditāti[K.muditaṃ ca]pārthivam|

8.36  pañcamaṃ vyayamuśanti śobhanaṃ manmathaprabalamutsavākulam||

8.37  tvāṣṭre yuge sarvajidādya uktaḥ saṃvatsaro +anyaḥ khalu sarvadhārī|

8.37  tasmādvirodhī vikṛtaḥ kharaśca śasto dvitīyo +atra bhayāya śeṣāḥ||

8.38  nandano +atha vijayo jayastathā manmatho +asya parataśca durmukhaḥ|

8.38  kāntamatra yuga āditastrayaṃ manmathaḥ samaphalo +adhamo +aparaḥ||

8.39  hemalamba iti saptame yuge syādvilambi parato vikāri c

8.39  śarvarīti tadanu plavaḥ smṛto vatsaro guruvaśena pañcamaḥ||

8.40  *ītiprāyā[K.itiprāyaḥ] pracurapavanā vṛṣṭirabde tu pūrve

8.40 mandaṃ sasyaṃ na bahusalilaṃ vatsare +ato dvitīy

8.40 atyudvegaḥ pracurasalilaḥ syāttṛtīyaścaturtho

8.40 durbhikṣāya plava iti tataḥ śobhano bhūritoyaḥ||

8.41  vaiśve yuge *śokahṛd[K.śobhakṛd] ityathādyaḥ saṃvatsaro +ataḥ śubhakṛddvitīyaḥ|

8.41  krodhī tṛtīyaḥ parataḥ krameṇa viśvāvasuśceti parābhavaśc |

8.42  pūrvāparau prītikarau prajānāmeṣāṃ tṛtīyo bahudoṣado +abdaḥ|

8.42  antyau samau kintu parābhave +agniḥ śastrāmayārtirdvijagobhayaṃ c |

8.43  ādyaḥ plavaṅgo navame yuge +abdaḥ syātkīlako +anyaḥ parataśca saumyaḥ|

8.43  sādhāraṇo rodhakṛdity*athaivaṃ[K.athābdaḥ] śubhapradau kīlakasaumyasaṃjñau||

8.44  kaṣṭaḥ plavaṅgo bahuśaḥ prajānāṃ sādhāraṇe +alpaṃ jalamītayaśc

8.44  yaḥ pañcamo rodhakṛdityathābdaścitraṃ jalaṃ tatra ca sasyasampat ||

8.45  indrāgnidaivaṃ daśamaṃ yugaṃ yat*tatrādyavarṣaṃ[K.tarādyamabdaṃ] paridhāvisaṃjñam|

8.45  *pramādinaṃ vikramamapyato +anyat[K.pramādyathānandamataḥ paraṃ yat] syādrākṣasaṃ cānalasaṃjñitaṃ c |

8.46  paridhāvini madhyadeśanāśo nṛpahānirjalamalpamagnikopaḥ|

8.46  alasastu janaḥ pramādisaṃjñe ḍamaraṃ raktakapuṣpabījanāśaḥ||

8.47  *vikramaḥ[K.tatparaḥ] sakalalokanandano rākṣasaḥ kṣayakaro +analastathā|

8.47  grīṣmadhānyajanano +atra rākṣaso vahnikopamarakaprado +analaḥ||

8.48  ekādaśe piṅgalakālayuktasiddhārtharaudrāḥ khalu durmatiśc

8.48  ādye tu vṣṛṭirmahatī sacaurā śvāso hanūkampayutaśca kāsaḥ||

8.49  yatkālayuktaṃ tadanekadoṣaṃ siddhārthasaṃjñe bahavo guṇāśc

8.49  raudro +atiraudraḥ kṣayakṛtpradiṣṭo yo durmatirmadhyamavṛṣṭikṛtsaḥ||

8.50  bhāgye yuge dundubhisaṃjñamādyaṃ sasyasya vṛddhiṃ mahatīṃ karoti|

8.50  *aṅgārasaṃjñaṃ[K.udgārisaṃjñaṃ] tadanu kṣayāya nareśvarāṇāṃ viṣamā ca vṛṣṭiḥ||

8.51  raktākṣamabdaṃ kathitaṃ tṛtīyaṃ tasmin bhayaṃ daṃṣṭrikṛtaṃ gadāśc

8.51  krodhaṃ bahukrodhakaraṃ caturthaṃ rāṣṭrāṇi śūnyīkurute virodhaiḥ||

8.52  kṣayamiti yugasyāntyasyāntyaṃ bahukṣayakārakaṃ

8.52 janayati bhayaṃ tadviprāṇāṃ kṛṣībalavṛddhidam|

8.52 upacayakaraṃ viṭśūdrāṇāṃ parasvahṛtāṃ tathā

8.52 kathitamakhilaṃ ṣaṣṭyabde yattadatra samāsataḥ||

8.53  akaluṣāṃśujaṭilaḥ pṛthumūrtiḥ kumudakundakusumasphaṭikābhaḥ|

8.53  grahahato na yadi satpathavartī *hitakaro[K.hatakiro] +amaragururmanujānām||



9 śukracārādhyāyaḥ



9.01  nāgagajāirāvatavṛṣabhagojaradgavamṛgājadahanākhyāḥ|

9.01  aśvinyādyāḥ kaiścittribhāḥ kramādvīthayaḥ kathitāḥ||

9.02  nāgā tu pavanayāmyānalāni paitāmahāttribhāstisraḥ|

9.02  govīthyāmaśvinyaḥ pauṣṇaṃ dve cāpi bhadrapad |

9.03  jāradgavyāṃ śravaṇāttribhaṃ mṛgākhyā tribhaṃ tu maitrādyam|

9.03  hastaviśākhātvāṣṭrāṇyajetyaṣāḍhādvayaṃ dahanā||

9.04  tisrastisrastāsāṃ kramādudaṅmadhyayāmyamārgasthāḥ|

9.04  tāsāmapyuttaramadhya*dakṣiṇena sthitaikaikā[K.dakṣiṇāvasthitaikaikā]||

9.05  vīthīmārgānapare kathayanti yathāsthitān bhamārgasy

9.05  nakṣatrāṇāṃ tārā yāmyottaramadhyamāstadvat ||

9.06  uttaramārgo yāmyādi nigadito madhyamastu bhāgyādyaḥ|

9.06  dakṣiṇamārgoāṣāḍhādi kaiścidevaṃ kṛtā mārgāḥ||

9.07  *jyautiṣam[K.jyotisam] āgamaśāstraṃ vipratipattau na yogyamasmākam|

9.07  svayamevā vikalpayituṃ kintu bahūnāṃ mataṃ vakṣy |

9.08  uttaravīthiṣu śukraḥ subhikṣaśivakṛdgato +astamudayam *ca[K.vā]|

9.08  madhyāsu madhyaphaladaḥ kaṣṭaphalo dakṣiṇasthāsu||

9.09  atyuttamottamonaṃ samamadhyanyūnamadhamakaṣṭaphalam|

9.09  *kaṣṭataraṃ[K.kaṣṭatamaṃ] saumyādyāsu vīthiṣu yathākramaṃ brūyāt ||

9.10  bharaṇīpūrvaṃ maṇḍalamṛkṣacatuṣkaṃ subhikṣakaramādyam|

9.10  vaṅgāṅgamahiṣa*bāhlika[K.vāhlika]kaliṅgadeśeṣu bhayajananam||

9.11  atroditamārohedgraho +aparo yadi sitaṃ tato hanyāt |

9.11  bhadrāśvaśūrasenakayaudheyakakoṭivarṣanṛpān||

9.12  bhacatuṣṭayamārdrādyaṃ dvitīyamamitāmbusasyasampattyai|

9.12  viprāṇāmaśubhakaraṃ viśeṣataḥ krūraceṣṭānām||

9.13  anyenātrākrānte mlecchā*āṭavika[K.& vātavikā]śvajīvigomantān|

9.13  gonardanīcaśūdrān vaidehāṃścānayaḥ spṛśati||

9.14  vicaranmaghādipañcakamuditaḥ sasyapraṇāśakṛtśukraḥ|

9.14  kṣuttaskarabhayajanano nīconnatisaṅkarakaraśc |

9.15  pitryādye +avaṣṭabdho hantyanyenāvikān śabaraśūdrān|

9.15  puṇḍrāparāntyaśūlikavanavāsidraviḍasāmudrān||

9.16  svātyādyaṃ bhatritayaṃ maṇḍalametaccaturthamabhayakaram|

9.16  brahmakṣatrasubhikṣābhivṛddhaye mitrabhedāy |

9.17  atrākrānte mṛtyuḥ kirātabhartuḥpinaṣṭi cekṣvākūn|

9.17  pratyantāvantipulindataṅgaṇān śūrasenāṃśc |

9.18  jyeṣṭhādyaṃ pañcarkṣaṃ kṣuttaskararogadaṃ prabādhayat

9.18  kāśmīrāśmakamatsyān sacārudevīnavantīṃśc |

9.19  *atrāroheddraviḍābhīrāmbaṣṭha[K.ārohe +atrābhīrān draviḍāmbaṣṭha]trigartasaurāṣṭrān|
9.19  nāśayanti sindhusauvīrakāṃśca kāśīśvarasya vadhaḥ||

9.20  ṣaṣṭhaṃ ṣaṇnakṣatraṃ śubhametanmaṇḍalaṃ dhaniṣṭhādyam|

9.20  bhūridhanagokulākulamanalpadhānyaṃ kva citsabhayam||

9.21  atra*ārohec[K.ārohe]śūlikagāndhārāvantayaḥ prapīḍyant

9.21  vaidehavadhaḥ pratyantayavanaśakadāsaparivṛddhiḥ||

9.22  aparasyāṃ svātyādyaṃ jyeṣṭhādyaṃ cāpi maṇḍalaṃ śubhadam|

9.22  pitryādyaṃ pūrvasyāṃ śeṣāṇi yathoktaphaladāni||

9.23  dṛṣṭo *+anastamite[K.+anastagate +arke] bhayakṛtkṣudrogakṛtsamastamahaḥ|

9.23  *ardhadivase[K.arthadivasaṃ] ca sendurnṛpabalapurabhedakṛtśukraḥ||

9.24  bhindan gato +analarkṣaṃ kūlātikrāntavārivāhābhiḥ|

9.24  avyaktatuṅganimnā samā saridbhirbhavati dhātrī||

9.25  prājāpatye śakaṭe bhinne kṛtveva pātakaṃ vasudhā|

9.25  keśāsthiśakalaśabalā kāpālamiva vrataṃ dhatt |

9.26  saumyopagato rasasasyasaṃkṣayāyośanāḥ samuddiṣṭaḥ|

9.26  ārdrāgatastu kośalakaliṅgahā salilanikarakaraḥ||

9.27  aśmakavaidarbhāṇāṃ punarvasusthe site mahānanayaḥ|

9.27  puṣye puṣṭā vṛṣṭir*vidyādhararaṇa[ū.vidyādhara]vimardaśc |

9.28  āśleṣāsu bhujaṅgamadāruṇapīḍāvahaścaran śukraḥ|

9.28  bhindanmaghāṃ mahāmātradoṣakṛdbhūrivṛṣṭikaraḥ||

9.29  bhāgye śabarapulindapradhvaṃsakaro +ambunivahamokṣāy

9.29  āryamṇe kurujāṅgalapāñcālaghnaḥ saliladāyī||

9.30  kauravacitrakarāṇāṃ haste pīḍā jalasya ca nirodhaḥ|

9.30  kūpakṛdaṇḍajapīḍā citrāsthe śobhanā vṛṣṭiḥ||

9.31  svātau prabhūtavṛṣṭirdūtavaṇignāvikān spṛśatyanayaḥ|

9.31  aindrāgne +api suvṛṣṭirvaṇijāṃ ca bhayaṃ vijānīyāt ||

9.32  maitre kṣatravirodho jyeṣṭhāyāṃ kṣatramukhyasantāpaḥ|

9.32  maulikabhiṣajāṃ mūle triṣvapi caiteṣvanāvṛṣṭiḥ||

9.33  āpye salilajapīḍā viśveśe vyādhayaḥ prakupyanti|

9.33  śravaṇe śravaṇavyādhiḥ *pākhaṇḍi[K.pāṣaṇḍi]bhayaṃ dhaniṣṭhāsu||

9.34  śatabhiṣaji śauṇḍikānām *ajaikabhe[K.ajaikape] dyūtajīvināṃ *pīḍāṃ[K.pīḍā]|

9.34  kurupāñcālānāmapi karoti cāsmin sitaḥ salilam||

9.35  āhirbudhnye phalamūlatāpakṛdyāyināṃ ca revatyām|

9.35  aśvinyāṃ hayapānāṃ yāmye tu kirātayavanānām||

9.36  *caturdaśīṃ pañcadaśīṃ tathāṣṭamīṃ[K.caturdaśe pañcadaśe tathāṣṭame] tamisrapakṣasya *tithiṃ[K.tithau] bhṛgoḥ sutaḥ|

9.36  yadā vrajeddarśanamastameti vā tadā mahī vārimayīva lakṣyat |

9.37  gururbhṛguścāparapūrvakāṣṭhayoḥ parasparaṃ saptamarāśigau yadā|

9.37  tadā prajā rugbhayaśokapīḍitā na vāri paśyanti purandarojjhitam||

9.38  yadā sthitā jīvabudhārasūryajāḥ sitasya sarve +agrapathānuvartinaḥ|

9.38  nṛnāgavidyādharasaṅgarāstadā bhavanti vātāśca samucchritāntakāḥ||

9.39  na mitrabhāve suhṛdo vyavasthitāḥ kriyāsu samyagna ratā dvijātayaḥ|

9.39  na cālpamapyambu dadāti vāsavo bhinatti vajreṇa śirāṃsi bhūbhṛtām||

9.40  śanaiścare mlecchaviḍālakuñjarāḥ kharā mahiṣyo +asitadhānyaśūkarāḥ|

9.40  pulindaśūdrāśca sadakṣiṇāpathāḥ kṣayaṃ vrajantyakṣimarudgadodbhavaiḥ||

9.41  nihanti śukraḥ kṣitije +agrataḥ *prajāṃ[K.prajā] hutāśaśastrakṣudavṛṣṭitaskaraiḥ|

9.41  carācaraṃ vyaktamathottarāpathaṃ diśo +agnividyudrajasā ca pīḍyet ||

9.42  bṛhaspatau hanti puraḥsthite sitaḥ sitaṃ samastaṃ dvijagosurālayān|

9.42  diśaṃ ca pūrvāṃ karakāsṛjo +ambudā gale gadā bhūri bhavecca śāradam||

9.43  saumyo +astodayoḥ puro bhṛgusutasyāvasthitastoyakṛd

9.43 rogān pittaja*kāmalāṃś[K.kāmalāṃ K'str. kāmalāmśorkāmalāś] ca kurute puṣṇāti ca *graiṣmikān[K.graiṣmikam]|

9.43 hanyātpravrajitāgnihotrikabhiṣagraṅgopajīvyān hayān

9.43 vaiśyān gāḥ saha vāhanairnarapatīn pītāni paścāddiśam||

9.44  śikhibhayamanalābhe śastrakopaśca rakte

9.44 kanakanikaṣagaure vyādhayo daityapūjy

9.44 haritakapilarūpe śvāsakāsaprakopaḥ

9.44 patati na salilaṃ khādbhasmarūkṣāsitābh |

9.45  dadhikumudaśaśāṅkakāntibhṛtsphuṭavikasatkiraṇo bṛhattanuḥ|

9.45  sugatiravikṛto jayānvitaḥ kṛtayugarūpakaraḥ sitāhvayaḥ||



10 śanaiścaracārādhyāyaḥ



10.01  śravaṇānilahastārdrābharaṇībhāgyopagaḥ suto +arkasy

10.01  pracurasalilopagūḍhāṃ karoti dhātrīṃ yadi snigdhaḥ||

10.02  ahivaruṇapurandaradaivateṣu sukṣemakṛnna cātijalam|

10.02  kṣutśastrāvṛṣṭikaro mūle pratyekamapi vakṣy |

10.03  turagaturagopacārakakavivaidyāmātyahārkajo +aśvigataḥ|

10.03  yāmye nartakavādakageyajñakṣudranaikṛtikān||

10.04  bahulāsthe pīḍyante saure +agnyupajīvinaścamūpāśc

10.04  rohiṇyāṃ kośalamadrakāśipañcālaśākaṭikāḥ||

10.05  mṛgaśirasi vatsayājakayajamānāryajanamadhyadeśāsc

10.05  raudrasthe *pārataramaṭhāstailikarajaka[K.pāratarātailikarajaka]caurāśc |

10.06  āditye pāñcanadapratyantasurāṣṭrasindhusauvīrāḥ|

10.06  puṣye ghāṇṭhikaghauṣikayavanavaṇikkitavakusumāni||

10.07  sārpe jalaruhasarpāḥ pitrye bāhlīkacīnagāndhārāḥ|

10.07  śūlikapāratavaiśyāḥ koṣṭhāgārāṇi vaṇijaśc |

10.08  bhāgye rasavikrayiṇaḥ paṇyastrīkanyakāmahārāṣṭrāḥ|

10.08  āryamṇe nṛpaguḍalavaṇabhikṣukāmbūni takṣaśilā||

10.09  haste nāpitacākrikacaurabhiṣaksūcikā dvipagrāhāḥ|

10.09  bandhakyaḥ kauśalakā mālākārāśca pīḍyant |

10.10  citrāsthe pramadājanalekhakacitrajñacitrabhāṇḍāni|

10.10  svātau māgadhacaradūtasūtapotaplavanaṭādyāḥ||

10.11  aindrāgnākhye traigartacīnakaulūtakuṅkumaṃ lākṣā|

10.11  sasyānyatha māñjiṣṭhaṃ kausumbhaṃ ca kṣayaṃ yāti||

10.12  maitre kulūtataṅgaṇakhasakāśmīrāḥ samantricakracarāḥ|

10.12  upatāpaṃ yānti ca ghāṇṭikā vibhedaśca mitrāṇām||

10.13  jyeṣṭhāsu nṛpapurohitanṛpasatkṛtaśūragaṇakulaśreṇyaḥ|

10.13  mūle tu kāśikośalapāñcālaphalāuṣadhīyodhāḥ||

10.14  āpye +aṅgavaṅgakauśalagirivrajā magadhapuṇḍramithilāśc

10.14  upatāpaṃ yānti janā vasanti ye tāmraliptyāṃ c |

10.15  viśveśvare +arkaputraścaran daśārṇānnihanti yavanāṃśc

10.15  ujjayinīṃ śabarān pāriyātrikān kuntibhojāṃśc |

10.16  śravaṇe rājādhikṛtān viprāgryabhiṣakpurohitakaliṅgān|

10.16  vasubhe magadheśajayo vṛddhiśca dhaneṣvadhikṛtānām||

10.17  sāje śatabhiṣaji bhiṣakkaviśauṇḍikapaṇyanīti*vṛttīnām[K.varttānām]|

10.17  āhirbudhnye nadyo yānakarāḥ strīhiraṇyaṃ c |

10.18  revatyāṃ rājabhṛtāḥ krauñcadvīpāśritāḥ śaratsasyam|

10.18  śabarāśca nipīḍyante yavanāśca śanaiścare carati||

10.19  yadā viśākhāsu mahendramantrī sutaśca bhānordahanarkṣayātaḥ|

10.19  tadā prajānāmanayo +atighoraḥ puraprabhedo gatayorbhamekam||

10.20  aṇḍajahā ravijo yadi citraḥ kṣudbhayakṛdyadi pītamayūkhaḥ|

10.20  śastrabhayāya ca raktasavarṇo bhasmanibho bahuvairakaraśc |

10.21  vaidūryakāntivimalaḥ śubhakṛtprajānāṃ

10.21 bāṇātasīkusumavarṇanibhaśca śastaḥ|

10.21 *yaṃ cāpi[K.pañcāpi] varṇamupagacchati tatsavarṇān

10.21 sūryātmajaḥ kṣayatīti munipravādaḥ||



11 ketucārādhyāyaḥ



11.01  gārgīyaṃ śikhicāraṃ pārāśaramasitadevalakṛtaṃ c

11.01  anyāṃśca bahūn dṛṣṭvā kriyate +ayamanākulaścāraḥ||

11.02  darśanamastamayo vā na gaṇitavidhināsya śakyate jñātum|

11.02  divyāntarikṣabhaumāstrividhāḥ syuḥ ketavo yasmāt ||

11.03  ahutāśe +analarūpaṃ yasmiṃstatketurūpamevoktam|

11.03  khadyotapiśācālayamaṇiratnādīn parityajy |

11.04  dhvajaśastrabhavanataruturagakuñjarādyeṣvathāntarikṣāst

11.04  divyā nakṣatrasthā bhaumāḥ syurato +anyathā śikhinaḥ||

11.05  śatamekādhikameke sahasramapare vadanti ketūnām|

11.05  bahurūpamekameva prāha munirnāradaḥ ketum||

11.06  yadyeko yadi bahavaḥ kimanena phalaṃ tu sarvathā vācyam|

11.06  udayāstamayaiḥ sthānaiḥ sparśairādhūmanairvarṇaiḥ|

11.07  yāvantyahāni dṛśyo māsāstāvanta eva phalapākaḥ|

11.07  māsairabdāṃśca vadetprathamātpakṣatrayātparataḥ||

11.08  hrasvastanuḥ prasannaḥ snigdhastvṛjuracirasaṃsthitaḥ śuklaḥ|

11.08  udito *+atha vābhivṛṣṭaḥ[K.vāpyabhidṛṣṭaḥ] subhikṣasaukhyāvahaḥ ketuḥ||

11.09  uktaviparītarūpo na śubhakaro dhūmaketurutpannaḥ|

11.09  indrāyudhānukārī viśeṣato dvitricūlo vā||

11.10  hāramaṇihemarūpāḥ kiraṇākhyāḥ pañcaviṃśatiḥ saśikhāḥ|

11.10  prāgaparadiśordṛśyā nṛpativirodhāvahā ravijāḥ||

11.11  śukadahanabandhujīvakalākṣākṣatajopamā hutāśasutāḥ|

11.11  āgneyyāṃ dṛśyante tāvantaste +api śikhibhayadāḥ||

11.12  vakraśikhā mṛtyusutā rūkṣāḥ kṛṣṇāśca te +api tāvantaḥ|

11.12  dṛśyante yāmyāyāṃ janamarakāvedinaste c |

11.13  darpaṇavṛttākārā viśikhāḥ kiraṇānvitā dharātanayāḥ|

11.13  kṣudbhayadā dvāviṃśatiraiśānyāmambutailanibhāḥ||

11.14  śaśikiraṇarajatahimakumudakundakusumopamāḥ sutāḥ śaśinaḥ|

11.14  uttarato dṛśyante trayaḥ subhikṣāvahāḥ śikhinaḥ||

11.15  brahmasuta eka eva triśikho varṇaistribhiryugāntakaraḥ|

11.15  aniyatadiksamprabhavo vijñeyo brahmadaṇḍākhyaḥ||

11.16  śatamabhihitamekasametametadekena virahitānyasmāt |

11.16  kathayiṣye ketūnāṃ śatāni nava lakṣaṇaiḥ spaṣṭaiḥ||

11.17  saumyaiśānyorudayaṃ śukrasutā yānti caturaśītyākhyāḥ|

11.17  vipulasitatārakāste snigdhāśca bhavanti tīvraphalāḥ||

11.18  snigdhāḥ prabhāsametā dviśikhāḥ ṣaṣṭiḥ śanaiścarāṅgaruhāḥ|

11.18  atikaṣṭaphalā dṛśyāḥ sarvatraite kanakasaṃjñāḥ||

11.19  vikacā nāma gurusutāḥ sitaikatārāḥ śikhāparityaktāḥ|

11.19  ṣaṣṭiḥ pañcabhiradhikā snigdhā yāmyāśritāḥ pāpāḥ||

11.20  nātivyaktāḥ sūkṣmā dīrghāḥ śuklā yatheṣṭadikprabhavāḥ|

11.20  budhajāstaskarasaṃjñāḥ pāpaphalāstvekapañcāśat ||

11.21  kṣatajānalānurūpāstricūlatārāḥ kujātmajāḥ ṣaṣṭiḥ|

11.21  nāmnā ca kauṅkumāste saumyāśāsaṃsthitāḥ pāpāḥ||

11.22  triṃśatyadhikā rāhoste tāmasakīlakā iti khyātāḥ|

11.22  raviśaśigā dṛśyante teṣāṃ phalamarkacāroktam||

11.23  viṃśatyadhikamanyatśatamagnerviśvarūpasaṃjñānām|

11.23  tīvrānalabhayadānāṃ jvālāmālākulatanūnām||

11.24  śyāmāruṇā vitārāścāmararūpā vikīrṇadīdhitayaḥ|

11.24  aruṇākhyā vāyoḥ saptasaptatiḥ pāpadāḥ paruṣāḥ||

11.25  tārāpuñjanikāśā gaṇakā nāma prajāpateraṣṭau|

11.25  dve ca śate caturadhike *caturasrā[K.caturaśrā] brahmasantānāḥ||

11.26  kaṅkā nāma varuṇajā dvātriṃśadvaṃśagulmasaṃsthānāḥ|

11.26  śaśivatprabhāsametāstīvraphalāḥ ketavaḥ proktāḥ||

11.27  ṣaṇṇavatiḥ kālasutāḥ kabandhasaṃjñāḥ kabandhasaṃsthānāḥ|

11.27  *puṇḍra[K.caṇḍa]abhayapradāḥ syurvirūpatārāśca te śikhinaḥ||

11.28  śuklavipulaikatārā nava vidiśāṃ ketavaḥ samutpannāḥ|

11.28  evaṃ ketusahasraṃ viśeṣameṣāmato vakṣy |

11.29  udagāyato mahān snigdhamūrtiraparodayī vasāketuḥ|

11.29  sadyaḥ karoti marakaṃ subhikṣamapyuttamaṃ kurut |

11.30  tallakṣaṇo +asthiketuḥ sa tu rūkṣaḥ kṣudbhayāvahaḥ proktaḥ|

11.30  snigdhastādṛkprācyāṃ śastrākhyo ḍamaramarakāy |

11.31  dṛśyo +amāvāsyāyāṃ kapālaketuḥ sadhūmraraśmiśikhaḥ|

11.31  prāṅnabhaso +ardhavicārī kṣutmarakāvṛṣṭirogakaraḥ||

11.32  prāg*vaiśvānara[K.vaśvānara, K'str. vaiśvānara]mārge śūlāgraḥ śyāvarūkṣatāmrārciḥ|

11.32  nabhasastribhāgagāmī raudra iti kapālatulyaphalaḥ||

11.33  aparasyāṃ calaketuḥ śikhayā yāmyāgrayāṅgulocchritayā|

11.33  gacchedyathā yathodaktathā tathā dairghyamāyāti||

11.34  saptamunīn saṃspṛśya dhruvamabhijitameva ca pratinivṛttaḥ|

11.34  nabhaso +ardhamātramitvā yāmyenāstaṃ samupayāti||

11.35  hanyātprayāgakūlādyāvadavantīṃ ca *puṣkarāraṇyam[K.puṣkarāṇyam]|

11.35  udagapi ca devikāmapi bhūyiṣṭhaṃ madhyadeśākhyam||

11.36  anyānapi sa deśān kva citkva ciddhanti rogadurbhikṣaiḥ|

11.36  daśa māsān phalapāko +asya kaiścidaṣṭādaśa proktaḥ||

11.37  prāgardharātradṛśyo yāmyāgraḥ śvetaketuranyaśc

11.37  ka iti yugākṛtirapare yugapattau saptadinadṛśyau||

11.38  snigdhau subhikṣaśivadāvathādhikaṃ dṛśyate kanāmā yaḥ|

11.38  daśa varṣāṇyupatāpaṃ janayati śastraprakopakṛtam||

11.39  śveta iti jaṭākāro rūkṣaḥ śyāvo viyattribhāgagataḥ|
11.39  vinivartate +apasavyaṃ tribhāgaśeṣāḥ prajāḥ kurut |

11.40  ādhūmrayā tu śikhayā darśanamāyāti kṛttikāsaṃsthaḥ|

11.40  jñeyaḥ sa raśmiketuḥ śvetasamānaṃ phalaṃ dhatt |

11.41  dhruvaketuraniyatagatipramāṇavarṇākṛtirbhavati viṣvak |

11.41  divyāntarikṣabhaumo bhavatyayaṃ snigdha iṣṭaphalaḥ||

11.42  senāṅgeṣu nṛpāṇāṃ gṛhataruśaileṣu cāpi deśānām|

11.42  gṛhiṇāmupaskareṣu ca vināśināṃ darśanaṃ yāti||

11.43  kumuda iti kumudakāntirvāruṇyāṃ prākśikho niśāmekām|

11.43  dṛṣṭaḥ subhikṣamatulaṃ daśa kila varṣāṇi sa karoti||

11.44  sakṛdekayāmadṛśyaḥ susūkṣmatāro +apareṇa maṇiketuḥ|

11.44  ṛjvī śikhāsya śuklā stanodgatā kṣīradhārev |

11.45  udayanneva subhikṣaṃ caturo māsān karotyasau sārdhān|

11.45  prādurbhāvaṃ prāyaḥ karoti ca kṣudrajantūnām||

11.46  jalaketurapi ca paścātsnigdhaḥ śikhayāpareṇa connatayā|

11.46  nava māsān sa subhikṣaṃ karoti śāntiṃ ca lokasy |

11.47  bhavaketurekarātraṃ dṛśyaḥ prāksūkṣmatārakaḥ snigdhaḥ|

11.47  harilāṅgūlopamayā pradakṣiṇāvartayā śikhayā||

11.48  yāvata eva muhūrtān darśanamāyāti nirdiśetmāsān|

11.48  tāvadatulaṃ subhikṣaṃ rūkṣe prāṇāntikān rogān||

11.49  apareṇa padmaketurmṛṇālagauro bhavenniśāmekām|

11.49  sapta karoti subhikṣaṃ varṣāṇyatiharṣayuktāni||

11.50  āvarta iti niśārdhe savyaśikho +aruṇanibho +apare snigdhaḥ|

11.50  yāvatkṣaṇān sa dṛśyastāvanmāsān subhikṣakaraḥ||

11.51  paścātsandhyākāle saṃvarto nāma dhūmratāmraśikhaḥ|

11.51  ākramya viyattryaṃśaṃ śūlāgrāvasthito raudraḥ||

11.52  yāvata eva muhūrtān dṛśyo varṣāṇi hanti tāvanti|

11.52  bhūpān śastranipātairudayarkṣaṃ cāpi pīḍayati||

11.53  ye śastāstān hitvā ketubhir*ādhūpite[K.ādhūmite] +atha vā spṛṣṭ

11.53  nakṣatre bhavati vadho yeṣāṃ rājñāṃ pravakṣye tān||

11.54  aśvinyāmaśmakapaṃ bharaṇīṣu kirātapārthivaṃ hanyāt |

11.54  bahulāsu kaliṅgeśaṃ rohiṇyāṃ śūrasenapatim||

11.55  auśīnaramapi saumye jalajājīvādhipaṃ tathārdrāsu|

11.55  āditye +aśmaka*nāthān[K.nāthaṃ] puṣye maghadhādhipaṃ hanti||

11.56  asikeśaṃ bhaujaṅge pitrye +aṅgaṃ pāṇḍyanāthamapi bhāgy

11.56  *aujjayinikam[K.aujjayanikam] āryamṇe sāvitre daṇḍakādhipatim||

11.57  citrāsu kurukṣetrādhipasya maraṇaṃ samādiśettajjñaḥ|

11.57  kāśmīrakakāmbojau nṛpatī prābhañjane na staḥ||

11.58  ikṣvākuralaka*nāthaś[K.nāthau, K'str. nātho] ca hanyate yadi bhavedviśākhāsu|

11.58  maitre puṇḍrādhipatir*jyeṣṭhāsu ca[K.jyeṣṭhāsvatha] sārvabhaumavadhaḥ||

11.59  mūle +andhramadrakapatī jaladeve kāśipo maraṇameti|

11.59  yaudheyakārjunāyanaśivicaidyān vaiśvadeve c |

11.60  hanyātkaikayanāthaṃ pāñcanadaṃ siṃhalādhipaṃ vāṅgam|

11.60  naimiṣanṛpaṃ kirātaṃ śravaṇādiṣu ṣaṭsvimān kramaśaḥ||

11.61  ulkābhitāḍitaśikhaḥ śikhī śivaḥ śivataro *+atidṛṣṭo[K.abhivṛṣṭo] yaḥ|

11.61  aśubhaḥ sa eva colāvagāṇasitahūṇacīnānām||

11.62  namrā yataḥ śikhiśikhābhisṛtā yato vā

11.62 ṛkṣaṃ ca yatspṛśati tatkathitāṃśca deśān|

11.62 divyaprabhāvanihatān sa yathā garutmān

11.62 bhuṅkte gato narapatiḥ parabhogibhogān||



12 agastyacārādhyāyaḥ



12.01  samudro +antaḥ śailairmakaranakharotkhātaśikharaiḥ

12.01 kṛtastoyocchittyā sapadi sutarāṃ yena ruciraḥ|

12.01 patanmuktāmiśraiḥ pravaramaṇiratnāmbunivahaiḥ

12.01 surān pratyādeṣṭuṃ *mita[K.sita]mukuṭaratnāniva purā||

12.02  yena cāmbuharaṇe +api vidrumairbhūdharaiḥ samaṇiratnavidrumaiḥ|

12.02  nirgataistaduragaiśca rājitaḥ sāgaro +adhikataraṃ virājitaḥ||

12.03  prasphurattimijalebhajihmagaḥ kṣiptaratnanikaro mahodadhiḥ|

12.03  āpadāṃ padagato +api yāpito yena pītasalilo +amaraśriyam||

12.04  pracalattimiśuktijaśaṅkhacitaḥ salile +apahṛte +api patiḥ saritām|

12.04  sataraṅgasitotpalahaṃsabhṛtaḥ sarasaḥ śaradīva vibharti *rucim[K.rucam]||

12.05  timisitāmbudharaṃ maṇitārakaṃ sphaṭikacandramanambuśaraddyutiḥ|

12.05  phaṇiphaṇopalaraśmiśikhigrahaṃ kuṭilageśaviyacca cakāra yaḥ||

12.06  dinakararathamārgavicchittaye +abhyudyataṃ yaccalatśṛṅgamudbhrāntavidyādharāṃsāvasaktapriyāvyagradattāṅkadehāvalambāmbara*aty[K.abhy]ucchritoddhūyamānadhvajaiḥ śobhitam|

12.06 karikaṭamadamiśraraktāvalehānuvāsānusāridvirephāvalīnottamāṅgaiḥ kṛtān bāṇapuṣpairivottaṃsakān dhārayadbhirmṛgendraiḥ sanāthīkṛtāntardarīnirjharam|

12.06 gaganatalamivollikhantaṃ pravṛddhairgajākṛṣṭaphulladrumatrāsavibhrāntamattadvirephāvalī*hṛṣṭa[K.gīta]mandrasvanaiḥ śailakūṭaistarakṣarkṣaśārdūlaśākhāmṛgādhyāsitaiḥ|

12.06 rahasi madanasaktayā revayā kāntayevopagūḍhaṃ surādhyāsitodyānamambhośanānnamūlānilāhāraviprānvitaṃ vindhyamastambhayadyaśca tasyodayaḥ śrūyatām||

12.07  udaye ca muneragastyanāmnaḥ kusamāyogamalapradūṣitāni|

12.07  hṛdayāni satāmiva svabhāvātpunarambūni bhavanti nirmalāni||

12.08  pārśvadvayādhiṣṭhitacakravākāmāpuṣṇatī sasvanahaṃsapaṅktim|

12.08  tāmbūlaraktotkaṣitāgradantī vibhāti yoṣeva *śarat[K.sarit] sahāsā||

12.09  indīvarāsannasitotpalānvitā *śarad[K.sarit] bhramatṣaṭpadapaṅktibhūṣitā|

12.09  sabhrūlatākṣepakaṭākṣavīkṣaṇā vidagdhayoṣeva vibhāti sasmarā||

12.10  indoḥ payodavigamopahitāṃ vibhūtiṃ

12.10 draṣṭuṃ taraṅgavalayā kumudaṃ niśāsu|

12.10 unmīlayatyalinilīnadalaṃ supakṣma

12.10 vāpī vilocanamivāsitatārakāntam||

12.11  nānāvicitrāmbujahaṃsakokakāraṇḍavāpūrṇataḍāgahastā|

12.11  ratnaiḥ prabhūtaiḥ kusumaiḥ phalaiśca bhūryacchatīvārghamagastyanāmn |

12.12  salilamamarapājñayojjhitaṃ yaddhanapariveṣṭitamūrtibhirbhujaṅgaiḥ|

12.12  phaṇijanitaviṣāgnisampraduṣṭaṃ bhavati śivaṃ tadagastyadarśanen |

12.13  smaraṇādapi pāpamapākurute kimuta stutibhirvaruṇāṅgaruhaḥ|

12.13  munibhiḥ kathito +asya yathārghavidhiḥ kathayāmi tathaiva narendrahitam||

12.14  saṃkhyāvidhānātpratideśamasya vijñāya sandarśanamādiśejjñaḥ|

12.14  tacca*ujjayinyām[K.ujjayanyām] agatasya kanyāṃ bhāgaiḥ svarākhyaiḥ sphuṭabhāskarasy |

12.15  īṣatprabhinne +aruṇaraśmijālairnaiśe +andhakāre diśi dakṣiṇasyām|

12.15  sāṃvatsarāveditadigvibhāge bhūpo +arghamurvyāṃ prayataḥ prayacchet ||

12.16  kālodbhavaiḥ surabhibhiḥ kusumaiḥ phalaiśca

12.16 ratnaiśca sāgarabhavaiḥ kanakāmbaraiśc

12.16 dhenvā vṛṣeṇa paramānnayutaiśca bhakṣyair

12.16 dadhyakṣataiḥ surabhidhūpavilepanaiśc |

12.17  narapatirimamarghaṃ śraddadhāno dadhānaḥ

12.17 pravigatagadadoṣo nirjitārātipakṣaḥ|

12.17 bhavati yadi ca dadyātsapta varṣāṇi samyag

12.17 jalanidhi*raśanāyāḥ[K.rasanāyāḥ] svāmitāṃ yāti bhūmeḥ||

12.18  dvijo yathālābhamupāhṛtārghaḥ prāpnoti vedān pramadāśca putrān|

12.18  vaiśyaśca gāṃ bhūri ghanaṃ ca śūdro rogakṣayaṃ dharmaphalaṃ ca sarv |

12.19  rogān karoti paruṣaḥ kapilastvavṛṣṭiṃ

12.19 dhūmro gavāmaśubhakṛtsphuraṇo bhayāy

12.19 māñjiṣṭharāgasadṛśaḥ kṣudhamāhavāṃśca

12.19 kuryādaṇuśca purarodhamagastyanāmā||

12.20  śātakumbhasadṛśaḥ sphaṭikābhastarpayanniva mahīṃ *kiraṇāgraiḥ[K.kiraṇaughaiḥ]|

12.20  dṛśyate yadi *tadā[K.tataḥ] pracurānnā bhūrbhavatyabhayarogajanāḍhyā||

12.21  ulkayā vinihataḥ śikhinā vā kṣudbhayaṃ marakameva *vidhatte[K.dhatte]|

12.21  dṛśyate sa kila hastagate +arke rohiṇīmupagate +astamupaiti||



13 saptarṣicārādhyāyaḥ



13.01  saikāvalīva rājati sasitotpalamālinī sahāsev

13.01  nāthavatīva ca digyaiḥ kauverī saptabhirmunibhiḥ||

13.02  dhruvanāyakopadeśān *narinartī[K.narinarttī]ivottarā bhramadbhiśc

13.02  yaiścāramahaṃ teṣāṃ kathayiṣye vṛddhagargamatāt ||

13.03  āsanmaghāsu munayaḥ śāsati pṛthvīṃ yudhiṣṭhire nṛpatau|

13.03  ṣaḍdvikapañcadviyutaḥ śakakālastasya rājñaśc |

13.04  ekaikasminnṛkṣe śataṃ śataṃ te caranti varṣāṇām|

13.04  *prāgudayato +apyavivarādrjūnnayati tatra saṃyuktāḥ[K.prāguttarataśca ete sadā udayante sasādhvīkāḥ]||

13.05  pūrve bhāge bhagavānmarīcirapare sthito vasiṣṭho +asmāt |

13.05  tasyāṅgirāstato +atristasyāsannaḥ pulastyaśc |

13.06  pulahaḥ kraturiti bhagānāsannā anukrameṇa *pūrvādyāt[K.pūrvādyāḥ]|

13.06  tatra vasiṣṭhaṃ munivaramupāśritārundhatī sādhvī||

13.07  ulkāśanidhūmādyairhatā vivarṇā viraśmayo hrasvāḥ|

13.07  hanyuḥ svaṃ svaṃ vargaṃ vipulāḥ snigdhāśca tadvṛddhyai||

13.08  gandharvadevadānavamantrāuṣadhisiddhayakṣanāgānām|

13.08  pīḍākāro marīcirjñeyo vidyādharāṇāṃ c |

13.09  śakayavanadaradapāratakāmbojāṃstāpasān vanopetān|

13.09  hanti vasiṣṭho +abhihato vivṛddhido raśmisampannaḥ||

13.10  aṅgiraso jñānayutā dhīmanto brāhmaṇāśca nirdiṣṭāḥ|

13.10  atreḥ kāntārabhavā jalajānyambhonidhiḥ saritaḥ||

14 nakṣatrakūrmādhyāyaḥ



14.01  nakṣatratrayavargairāgneyādyairvyavasthitairnavadhā|

16.35  kharacaraniyuddhavittīvraroṣa*garttāśrayā[K.garbhāśayā] nīcāḥ||

16.36  upahatadāmbhikarākṣasanidrābahulāśca jantavaḥ sarv

16.36  dharmeṇa ca saṃtyaktā māṣatilāścārkaśaśiśatroḥ||

16.37  giridurgapahlavaśvetahūṇacolāvagāṇamarucīnāḥ|

16.37  pratyantadhanimahecchavyavasāyaparākramopetāḥ||

16.38  paradāravivādaratāḥ pararandhrakutūhalā madotsiktāḥ|

16.38  mūrkhādhārmikavijigīṣavaśca ketoḥ samākhyātāḥ||

16.39  udayasamaye yaḥ snigdhāṃśurmahān prakṛtisthito

16.39 yadi ca na hato nirghātolkārajograhamardanaiḥ|

16.39 svabhavanagataḥ svoccaprāptaḥ śubhagrahavīkṣitaḥ

16.39 sa bhavati śivasteṣāṃ yeṣāṃ prabhuḥ parikīrtitaḥ||

16.40  abhihitaviparīta*lakṣaṇe[K.lakṣanaiḥ] kṣayamupagacchati tatparigrahaḥ|

16.40  ḍamarabhayagadāturā janā narapatayaśca bhavanti duḥkhitāḥ||

16.41  yadi na ripukṛtaṃ bhayaṃ nṛpāṇāṃ svasutakṛtaṃ niyamādamātyajaṃ vā|

16.41  bhavati janapadasya cāpyavṛṣṭyā gamanamapūrvapurādrinimnagāsu||



17 grahayuddhādhyāyaḥ|



17.01  yuddhaṃ yathā yadā vā *bhaviṣyam[K.bhaviṣyad] ādiśyate trikālajñaiḥ|

17.01  tadvijñānaṃ karaṇe mayā kṛtaṃ sūrya*siddhānte[K.siddhāntāt]||

17.02  viyati caratāṃ grahāṇāmuparyuparyātmamārgasaṃsthānām|

17.02  atidūrāddṛgviṣaye samatāmiva samprayātānām||

17.03  āsannakramayogādbhedollekhāṃśumardana*asavyaiḥ[K.asavyaḥ]|

17.03  yuddhaṃ catuṣprakāraṃ parāśarādyairmunibhiruktam||

17.04  bhede vṛṣṭivināśo bhedaḥ suhṛdāṃ mahākulānāṃ c

17.04  ullekhe śastrabhayaṃ mantrivirodhaḥ priyānnatvam||

17.05  amśuvirodhe yuddhāni bhūbhṛtāṃ śastrarukkṣudavamardāḥ|
17.05  yuddhe cāpyapasavye bhavanti yuddhāni bhūpānām||

17.06  ravirākrando madhye pauraḥ pūrve +apare sthito yāyī|

17.06  paurā budhagururavijā nityaṃ śītāṃśur*ākrandaḥ[K.ākrandraḥ]||

17.07  ketukujarāhuśukrā yāyina ete hatā *ghnanti[K.grahā hanyuḥ]|

17.07  ākrandayāyipaurān jayino *jayadāḥ[K.jayadā] svavargasy |

17.08  paure paureṇa hate paurāḥ paurānnṛpān vinighnanti|

17.08  evaṃ *yāyyākrandā[K.yāyyākrandau] nāgarayāyigrahāścaiv |

17.09  dakṣiṇadiksthaḥ paruṣo vepathuraprāpya sannivṛtto +aṇuḥ|

17.09  adhirūḍho vikṛto niṣprabho vivarṇaśca yaḥ sa jitaḥ||

17.10  uktaviparītalakṣaṇasampanno jayagato *vinirdeśyaḥ[K.vinirdiṣṭaḥ]|

17.10  vipulaḥ snigdho dyutimān dakṣiṇadikstho +api jayayuktaḥ||

17.11  dvāvapi mayūkhayuktau vipulau snigdhau samāgame bhavataḥ|

17.11  tatra *anyonyaṃ prītir[K.anyonyaprītir] viparītāvātmapakṣaghnau||

17.12  yuddhaṃ samāgamo vā yadyavyaktau *svalakṣaṇair[K.tu lakṣaṇair] bhavataḥ|

17.12  bhuvi bhūbhṛtāmapi tathā phalamavyaktaṃ vinirdeśyam||

17.13  guruṇā jite +avanisute bāhlīkā yāyino *+agnivārtāśca[K.agnivārttāśca]|

17.13  śaśijena śūrasenāḥ kaliṅga*śālvāś[K.sālvāś] ca pīḍyant |

17.14  saureṇāre vijite jayanti paurāḥ prajāśca sīdanti|

17.14  *koṣṭhāgāra[K.koṣṭhāgāra]mlecchakṣatriyatāpaśca śukrajit |

17.15  bhaumena hate śaśije vṛkṣasarittāpasāśmakanarendrāḥ|

17.15  uttaradiksthāḥ kratudīkṣitāśca santāpamāyānti||

17.16  guruṇā *jite budhe[K.budhe jite] mlecchaśūdracaurārthayuktapaurajanāḥ|

17.16  traigartapārvatīyāḥ pīḍyante kampate ca mahī||

17.17  ravijena budhe dhvaste nāvikayodhābjasadhanagarbhiṇyaḥ|

17.17  bhṛguṇā jite +agnikopaḥ sasyāmbudayāyividhvaṃsaḥ||

17.18  jīve śukrābhihate kulūtagāndhārakaikayā madrāḥ|

17.18  *śālvā[K.sālvā] vatsā vaṅgā gāvaḥ sasyāni *pīḍyante[K.naśyanti]||

17.19  bhaumena hate jīve madhyo deśo nareśvarā gāvaḥ|

17.19  saureṇa cārjunāyanavasātiyaudheyaśibiviprāḥ||

17.20  śaśitanayenāpi jite bṛhaspatau mlecchasatyaśastrabhṛtaḥ|

17.20  upayānti madhyadeśaśca saṃkṣayaṃ yacca bhaktiphalam||

17.21  śukre bṛhaspati*jite[K.hate] yāyī śreṣṭho vināśamupayāti|

17.21  brahmakṣatravirodhaḥ salilaṃ ca na vāsavastyajati||

17.22  kośalakaliṅgavaṅgā vatsā matsyāśca madhyadeśayutāḥ|

17.22  mahatīṃ vrajanti pīḍāṃ napuṃsakāḥ śūrasenāśc |

17.23  kujavijite bhṛgutanaye balamukhyavadho narendrasaṃgrāmāḥ|

17.23  saumyena pārvatīyāḥ kṣīravināśo +alpavṛṣṭiśc |

17.24  ravijena site vijite *guṇamukhyāḥ[ū.gaṇamukhyāḥ] śastrajīvinaḥ kṣatram|

17.24  jalajāśca nipīḍyante sāmānyaṃ bhaktiphalamanyat ||

17.25  asite sitena nihate +arghavṛddhirahivihagamānināṃ pīḍā|

17.25  kṣitijena taṅgaṇāndhroḍrakāśibāhlīkadeśānām||

17.26  saumyena parābhūte mande +aṅgavaṇigvihaṅgapaśunāgāḥ|

17.26  santāpyante guruṇā strībahulā mahiṣakaśakāśc |

17.27  ayaṃ viśeṣo +abhihito hatānāṃ kujajñavāgīśasitāsitānām|

17.27  phalaṃ tu vācyaṃ grahabhaktito +anyadyathā tathā ghnanti hatāḥ svabhaktīḥ||



18 śaśigrahasamāgamādhyāyaḥ



18.01  bhānāṃ yathāsambhavamuttareṇa yāto grahāṇāṃ yadi vā śaśāṅkaḥ|

18.01  pradakṣiṇaṃ tat*śubhadaṃ nṛpāṇāṃ[K.śubhakṛnnarāṇāṃ] yāmyena yāto na śivaḥ śaśāṅkaḥ||

18.02  candramā yadi kujasya yātyudakpārvatīyabalaśālināṃ jayaḥ|

18.02  kṣatriyāḥ pramuditāḥ sayāyino bhūridhānyamuditā vasundharā||

18.03  uttarataḥ svasutasya śaśaṅkaḥ paurajayāya subhikṣakaraśc

18.03  sasyacayaṃ kurute janahārdiṃ kośacayaṃ ca narādhipatīnām||

18.04  bṛhaspateruttarage śaśāṅke pauradvijakṣatriyapaṇḍitānām|

18.04  dharmasya deśasya ca madhyamasya vṛddhiḥ subhikṣaṃ muditāḥ prajāśc |

18.05  bhārgavasya yadi yātyudakśaśī kośayuktagajavājivṛddhidaḥ|

18.05  yāyināṃ ca vijayo dhanuṣmatāṃ sasyasampadapi cottamā tadā||

18.06  ravijasya śaśī pradakṣiṇaṃ kuryāccetpurabhūbhṛtāṃ jayaḥ|

18.06  śakabāhlikasindhupahlavā *mudabhājo[K.mudbhājo] yavanaiḥ samanvitāḥ||

18.07  yeṣāmudaggaccchati bhagrahāṇāṃ prāleyaraśmirnirupadravaśc

18.07  taddravyapauretarabhaktideśān puṣṇāti yāmyena nihanti tāni||

18.08  śaśini phalam *udaksthe[K.udakasthe] yadgrahasyopadiṣṭaṃ

18.08 bhavati tadapasavye sarvameva pratīpam|

18.08 iti śaśisamavāyāḥ *kīrtitā[K.kīrttitā] bhagrahāṇāṃ

18.08 na khalu bhavati yuddhaṃ sākamindorgraharkṣaiḥ||



19 grahavarṣaphalādhyāyaḥ



19.01  sarvatra bhūrviralasasyayutā vanāni

19.01 daivādbibhakṣayiṣudaṃṣṭrisamāvṛtāni|

19.01 *nadyaś[K.syandanti] ca naiva *hi[K.ca] payaḥ pracuraṃ *sravanti[K.sravantyo]

19.01 rugbheṣajāni na tathātibalānvitāni||

19.02  tīkṣṇaṃ tapatyaditijaḥ śiśire +api kāle

19.02 nātyambudā jalamuco +acalasannikāśāḥ|

19.02 naṣṭaprabharkṣagaṇaśītakaraṃ nabhaśca

19.02 sīdanti tāpasakulāni sagokulāni||

19.03  hastyaśvapattimadasahyabalairupetā

19.03 bāṇāsanāsimuśalātiśayāścaranti|

19.03 ghnanto nṛpā yudhi nṛpānucaraiśca deśān

19.03 saṃvatsare dinakarasya dine +atha mās|

19.04  vyāptaṃ nabhaḥ pracalitācalasannikāśair

19.04 vyālāñjanāligavalacchavibhiḥ payodaiḥ|

19.04 gāṃ pūrayadbhirakhilāmamalābhiradbhir

19.04 *utkaṇṭhitena[K.utkaṇṭhakena] guruṇā dhvanitena cāśāḥ||

19.05  toyāni padmakumudotpalavantyatīva

19.05 phulladrumāṇyupavanānyalināditāni|

19.05 gāvaḥ prabhūtapayaso nayanābhirāmā

19.05 rāmā ratairavirataṃ ramayanti rāmān||

19.06  godhūmaśāliyavadhānyavarekṣuvāṭā

19.06 bhūḥ pālyate nṛpatibhirnagarākarāḍhyā|

19.06 cityaṅkitā kratuvareṣṭivighuṣṭanādā

19.06 saṃvatsare śiśiragorabhisampravṛtt |

19.07  vātoddhataścarati vahniratipracaṇḍo

19.07 grāmān vanāni nagarāṇi ca sandidhakṣuḥ|

19.07 hāheti dasyugaṇapātahatā raṭanti

19.07 niḥsvīkṛtā vipaśavo bhuvi martyasaṃghāḥ||

19.08  abhyunnatā viyati saṃhatamūrtayo +api

19.08 muñcanti *kutra cid[K.na kva cid] apaḥ pracuraṃ payodāḥ|

19.08 sīmni prajātamapi śoṣamupaiti sasyaṃ

19.08 niṣpannamapyavinayādapare haranti||

19.09  bhūpā na samyagabhipālanasaktacittāḥ

19.09 pittottharukpracuratā bhujagaprakopaḥ|

19.09 evaṃvidhair*upahṛtā[K.ūpahatā] bhavati prajeyaṃ

19.09 saṃvatsare +avanisutasya vipannasasyā||

19.10  māyendrajālakuhakākaranāgarāṇāṃ

19.10 gāndharvalekhyagaṇitāstravidāṃ ca vṛddhiḥ|

19.10 piprīṣayā nṛpatayo +adbhutadarśanāni

19.10 ditsanti tuṣṭijananāni parasparebhyaḥ||

19.11  *vārtā[K.vārttā] jagatyavitathā vikalā trayī ca

19.11 samyakcaratyapi manoriva daṇḍanītiḥ|

19.11 *adhyakṣara[K.adhyakṣaraṃ]svabhiniviṣṭadhiyo *+api[K.atra] ke cid

19.11 ānvīkṣikīṣu ca paraṃ padamīhamānāḥ||

19.12  hāsyajñadūtakavibālanapuṃsakānāṃ

19.12 yuktijñasetujalaparvatavāsināṃ c

19.12 hārdiṃ karoti mṛgalāñchanajaḥ svake +abde

19.12 māse +atha vā *pracuratā[K.pracuratāṃ] bhuvi cāuṣadhīnām||

19.13  dhvaniruccarito +adhvare dyugāmī

19.13 vipulo yajñamuṣāṃ manāṃsi bhindan|

19.13 vicaratyaniśaṃ dvijottamānāṃ

19.13 hṛdayānandakaro +adhvarāṃśabhājām||

19.14  kṣitiruttamasasyavatyanekadvipapattyaśvadhanorugokulāḍhyā|

19.14  kṣitipairabhipālanapravṛddhā dyucaraspardhijanā tadā vibhāti||

19.15  vividhairviyadunnataiḥ payodairvṛtamurvīṃ payasābhitarpayadbhiḥ|

19.15  surarājaguroḥ śubhe *tu[K.atra] varṣe bahusasyā kṣitiruttamarddhiyuktā||

19.16  śālīkṣumatyapi dharā dharaṇīdharābha

19.16 dhārādharojjhitapayaḥparipūrṇavaprā|

19.16 śrīmatsaroruhatatāmbutaḍāgakīrṇā

19.16 yoṣeva bhātyabhinavābharaṇojjvalāṅgī||

19.17  kṣatraṃ kṣitau kṣapitabhūribalāripakṣam

19.17 udghuṣṭanaikajayaśabdavirāvitāśam|

19.17 saṃhṛṣṭaśiṣṭajanaduṣṭavinaṣṭavargāṃ

19.17 gāṃ pālayantyavanipā nagarākarāḍhyām||

19.18  pepīyate madhu madhau saha kāminībhir

19.18 jegīyate śravaṇahāri saveṇuvīṇam|

19.18 bobhujyate +atithisuhṛtsvajanaiḥ sahānnam

19.18 abde sitasya madanasya jayāvaghoṣaḥ||

19.19  *udvṛtta[ū.uddhata]dasyugaṇabhūriraṇākulāni

19.19 rāṣṭrāṇyanekapaśuvittavinākṛtāni|

19.19 rorūyamāṇahatabandhujanairjanaiśca

19.19 rogottamākulakulāni bubhukṣayā c |

19.20  vātoddhatāmbudharavarjitamantarikṣam

19.20 ārugṇanaikaviṭapaṃ ca dharātalaṃ dyauḥ|

19.20 naṣṭārkacandrakiraṇātirajo +avanaddhā

19.20 toyāśayāśca vijalāḥ sarito +api tanvyaḥ||

19.21  jātāni kutra cidatoyatayā vināśam

19.21 ṛcchanti puṣṭimaparāṇi jalokṣitāni|

19.21 sasyāni mandamabhivarṣati *vṛtraśatrur[K.vṛtraśatrau]

19.21 varṣe divākarasutasya sadā pravṛtt |

19.22  aṇurapaṭumayūkho nīcago +anyairjito vā

19.22 na sakalaphaladātā puṣṭido +ato +anyathā yaḥ|

19.22 yadaśubhamaśubhe +abde māsajaṃ tasya vṛddhiḥ

19.22 śubhaphalamapi caivaṃ yāpyamanyonyatāyām||



20 grahaśṛṅgāṭakādhyāyaḥ



20.01  yasyāṃ diśi dṛśyante viśanti tārāgrahā raviṃ sarv

20.01  bhavati bhayaṃ diśi tasyāmāyudhakopakṣudhaātaṅkaiḥ||

20.02  cakradhanuḥśṛṅgāṭakadaṇḍapuraprāsavajrasaṃsthānāḥ|

20.02  kṣudvṛṣṭikarā[K.vavṛṣṭikarā] loke samarāya ca mānavendrāṇām||

20.03  yasmin khāṃśe dṛśyā grahamālā dinakare dināntagat

20.03  tatra +anyo bhavati nṛpaḥ paracakropadravaśca mahān||

20.04  *tasminn[K.yasminn] ṛkṣe kuryuḥ samāgamaṃ tajjanān grahā hanyuḥ|

20.04  *avibhedinaḥ[K.avibhedanāḥ] parasparamamalamayūkhāḥ śivāsteṣām||

20.05  grahasaṃvartasamāgamasammohasamājasannipātākhyāḥ|

20.05  kośaścetyeteṣāmabhidhāsye lakṣaṇaṃ saphalaṃ||

20.06  ekarkṣe catvāraḥ saha paurairyāyino +atha vā pañc

20.06  saṃvarto nāma bhavetśikhirāhuyutaḥ sa sammohaḥ||

20.07  pauraḥ paurasameto yāyī saha yāyinā samājākhyaḥ|

20.07  yamajīvasaṅgame +anyo yadyāgacchettadā kośaḥ||

20.08  uditaḥ paścādekaḥ prākcānyo yadi sa sannipātākhyaḥ|

20.08  avikṛtatanavaḥ snigdhā vipulāśca samāgame dhanyāḥ||

20.09  samau tu saṃvartasamāgamākhyau sammohakośau bhayadau prajānām|

20.09  *samājasaṃjño susamā pradiṣṭā[K.samājñaḥ susamaḥ pradiṣṭo] vairaprakopaḥ khalu sannipāt |



21 garbhalakṣaṇādhyāyaḥ



21.01  annaṃ jagataḥ prāṇāḥ prāvṛṭkālasya cānnamāyattam|

21.01  yasmādataḥ parīkṣyaḥ prāvṛṭkālaḥ prayatnen |

21.02  tallakṣaṇāni munibhiryāni nibaddhāni tāni dṛṣṭvedam|

21.02  kriyate gargaparāśarakāśyapa*vajrādi[K.vātsyādi]racitāni||

21.03  daivavidavihitacitto[K.avahitacitto] dyuniśaṃ yo garbhalakṣaṇe bhavati|

21.03  tasya muneriva vāṇī na bhavati mithyāmbunirdeś |

21.04  kiṃ vātaḥ paramanyat*śāstrajyāyo[K.śāstraṃ jyāyo] +asti yadviditvaiv
21.04  pradhvaṃsinyapi kāle trikāladarśī kalau bhavati||

21.05  ke cidvadanti *kārtika[K.kārttika]śuklāntamatītya garbhadivasāḥ syuḥ|

21.05  na *ca[K.tu] tanmataṃ bahūnāṃ gargādīnāṃ mataṃ vakṣy |

21.06  *mārgaśiraḥsita[K.mārgaśiraśukla]pakṣapratipatprabhṛti kṣapākare +aṣāḍhām|

21.06  pūrvāṃ vā samupagate garbhāṇāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ jñeyam||

21.07  yannakṣatramupagate garbhaścandre bhavetsa candravaśāt |

21.07  pañcanavate dinaśate tatraiva prasavamāyāti||

21.08  sitapakṣabhavāḥ kṛṣṇe śukle kṛṣṇā dyusambhavā rātrau|

21.08  naktaṃprabhavāścāhani sandhyājātāśca sandhyāyām||

21.09  mṛgaśīrṣādyā garbhā mandaphalāḥ pauṣaśuklajātāśc

21.09  pauṣasya kṛṣṇapakṣeṇa nirdiśetśrāvaṇasya sitam||

21.10  māghasitotthā garbhāḥ śrāvaṇakṛṣṇe prasūtimāyānti|

21.10  māghasya kṛṣṇapakṣeṇa nirdiśedbhādrapadaśuklam||

21.11  phālgunaśuklasamutthā bhādrapadasyāsite vinirdeśyāḥ|

21.11  tasyaiva kṛṣṇapakṣodbhavāstu ye te +aśvayukśukl |

21.12  caitrasitapakṣajātāḥ kṛṣṇe +aśvayujasya vāridā garbhāḥ|

21.12  caitrāsitasambhūtāḥ *kārtikaśukle[K.kārttikaśukle] +abhivarṣanti||

21.13  pūrvodbhūtāḥ paścādaparotthāḥ prāgbhavanti jīmūtāḥ|

21.13  śeṣāsvapi dikṣvevaṃ viparyayo bhavati vāyośc |

21.14  hlādimṛdūdakśivaśakradigbhavo māruto viyadvimalam|

21.14  snigdhasitabahulapariveṣaparivṛtau hima*mayakhārkau[K.ū.mayūkhārkau]||

21.15  pṛthubahulasnigdhaghanaṃ ghanasūcīkṣurakalohitābhrayutam|

21.15  kākāṇḍamecakābhaṃ viyadviśuddhendunakṣatram||

21.16  suracāpamandragarjitavidyut*pratisūryakā[K.pratisūryakāḥ] śubhā sandhyā|

21.16  śaśiśivaśakrāśāsthāḥ śāntaravāḥ pakṣimṛgasaṅghāḥ||

21.17  vipulāḥ pradakṣiṇacarāḥ snigdhamayūkhā grahā nirupasargāḥ|

21.17  taravaśca nirupasṛṣṭāṅkurā naracatuṣpadā hṛṣṭāḥ||

21.18  garbhāṇāṃ puṣṭikarāḥ sarveṣāmeva yo +atra tu viśeṣaḥ|

21.18  svartusvabhāvajanito garbha*vivṛddhyai[K.vivṛddhau] tamabhidhāsy |

21.31 b[K.21.35 b]| pañcanimittaiḥ śatayojanaṃ tadardhārdhamekahānyā +ataḥ|

21.32 d[K.21.36 d]| ṣaḍvidyutā navābhraiḥ stanitena dvādaśa prasav |

21.33  krūragrahasaṃyukte karakāśanimatsyavarṣadā garbhāḥ|

21.35  ātmīyagarbhasamaye karakāmiśraṃ dadātyambhaḥ||

21.36  kāṭhinyaṃ yāti yathā cirakāladhṛtaṃ payaḥ payasvinyāḥ|

24.01  kanakaśilācayavivarajatarukusumāsaṅgimadhukarānurut

36.01  sitaraktapītakṛṣṇaṃ viprādīnāmabhāvāy |

36.02  nāgaranṛpatijayāvahamudagvidiksthaṃ vivarṇanāśāy

36.02  śāntāśāyāṃ dṛṣṭaṃ satoraṇaṃ nṛpativijayāy |

36.03  sarvadigutthaṃ satatothitaṃ ca bhayadaṃ narendrarāṣṭrāṇām|

36.03  caurāṭavikān hanyāddhūmānalaśakracāpābham||

36.04  gandharvanagaramutthitamāpāṇḍuramaśanipātavātakaram|

36.04  dīpte narendramṛtyurvāme +aribhayaṃ jayaḥ savy |

36.05  anekavarṇākṛti khe prakāśate puraṃ patākādhvajatoraṇānvitam|

36.05  yadā tadā nāgamanuṣyavājināṃ pibatyasṛgbhūri raṇe vasundharā||



37 pratisūryalakṣaṇādhyāyaḥ



37.01  pratisūryakaḥ praśasto divasakṛdṛtuvarṇasaprabhaḥ snigdhaḥ|

37.01  vaidūryanibhaḥ svacchaḥ śuklaśca kṣemasaubhikṣaḥ||

37.02  pīto vyādhiṃ janayatyaśokarūpaśca śastrakopāy

37.02  pratisūryāṇāṃ mālā dasyubhayātaṅkanṛpahantrī||

37.03  divasakṛtaḥ pratisūryo jalakṛdudagdakṣiṇe sthito +anilakṛt |

37.03  ubhayasthaḥ salilabhayaṃ nṛpamupari nihantyadho janahā||



[K.chap. 38 rajolakṣaṇaminserted



K.38.01  kathayanti pārthivavadhaṃ rajasā ghanatimirasañcayanibhen

K.38.01  avibhāvyamānagiripurataravaḥ sarvā diśaśchannāḥ||

K.38.02  yasyāṃ diśi dhūmacayaḥ prākprabhavati nāśameti vā yasyām|

K.38.02  āgacchati saptāhāttatra eva bhayaṃ na sandehaḥ||

K.38.03  śvete rajoghanaughe pīḍā syānmantrijanapadānāṃ c

K.38.03  nacirātprakopamupayāti śastramatisaṅkulā siddhiḥ||

K.38.04  arkodaye vijṛmbhati yadi dinamekaṃ dinadvayaṃ vāpi|

K.38.04  sthagayanniva gaganatalaṃ bhayamatyugraṃ nivedayati||

K.38.05  anavaratasañcayavahaṃ rajanīmekāṃ pradhānanṛpahantṛ|

K.38.05  kṣemāya ca śeṣāṇāṃ vicakṣaṇānāṃ narendrāṇām||

K.38.06  rajanīdvayaṃ visarpati yasmin rāṣṭre rajoghanaṃ bahulam|

K.38.06  paracakrasya āgamanaṃ tasminnapi sanniboddhavyam||

K.38.07  nipatati rajanītritayaṃ catuṣkamapyannarasavināśāy

K.38.07  rājñāṃ sainyakṣobho rajasi bhavetpañcarātrabhav |

K.38.08  ketvādyudayavimuktaṃ yadā rajo bhavati tīvrabhayadāyi|

K.38.08  śiśirādanyatrartau phalamavikalamāhu ācāryāḥ||]



38 nirghātalakṣaṇādhyāyaḥ



38.01  pavanaḥ pavanābhihato gaganādavanau yadā samāpatati|

38.01  bhavati tadā nirghātaḥ sa ca pāpo dīptavihagarutaḥ||

38.02  arkodaye +adhikaraṇikanṛpadhaniyodhāṅganāvaṇigveśyāḥ|

38.02  āpraharāṃśe +ajāvikamupahanyātśūdrapaurāṃśc |

38.03  āmadhyāhnādrājopasevino brāhmaṇāṃśca pīḍayati|

38.03  vaiśyajaladāṃstṛtīye caurān prahare caturthe tu||

38.04  astaṃ yāte nīcān prathame yāme nihanti sasyāni|

38.04  rātrau dvitīyayāme piśācasaṅghānnipīḍayati||

38.05  turagakariṇastṛtīye vinihanyādyāyinaścaturthe c

38.05  bhairavajarjaraśabdo yāti yatastāṃ diśaṃ hanti||



39 sasyajātakādhyāyaḥ



39.01  vṛścikavṛṣapraveśe bhānorye bādarāyaṇenoktāḥ|

39.01  grīṣmaśaratsasyānāṃ sadasadyogāḥ kṛtāsta im |

39.02  bhānoralipraveśe kendraistasmātśubhagrahākrāntaiḥ|

39.02  balavadbhiḥ saumyairvā *nirīkṣite[K.nirīkṣitair] graiṣmikavivṛddhiḥ||

39.03  aṣṭamarāśigate +arke guruśaśinoḥ kumbhasiṃha*saṃsthitayoḥ[K.sthitayoḥ]|

39.03  siṃhaghaṭasaṃsthayorvā niṣpattirgrīṣmasasyasy |

39.04  arkātsite dvitīye budhe +atha vā yugapadeva vā sthitayoḥ|

39.04  vyayagatayorapi tadvanniṣpattiratīva gurudṛṣṭyā||

39.05  śubhamadhye +alini sūryādguruśaśinoḥ saptame parā sampat |

39.05  alyādisthe savitari gurau dvitīye +ardhaniṣpattiḥ||

39.06  lābhahibukārthayuktaiḥ sūryādaligātsitenduśaśiputraiḥ|

39.06  sasyasya parā sampatkarmaṇi jīve gavāṃ cāgryā||

39.07  kumbhe gururgavi śaśī sūryo +alimukhe kujārkajau makar

39.07  niṣpattirasti mahatī paścātparacakrabhayarogam||

39.08  madhye pāpagrahayoḥ sūryaḥ sasyaṃ vināśayatyaligaḥ|

39.08  pāpaḥ saptamarāśau jātaṃ jātaṃ vināśayati||

39.09  arthasthāne krūraḥ saumyairanirīkṣitaḥ prathamajātam|

39.09  sasyaṃ nihanti paścāduptaṃ niṣpādayedvyaktam||

39.10  jāmitrakendrasaṃsthau krūrau sūryasya vṛścikasthasy

39.10  sasyavipattiṃ kurutaḥ saumyairdṛṣṭau na sarvatr |

39.11  vṛścikasaṃsthādarkātsaptamaṣaṣṭhopagau yadā krūrau|

39.11  bhavati tadā niṣpattiḥ sasyānāmarghaparihāniḥ||

39.12  vidhinānenaiva ravirvṛṣapraveśe śaratsamutthānām|

39.12  vijñeyaḥ sasyānāṃ nāśāya śivāya vā tajjñaiḥ||

39.13  triṣu meṣādiṣu sūryaḥ saumyayuto vīkṣito +api vā vicaran|

39.13  *graiṣmika[K.graṣmika]dhānyaṃ kurute *samargham[K.samartham] abhayopayogyaṃ c |

39.14  kārmukamṛgaghaṭasaṃsthaḥ *śāradasasyasya[K.śāradasya] tadvadeva raviḥ|

39.14  saṃgrahakāle jñeyo viparyayaḥ krūradṛg*yogāt[K.yāgāt]||



40 dravyaniścayādhyāyaḥ



40.01  ye yeṣāṃ dravyāṇāmadhipatayo rāśayaḥ samuddiṣṭāḥ|

40.01  munibhiḥ śubhāśubhārthaṃ tānāgamataḥ pravakṣyāmi||

40.02  vastrāvikakutupānāṃ masūragodhūmarālakayavānām|

40.02  sthalasambhavauṣadhīnāṃ kanakasya ca kīrtito meṣaḥ||

40.03  gavi vastrakusumagodhūmaśāliyavamahiṣasurabhitanayāḥ syuḥ|

40.03  mithune +api dhānyaśāradavallīśālūkakārpāsāḥ||

40.04  karkiṇi kodravakadalīdūrvāphalakandapatracocāni|

40.04  siṃhe tuṣadhānyarasāḥ siṃhādīnāṃ tvacaḥ saguḍāḥ||

40.05  ṣaṣṭhe +atasīkalāyāḥ kulatthagodhūmamudganiṣpāvāḥ|

40.05  saptamarāśau māṣā *yavagodhūmāḥ sasarṣapāścaiva[K.godhūmāḥ sarṣapāḥ sayavāḥ]||

40.06  aṣṭamarāśāvikṣuḥ saikyaṃ lohānyajāvikaṃ cāpi|

40.06  navame tu turagalavaṇāmbarāstratiladhānyamūlāni||

40.07  makare tarugulmādyaṃ saikyekṣusuvarṇakṛṣṇalohāni|

40.07  kumbhe salilajaphalakusumaratnacitrāṇi rūpāṇi||

40.08  mīne kapālasambhavaratnānyambūdbhavāni vajrāṇi|

40.08  snehāśca naikarūpā vyākhyātā matsyajātaṃ c |

40.09  rāśeścaturdaśārthāyasaptanavapañcamasthito jīvaḥ|

40.09  dvyekādaśadaśapañcāṣṭameṣu śaśijaśca vṛddhikaraḥ||

40.10  ṣaṭsaptamago hāniṃ vṛddhiṃ śukraḥ karoti śeṣeṣu|

40.10  upacayasaṃsthāḥ krūrāḥ śubhadāḥ śeṣeṣu hānikarāḥ||

40.11  rāśeryasya krūrāḥ pīḍāsthāneṣu saṃsthitā balinaḥ|

40.11  tatproktadravyāṇāṃ mahārghatā durlabhatvaṃ c |

40.12  iṣṭasthāne saumyā balino yeṣāṃ bhavanti rāśīnām|

40.12  taddravyāṇāṃ vṛddhiḥ *sāmarghyaṃ vallabhatvaṃ ca[K.sāmarthayamadurlabhatvaṃ ca]||

40.13  gocarapīḍāyāmapi rāśirbalibhiḥ śubhagrahairdṛṣṭaḥ|

40.13  pīḍāṃ na karoti tathā krūrairevaṃ viparyāsaḥ||



41 arghakāṇḍādhyāyaḥ



41.01  ativṛṣṭyulkādaṇḍān pariveṣagrahaṇaparidhipūrvāṃśc

41.01  dṛṣṭvāmāvāsyāyāmutpātān *paurṇamāsyāṃ[K.pūrṇamāsyāṃ] c |

41.02  brūyādarghaviśeṣān pratimāsaṃ rāśiṣu kramātsūry

41.02  anyatithāvutpātā ye te ḍamarārtaye rājñām||

41.03  meṣopagate sūrye grīṣmajadhānyasya saṃgrahaṃ *kṛtvā[K.kuryāt]|

41.03  vanamūlaphalasya vṛṣe caturthamāse tayorlābhaḥ||

41.04  mithunasthe sarvarasān dhānyāni ca saṃgrahaṃ samupanīy

41.04  ṣaṣṭhe māse vipulaṃ *vikretā[K.vikrīṇan] prāpnuyāllābham||

41.05  karkiṇyarke madhugandhatailaghṛtaphāṇitāni vinidhāy

41.05  dviguṇā dvitīyamāse labdhirhīnādhike chedaḥ||

41.06  siṃhe suvarṇamaṇicarmavarmaśastrāṇi mauktikaṃ rajatam|

41.06  pañcamamāse labdhirvikreturato +anyathā chedaḥ||

41.07  kanyāgate dinakare cāmarakharakarabhavājināṃ kretā|

41.07  ṣaṣṭhe māse dviguṇaṃ lābhamavāpnoti vikrīṇan||

41.08  taulini tāntavabhāṇḍaṃ maṇikambalakācapītakusumāni|

41.08  ādadyāddhānyāni ca *varṣārdhād[K.ṣaṇmāsād] dviguṇitā vṛddhiḥ||

41.09  vṛścikasaṃsthe savitari phalakandakamūlavividharatnāni|

41.09  varṣadvayamuṣitāni dviguṇaṃ lābhaṃ prayacchanti||

41.10  cāpagate gṛhṇīyāt*kuṅkuma[K.kuṅkama]śaṅkhapravālakācāni|

41.10  muktāphalāni ca tato varṣārdhāddviguṇatāṃ yānti||

41.11  *mṛgaghaṭasaṃsthe savitari gṛhṇīyāl[K.mṛgadhaṭage gṛhṇīyāddivākare] lohabhāṇḍadhānyāni|

41.11  sthitvā māsaṃ dadyāllābhārthī dviguṇamāpnoti||

41.12  savitari jhaṣamupayāte mūlaphalaṃ kandabhāṇḍaratnāni|

41.12  saṃsthāpya vatsarārdhaṃ lābhakamiṣṭaṃ samāpnoti||

41.13  rāśau rāśau yasmin śiśiramayūkhaḥ sahasrakiraṇo vā|

41.13  yukto +adhimitradṛṣṭastatrāyaṃ lābhako diṣṭaḥ||

41.14  savitṛsahitaḥ sampūrṇo vā śubhairyutavīkṣitaḥ

41.14 śiśirakiraṇaḥ sadyo +arghasya pravṛddhikaraḥ smṛtaḥ|

41.14 aśubhasahitaḥ sandṛṣṭo vā hinastyatha vā raviḥ

41.14 pratigṛhagatān bhāvān buddhvā vadetsadasatphalam||



42 indradhvajasampadadhyāyaḥ



42.01  brahmāṇamūcuramarā bhagavan śaktāḥ sma nāsurān samar

42.01  pratiyodhayitumatastvāṃ śaraṇyaśaraṇaṃ samupayātāḥ||

42.02  devānuvāca bhagavān kṣīrode keśavaḥ sa vaḥ ketum|

42.02  yaṃ dāsyati taṃ dṛṣṭvā nājau sthāsyanti vo daityāḥ||

42.03  labdhavarāḥ kṣīrodaṃ gatvā te tuṣṭuvuḥ surāḥ sendrāḥ|

42.03  śrīvatsāṅkaṃ kaustubhamaṇikiranodbhāsitoraskam||

42.04  śrīpatimacintyamasamaṃ *samaṃ tataḥ[K.samantataḥ] sarvadehināṃ sūkṣmam|

42.04  paramātmānamanādiṃ viṣṇumavijñātaparyantam||

42.05  taiḥ saṃstutaḥ sa devastutoṣa nārāyaṇo dadau caiṣām|

42.05  dhvajamasurasuravadhūmukhakamalavanatuṣāratīkṣṇāṃśum||

42.06  taṃ viṣṇutejobhavamaṣṭacakre rathe sthitaṃ bhāsvati ratnacitr

42.06  dedīpyamānaṃ śaradīva sūryaṃ dhvajaṃ samāsādya mumoda śakraḥ||
42.07  sa kiṅkiṇījāla*pariṣkṛtena[K.pariskṛtena] srakchatraghaṇṭāpiṭakānviten

42.07  samucchritenāmararāḍdhvajena ninye vināśaṃ samare +arisainyam||

42.08  uparicarasyāmarapo vasordadau cedipasya veṇumayīm|

42.08  yaṣṭiṃ tāṃ sa narendro vidhivatsampūjayāmās|

42.09  prīto mahena *maghavā[K.maghavān] prāhaivaṃ ye nṛpāḥ kariṣyanti|

42.09  vasuvadvasumantaste bhuvi siddhājñā bhaviṣyanti||

42.10  muditāḥ prajāśca teṣāṃ bhayarogavivarjitāḥ prabhūtānnāḥ|

42.10  dhvajaiva cābhidhāsyati jagati nimittaiḥ phalaṃ sadasat ||

42.11  pūjā tasya narendrairbalavṛddhijayārthibhiryathā pūrvam|

42.11  śakrājñayā prayuktā tāmāgamataḥ pravakṣyāmi||

42.12  tasya vidhānaṃ śubhakaraṇadivasanakṣatramaṅgalamuhūrtaiḥ|

42.12  prāsthānikairvanamiyāddaivajñaḥ sūtradhāraśc |

42.13  udyānadevatālayapitṛvanavalmīkamārgacitijātāḥ|

42.13  kubjordhvaśuṣkakaṇṭakivallīvandākayuktāśc |

42.14  bahuvihagālayakoṭarapavanānalapīḍitāśca ye taravaḥ|

42.14  ye ca syuḥ strīsaṃjñā na te śubhāḥ śakraketvarth |

42.15  śreṣṭho +arjuno *+ajakarṇaḥ[K.aśvakamaḥ] priyakadhavodumbarāśca pañcait

42.15  eteṣām *ekatamaṃ[K.anyatamaṃ] praśastamatha vāparaṃ vṛkṣam||

42.16  gaurāsitakṣitibhavaṃ sampūjya yathāvidhi dvijaḥ pūrvam|

42.16  vijane sametya rātrau spṛṣṭvā brūyādimaṃ mantram||

42.17  yānīha vṛkṣe bhūtāni tebhyaḥ svasti namo +astu vaḥ|

42.17  upahāraṃ gṛhītvemaṃ kriyatāṃ vāsaparyayaḥ||

42.18  pārthivastvāṃ varayate svasti te +astu nagottam

42.18  dhvajārthaṃ devarājasya pūjeyaṃ pratigṛhyatām||

42.19  chindyātprabhātasamaye vṛkṣamudakprāṅmukho +api vā bhūtvā|

42.19  paraśorjarjaraśabdo neṣṭaḥ snigdho ghanaśca hitaḥ||

42.20  nṛpajayadamavidhvaṃstaṃ patanamanākuñcitaṃ ca pūrvodak |

42.20  avilagnaṃ cānyatarau viparītamatastyajetpatitam||

42.21  chittvāgre caturaṅgulamaṣṭau mūle jale kṣipedyaṣṭim|

42.21  uddhṛtya puradvāraṃ śakaṭena nayenmanuṣyairvā||

42.22  arabhaṅge balabhedo nemyā nāśo balasya vijñeyaḥ|

42.22  arthakṣayo +akṣabhaṅge tathāṇibhaṅge ca varddhakinaḥ||

42.23  bhādrapadaśuklapakṣasyāṣṭamyāṃ nāgarairvṛto rājā|

42.23  daivajñasacivakañcukiviprapramukhaiḥ suveṣadharaiḥ||

42.24  ahatāmbarasaṃvītāṃ yaṣṭiṃ paurandarīṃ puraṃ pauraiḥ|

42.24  sraggandhadhūpayuktāṃ praveśayetśaṅkhatūryaravaiḥ||

42.25  rucirapatākātoraṇavanamālālaṅkṛtaṃ prahṛṣṭajanam|

42.25  sammārjitārcitapathaṃ suveṣagaṇikājanākīrṇam||

42.26  abhyarcitāpaṇagṛhaṃ prabhūtapuṇyāhavedanirghoṣam|

42.26  naṭanartakageyajñairākīrṇacatuṣpathaṃ nagaram||

42.27  tatra patākāḥ śvetā *bhavanti vijayāya[K.vijayāya bhavanti] rogadāḥ pītāḥ|

42.27  jayadāśca citrarūpā raktāḥ śastraprakopāy |

42.28  yaṣṭiṃ praveśayantīṃ nipātayanto bhayāya nāgādyāḥ|

42.28  bālānāṃ talaśabde saṃgrāmaḥ sattvayuddhe vā||

42.29  santakṣya punastakṣā vidhivadyaṣṭiṃ praropayedyantr

42.29  jāgaramekādaśyāṃ nareśvaraḥ kārayeccāsyām||

42.30  sitavastroṣṇīṣadharaḥ purohitaḥ śākravaiṣṇavairmantraiḥ|

42.30  juhuyādagniṃ sāmvatsaro nimittāni gṛhṇīyāt ||

42.31  iṣṭadravyākāraḥ surabhiḥ snigdho ghano +analo +arciṣmān|

42.31  śubhakṛdato +anyo *+aniṣṭo[K.neṣṭo] yātrāyāṃ vistaro +abhihitaḥ||

42.32  svāhāvasānasamaye svayamujjvalārciḥ

42.32 snigdhaḥ pradakṣiṇaśikho hutabhugnṛpasy

42.32 gaṅgādivākarasutājalacāruhārāṃ

42.32 dhātrīṃ *samudraraśanāṃ[K.samudrarasanāṃ] vaśagāṃ karoti||

42.33  cāmīkarāśokakuraṇṭakābja

42.33 vaidūryanīlotpalasannibhe +agnau|

42.33 na dhvāntamantarbhavane +avakāśaṃ

42.33 karoti ratnāṃśuhataṃ nṛpasy |

42.34  yeṣāṃ rathaughārṇavameghadantināṃ

42.34 samasvano +agniryadi vāpi dundubheḥ|

42.34 teṣāṃ madāndhebhaghaṭāvaghaṭṭitā

42.34 bhavanti yāne timiropamā diśaḥ||

42.35  dhvajakumbhahayebhabhūbhṛtāmanurūpe vaśameti bhūbhṛtām|

42.35  udayāstadharādharā +adharā himavadvindhyapayodharā dharā||

42.36  dviradamadamahīsarojalājā[K.lājaiḥ]ghṛtamadhunā ca hutāśane sagandh

42.36  praṇatanṛpaśiromaṇiprabhābhirbhavati puraśchuriteva bhūrnṛpasy |

42.37  uktaṃ yaduttiṣṭhati śakraketau śubhāśubhaṃ saptamarīcirūpaiḥ|

42.37  tajjanmayajñagrahaśāntiyātrāvivāhakāleṣvapi cintanīyam||

42.38  guḍapūpapāyasādyairviprānabhyarcya dakṣiṇābhiśc

42.38  śravaṇena dvādaśyāmutthāpyo +anyatra vā śravaṇāt ||

42.39  śakrakumāryaḥ kāryāḥ prāha manuḥ sapta pañca vā tajjñaiḥ|

42.39  nandopanandasaṃjñe *pādonārdhe dhvajocchrāyāt[K.pādenārdhena cocchrāyāt]||

42.40  ṣoḍaśabhāgābhyadhike jayavijaye dve vasundhare cāny

42.40  adhikā śakrajanitrī madhye +aṣṭāṃśena caitāsām||

42.41  prītaiḥ kṛtāni vibudhairyāni purā bhūṣaṇāni suraketoḥ|

42.41  tāni krameṇa dadyātpiṭakāni vicitrarūpāṇi||

42.52  harārkavaivasvataśakrasomairdhaneśavaiśvānarapāśabhṛdbhiḥ|

42.53  yathā tvamūrjaskaraṇaikarūpaiḥ samarcitastvābharaṇairudāraiḥ|

42.54  tvamantakaḥ sarvaharaḥ kṛśānuḥ *sahasraśīrṣaḥ[K.sahasraśīrśā] śatamanyurīḍyaḥ||

42.62  kravyādakauśikakapotakakākakaṅkaiḥ

43 nīrājanādhyāyaḥ



43.01  bhagavati jaladharapakṣmakṣapākarārkekṣaṇe kamalanābh

43.01  unmīlayati *turaṅga[K.turaṅgana]karinaranīrājanaṃ kuryāt ||

43.04  sarjodumbara*kakubhaśākhāmaya[K.śākhākakubhamayaṃ]śāntisadma kuśabahulam|

43.07  abhyarcitā na paruṣaṃ vaktavyā nāpi tāḍanīyāst

45 utpātādhyāyaḥ



45.01  yānatrerutpātān gargaḥ provāca tānahaṃ vakṣy

45.06  divyamapi śamamupaiti prabhūtakanakānnagomahīdānaiḥ|

45.06  rudrāyatane bhūmau godohātkoṭihomācc |

45.14  rakṣaḥpiśāca*gṛhyaka[ū.guhyaka]nāgānām *evam[K.etad] eva *nirdiṣṭam[K.nirdeśyam]|

45.16  madhuparkeṇa purodhā *bhakṣyair[K.bakṣair] balibhiśca vidhivadupatiṣṭhet |

45.18  rāṣṭre yasyānagniḥ pradīpyate dīpyate ca nendhanavān|

45.18  manujeśvarasya pīḍā tasya *ca[K.omitted] rāṣṭrasya vijñeyā||

45.19  jalamāṃsārdrajvalane nṛpativadhaḥ praharaṇe raṇo raudraḥ|

45.24  mantrair*āgneyaiḥ[K.vāhnaiḥ] kṣīravṛkṣātsamidbhir

46 mayūracitrakādhyāyaḥ



46.01  divyāntarikṣāśrayamuktamādau mayā phalaṃ śastamaśobhanaṃ c

48 paṭṭalakṣaṇādhyāyaḥ



48.01  vistaraśo nirdiṣṭaṃ paṭṭāṇāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ yadācāryaiḥ|

52.01  vāstujñānamathātaḥ kamalabhavānmuniparaṃ parāyātam|

54 vṛkṣāyurvedādhyāyaḥ



54.01  prāntacchāyāvinirmuktā na manojñā jalāśayāḥ|

55.19  ityete viṃśatiḥ proktāḥ prāsādāḥ saṃjñayā mayā|

55.19  yathoktānukrameṇaiva lakṣaṇāni vadāmyataḥ||

55.20  tatra ṣaḍaśrirmerurdvādaśabhaumo vicitrakuharaśc

55.20  dvārairyutaścaturbhirdvātriṃśaddhastavistīrṇaḥ||

55.21  triṃśaddhastāyāmo daśabhaumo mandaraḥ śikharayuktaḥ|

55.21  kailāso +api śikharavānaṣṭāviṃśo +aṣṭabhaumaśc |

55.23  vṛttaḥ samudganāmā padmaḥ padmākṛtiḥ śayā aṣṭau[K.śayānāṣṭau]|

55.23  śṛṅgeṇaikena bhavedekaiva ca bhūmikā tasy |

55.24  garuḍākṛtiśca garuḍo nandīti ca ṣaṭcatuṣkavistīrṇaḥ|

55.24  kāryastu[K.ca] saptabhaumo vibhūṣito +aṇḍaistu[K.ca] viṃśatyā||

55.25  kuñjara iti gajapṛṣṭhaḥ ṣoḍaśahastaḥ samantato mūlāt |

55.26  vṛṣa ekabhūmiśṛṅgo dvādaśahastaḥ samantato vṛttaḥ|

55.26  haṃso haṃsākāro ghaṭo +aṣṭahastaḥ kalaśarūpaḥ||

55.27  dvārairyutaścaturbhirbahuśikharo bhavati sarvatobhadraḥ|

55.27  bahuruciracandraśālaḥ ṣaḍviṃśaḥ pañcabhaumaśc |

55.28  siṃhaḥ siṃhākrānto dvādaśakoṇo +aṣṭahastavistīrṇaḥ|

55.28  catvāro +añjanarūpāḥ pañcāṇḍayutastu caturasraḥ[K.caturaśraḥ]||

55.29  bhūmikāṅgulamānena mayasyāṣṭottaraṃ śatam|

55.29  sārdhaṃ hastatrayaṃ caiva kathitaṃ viśvakarmaṇā||

55.30  prāhuḥ sthapatayaścātra matamekaṃ vipaścitaḥ|

55.31  prāsādalakṣaṇamidaṃ kathitaṃ samāsād

56 vajralepalakṣaṇādhyāyaḥ



56.01  āmaṃ tindukamāmaṃ kapitthakaṃ puṣpamapi ca śālmalyāḥ|

56.01  bījāni śallakīnāṃ dhanvanavalko vacā ceti||
