56.02  avatāryo +asya ca kalko dravyairetaiḥ samanuyojyaḥ||

56.03  śrīvāsakarasaguggulubhallātakakundurūkasarjarasaiḥ|

56.03  atasībilvaiśca yutaḥ kalko +ayaṃ vajralepākhyaḥ||
56.04  prāsādaharmyavalabhīliṅgapratimāsu kuḍyakūpeṣu|

56.04  santapto dātavyo varṣasahasrāyutasthāyī||

56.05  lākṣākunduruguggulugṛhadhūmakapitthabilvamadhyāni|

56.05  nāga*phalanimba[K.balāphala]tindukamadanaphalamadhūkamañjiṣṭhāḥ||

56.06  sarjarasarasāmalakāni ceti kalkaḥ kṛto dvitīyo +ayam|

56.06  vajrākhyaḥ prathamaguṇairayamapi teṣveva kāryeṣu||

56.07  gomahiṣājaviṣāṇaiḥ khararomṇā mahiṣacarmagavyaiśc

56.07  nimbakapittharasaiḥ saha vajratalo[K.vajrataro] nāma kalko +anyaḥ||

56.08  aṣṭau sīsakabhāgāḥ kāṃsasya dvau tu rītikābhāgaḥ|

56.08  mayakathito yogo +ayaṃ vijñeyo vajrasaṅghātaḥ||



57 pratimālakṣaṇādhyāyaḥ|



57.01  jālāntarage bhānau yadaṇutaraṃ darśanaṃ rajo yāti|

57.01  tadvindyātparamāṇuṃ prathamaṃ taddhi pramāṇānām||

57.02  paramāṇurajo bālāgra[K.vālāgra]likṣayūkaṃ[K.yūkā] yavo +aṅgulaṃ ceti|

57.02  aṣṭaguṇāni yathottaramaṅgulamekaṃ bhavati saṃkhyā[K.mātrā]||

57.03  devāgāradvārasyāṣṭāmśonasya yastṛtīyo +aṃśaḥ|

57.03  tatpiṇḍikāpramāṇaṃ pratimā taddviguṇaparimāṇā||

57.04  svairaṅgulapramāṇairdvādaśa vīstīrṇam[ū.vistīrṇam] āyataṃ ca mukham|

57.04  nagnajitā tu caturdaśa dairghyeṇa drāviḍaṃ kathitam||

57.05  nāsālalāṭacibukagrīvāścaturāṅgulāstathā karṇau|

57.05  dve aṅgule ca hanunī[K.hanuke] cibukaṃ ca dvyaṅgulaṃ vitatam[K.vistṛtam]||

57.06  aṣṭāṅgulaṃ lalāṭaṃ vistārāddvyaṅgulātpare śaṅkhau|

57.06  caturaṅgulau tu śaṅkhau karṇau tu dvyaṅgulau[K.dvyaṅgulaṃ] pṛthulau||

57.07  karṇopāntaḥ kāryo +ardhapañcame bhrūsamena sūtreṇ

57.07  karṇasrotaḥ sukumārakaṃ ca netra[K.nayana]prabandhasamam||

57.08  caturaṅgulaṃ vasiṣṭhaḥ kathayati netrāntakarṇayorvivaram|

57.08  adharo +aṅgulapramāṇastasyārdhenottroṣṭhaśc |

57.09  ardhāṅgulā tu gocchā vaktraṃ caturaṅgulāyataṃ kāryam|

57.09  vipulaṃ tu sārdhamaṅgulam *avyāttaṃ tryaṅgulaṃ[K.adhyāttattryaṅgulaṃ] vyāttam||

57.10  dvyaṅgulatulyau nāsāpuṭau ca nāsā puṭāgrato jñeyā|

57.10  syāddvyaṅgulamucchrāyaścaturaṅgulamantaraṃ cākṣṇoḥ||

57.11  dvyaṅgulamito +akṣikośo dve netre tattribhāgikā tārā|

57.11  dṛktārā pañcāṃśo netravikāśo +aṅgulaṃ bhavati||

57.12  paryantātparyantaṃ daśa bhruvo +ardhāṅgulaṃ bhruvorlekhā|

57.12  bhrūmadhyaṃ dvyaṅgulakaṃ bhūr[ū.bhrūr] dhairghyeṇāṅgulacatuṣkam||

57.13  kāryā tu keśarekhā bhrūbandhasamāṅgulārdhavistīrṇā|

57.13  netrānte karavīrakamupanyasedaṅgulapramitam||

57.14  dvātriṃśatpariṇāhāccaturdaśāyāmato +aṅgulāni śiraḥ|

57.14  dvādaśa tu citrakarmaṇi dṛśyante viṃśatiradṛśyāḥ||

57.15  āsyaṃ sakeśanicayaṃ ṣoḍaśa dairghyeṇa nagnijit[ū.nagnajit]proktam|

57.15  grīvā daśa vistīrṇā pariṇāhādviṃśatiḥ saikā||

57.16  kaṇṭhāddvādaśa hṛdayaṃ hṛdayānnābhī[K.nābhiś] ca tatpramāṇen

57.16  nābhīmadhyādmeḍhrāntaraṃ ca tattulyamevoktam||

57.17  ūrū cāṅgulamānaiścaturyutā viṃśatistathā jaṅgh

57.17  jānukapicche caturaṅgule ca pādau ca tattulyau||

57.18  dvādaśadīrghau ṣaṭpṛthutayā ca pādau trikāyatāṅguṣṭhau|

57.18  pañcāṅgulapariṇāhau pradeśinī tryaṅgulaṃ dīrghā||

57.19  aṣṭāṃśāṃśonāḥ śeṣāṅgulyaḥ[K.śeṣāṅgulayaḥ] krameṇa kartavyāḥ|

57.19  sacaturthabhāgamaṅgulamutsedho +aṅguṣṭhakasyoktaḥ||

57.20  aṅguṣṭhanakhaḥ kathitascaturthabhāgonamaṅgulaṃ tajjñaiḥ|

57.20  śeṣanakhānāmardhāṅgulaṃ kramātkiṃcidūnaṃ vā||

57.21  jaṅghāgre pariṇāhaścaturdaśoktastu vistarāt[K.vistaraḥ] pañc

57.21  madhye tu sapta vipulā pariṇāhāttriguṇitāḥ sapt |

57.22  aṣṭau tu jānumadhye vaipulyaṃ tryaṣṭakaṃ tu pariṇāhaḥ|

57.22  vipulau caturdaśorū madhye dviguṇaśca tatparidhiḥ||

57.23  kaṭiraṣṭādaśa vipulā catvāriṃśaccaturyutā paridhau|

57.23  aṅgulamekaṃ nābhī[K.nābhir] vedhena tathā pramāṇen |

57.24  catvāriṃśaddviyutā nābhīmadhyena madhyapariṇāhaḥ|

57.24  stanayoḥ ṣoḍaśa cāntaramūrdhvaṃ kakṣye[K.kakṣe] ṣaḍaṅgulik |

57.25  *aṣṭāvaṃsau dvādaśa bāhū kāryau[K.kāryāvaṣṭāvaṃsau dvādaśa bāhū] tathā prabāhū c

57.25  bāhū ṣaḍvistīrṇau[K.ṣaḍvistirṇau] pratibāhū tvaṅgulacatuṣkam||

57.26  ṣoḍaśa bāhū mūle pariṇāhāddvādaśāgrahaste c

57.26  vistāreṇa karatalaṃ ṣaḍaṅgulaṃ sapta dairghyeṇ |

57.27  pañcāṅgulāni madhyā pradeśinī madhyaparvadalahīnā|

57.27  anayā tulyā cānāmikā kaniṣṭhā tu parvonā||

57.28  parvadvayamaṅguṣṭhaḥ śeṣāṅgulyas[K.śeṣāṅgulayas] tribhistribhiḥ kāryāḥ|

57.28  nakhaparimāṇaṃ kāryaṃ sarvāsāṃ parvaṇo +ardhen |

57.29  deśānurūpabhūṣaṇaveṣālaṅkāramūrtibhiḥ kāryā|

57.29  pratimā lakṣaṇayuktā sannihitā vṛddhidā bhavati||

57.30  daśarathatanayo rāmo baliśca vairocaniḥ śataṃ viṃśam|

57.30  dvādaśahānyā śeṣāḥ pravarasamanyūnaparimāṇāḥ||

57.31  kāryo +aṣṭabhujo bhagavāṃścaturbhujo dvibhuja eva vā viṣṇuḥ|

57.31  śrīvatsāṅkitavakṣāḥ kaustubhamaṇibhūṣitoraskaḥ||

57.32  atasīkusumaśyāmaḥ pītāmbaranivasanaḥ prasannamukhaḥ|

57.32  kuṇḍalakirīṭadhārī pīnagaloraḥsthalāṃsabhujaḥ||

57.33  khaḍgagadāśarapāṇirdakṣiṇataḥ śāntidaścaturthakaraḥ|

57.33  vāmakareṣu ca kārmukakheṭakacakrāṇi śaṅkhaśc |

57.34  atha ca caturbhujamicchati śāntida eko gadādharaścānyaḥ|

57.34  dakṣiṇapārśve tv[K.hy] evaṃ vāme śaṅkhaśca cakraṃ c |

57.35  dvibhujasya tu śāntikaro dakṣiṇahasto +aparaśca śaṅkhadharaḥ|

57.35  evaṃ viṣṇoḥ pratimā kartavyā bhūtimicchadbhiḥ||

57.36  baladevo halapāṇirmadavibhramalocanaśca kartavyaḥ|

57.36  vibhrat[K.bibhrat] kuṇḍalamekaṃ śaṅkhendumṛṇālagauratanuḥ[K.vapuḥ]||

57.37  ekānaṃśā kāryā devī baladevakṛṣṇayormadhy

57.37  kaṭisaṃsthitavāmakarā sarojamitareṇa codvahatī||

57.38  kāryā caturbhujā yā vāmakarābhyāṃ sapustakaṃ kamalam|

57.38  dvābhyāṃ dakṣiṇapārśve varamarthiṣvakṣasūtraṃ c |

57.39  *vāmo +atha vāṣṭabhujāyāḥ[K.vāmeṣvaṣṭabhujāyāḥ] kamaṇḍaluścāpamambujaṃ śāstram|

57.39  varaśaradarpaṇayuktāḥ savyabhujāḥ sākṣasūtrāśc |

57.40  śāmbaśca gadāhastaḥ pradyumnaścāpabhṛtsurūpaśc

57.40  anayoḥ striyau ca kārye kheṭakanistriṃśadhāriṇyau||

57.41  brahmā kamaṇḍalukaraścaturmukhaḥ paṇkajāsanasthaśc

57.41  skandaḥ kumārarūpaḥ śaktidharo barhiketuśc |

57.42  śuklacaturviṣāṇo dvipo mahendrasya vajrapāṇitvam|

57.42  tiryaglalāṭasaṃsthaṃ tṛtīyamapi locanaṃ cihnam||

57.43  śambhoḥ śirasīndukalā vṛṣadhvajo +akṣi ca tṛtīyamapi cordhvam|

57.43  śūlaṃ dhanuḥ pinākaṃ vāmārdhe vā girisutārdham||

57.44  padmāṅkitakaracaraṇaḥ prasannamūrtiḥ sunīcakeśaśc

57.44  padmāsanopaviṣṭaḥ piteva jagato bhavati[K.bhavet] buddhaḥ||

57.45  ājānulambabāhuḥ śrīvatsāṅkaḥ praśāntamūrtiśc

57.45  digvāsāstaruṇo rūpavāṃśca kāryo +arhatāṃ devaḥ||

57.46  nāsālalāṭajaṅghorugaṇḍavakṣāṃsi connatāni raveḥ|

57.46  kuryādudīcyaveṣaṃ gūḍhaṃ pādāduro yāvat ||

57.47  bibhrāṇaḥ svakararuhe bāhubhyāṃ[K.pāṇibhyāṃ] paṅkaje mukuṭadhārī|

57.47  kuṇḍalabhūṣitavadanaḥ pralambahāro viyadga[K'str. viyaṅga]vṛtaḥ||

57.48  kamalodaradyutimukhaḥ kañcukaguptaḥ smitaprasannamukhaḥ|

57.48  ratnojjvalaprabhāmaṇḍalaśca kartuḥ śubhakaro +arkaḥ||

57.49  saumyā tu hastamātrā vasudā hastadvayocchritā pratimā|

57.49  kṣemasubhikṣāya bhavettricaturhastapramāṇā yā||

57.50  nṛpabhayamatyaṅgāyāṃ hīnāṅgāyāmakalyatā kartuḥ|

57.50  śātodaryāṃ kṣudbhayamarthavināśaḥ kṛśāṅgāyām[K.kṛśāyāṃ ca]||

57.51  maraṇaṃ tu sakṣatāyāṃ śastranipātena nirdiśetkartuḥ|

57.51  vāmāvanatā patnīṃ dakṣiṇavinatā hinastyāyuḥ||

57.52  andhatvamūrdhvadṛṣṭyā karoti cintāmadhomukhī dṛṣṭiḥ|

57.52  sarvapratimāsvevaṃ śubhāśubhaṃ bhāskaroktasamam||

57.53  liṅgasya vṛttaparadhiṃ dairghyeṇāsūtrya tattridhā vibhajet |

57.53  mūle taccaturasraṃ[K.caturaśraṃ] madhye tvaṣṭāśriṃ vṛttamataḥ||

57.54  caturasram[K.caturaśram] avanikhāte madhyaṃ kāryaṃ tu piṇḍikāśvabhr

57.54  dṛśyocchrāyeṇa samā samantataḥ piṇḍikā[K.piṇḍakā] śvabhrāt ||

57.55  kṛśadīrghaṃ deśaghnaṃ pārśvavihīnaṃ purasya nāśay

57.55  yasya kṣataṃ bhavedmastake vināśāya talliṅgam||

57.56  mātṛgaṇaḥ kartavyaḥ svanāmadevānurūpakṛtacihnaḥ|

57.56  revanto +aśvārūḍho mṛgayākrīḍādiparivāraḥ||

57.57  daṇḍī yamo mahiṣago haṃsārūḍhaśca pāśabhṛdvaruṇaḥ|

57.57  naravāhanaḥ kubero vāmakirīṭī bṛhatkukṣiḥ||

[K.58.58  pramathādhipo gajamukhaḥ pralambajaṭharaḥ kuṭhāradhārī syāt |

K.58.58  ekaviṣāṇo bibhranmūlakakandaṃ sunīladalakandam||]



58 vanasampraveśādhyāyaḥ



58.01  karturanukūladivase daivajñaviśodhite śubhanimitt

58.01  maṅgalaśakunaiḥ prāsthānikaiśca vanasampraveśaḥ syāt ||

58.02  pitṛvanamārgasurālayavalmīkodyānatāpasāśramajāḥ|

58.02  caityasaritsaṅgamasambhavāśca ghaṭatoyasiktāśc |

58.03  kubjānujātavallīnipīḍitā vajramārutopahatāḥ|

58.03  svapatitahastinipīḍitaśuṣkāgnipluṣṭamadhunilayāḥ||

58.04  taravo varjayitavyāḥ śubhadāḥ syuḥ snigdhapatrakusumaphalāḥ|

58.04  abhimatavṛkṣaṃ gatvā kuryātpūjāṃ sabalipuṣpām||

58.05  suradārucandanaśamīmadhūkataravaḥ śubhā dvijātīnām|

58.05  kṣatrasyāriṣṭāśvatthakhadirabilvā vivṛddhikarāḥ||

58.06  vaiśyānāṃ jīvakakhadirasindhukasyandanāś[K.spandanāś] ca śubhaphaladāḥ|

58.06  tindukakesarasarjārjunāmraśālāśca śūdrāṇām||

58.07  liṅgaṃ vā pratimā vā drumavatsthāpyā yathā diśaṃ yasmāt |

58.07  tasmāccihnayitavyā diśo drumasyordhvamatha vādhaḥ|

58.08  paramānnamodakaudanadadhipalalollopikādibhirbhakṣyaiḥ|

58.08  madyaiḥ kusumairdhūpairgandhaiśca taruṃ samabhyarcy |

58.09  surapitṛpiśācarākṣasabhujagāsuragaṇavināyakādyānām|

58.09  kṛtvā rātrau pūjāṃ vṛkṣaṃ saṃspṛśya ca brūyāt ||

58.10  arcārthamamukasya tvaṃ devasya parikalpitaḥ|

58.10  namaste vṛkṣa pūjeyaṃ vidhivatsampragṛhyatām||

58.11  yānīha bhūtāni vasanti tāni

58.11 baliṃ gṛhītvā vidhivatprayuktam|

58.11 anyatra vāsaṃ parikalpyantu

58.11 kṣamantu tānyadya namo +astu tebhyaḥ||

58.12  vṛkṣaṃ prabhāte salilena siktvā

58.12 pūrvottarasyāṃ diśi sannikṛty

58.12 madhvājyadigdhena[K.liptena] kuṭhārakeṇa

58.12 pradakṣiṇaṃ śeṣamato nihanyāt[K.+abhihanyāt]||

58.13  pūrveṇa pūrvottarato +atha vodak

58.13 patedyadā vṛddhikarastadā syāt |

58.13 āgneyakoṇātkramaśo +agnidāha

58.13 rugrāga[K.kṣudroga]rogāsturagakṣayaśc |

58.14  yannoktamasmin vanasampraveśe

58.14 nipātavicchedanavṛkṣagarbhāḥ|

58.14 indradhvaje vāstuni ca pradiṣṭāḥ

58.14 pūrvaṃ mayā te +atra tathaiva yojyāḥ||



59 pratimāpratiṣṭhāpanādhyāyaḥ



59.01  diśi yāmyāyāṃ[K.saumyāyāṃ] kuryādadhivāsanamaṇḍapaṃ budhaḥ prāgvā|

59.01  toraṇacatuṣṭayayutaṃ śastadrumapallavacchannam||

59.02  pūrve bhāge citrāḥ srajaḥ patākāśca maṇḍapasyoktāḥ|

59.02  āgneyyāṃ diśi raktāḥ kṛṣṇāḥ syuryāmyanairṛtyoḥ[K.nairṛtayoḥ]||

59.03  śvetā diśyaparasyāṃ vāyavyāyāṃ tu pāṇḍurā ev

59.03  citrāścottarapārśve pītāḥ pūrvottare kāryāḥ[K.koṇe]||

59.04  āyuḥśrībalajayadā dārumayī mṛṇmayī[K.mṛnmayī] tathā pratimā|

59.04  lokahitāya maṇimayī sauvarṇī puṣṭidā bhavati||

59.05  rajatamayī kīrtikarī prajāvivṛddhiṃ karoti tāmramayī|

59.05  bhūlābhaṃ tu mahāntaṃ śailī pratimātha vā liṅgam||

59.06  śaṅkhūpahatā pratimā pradhānapuruṣaṃ kulaṃ ca ghātayati|

59.06  śvabhropahatā rogānupadravāṃśca kṣayam[K.akṣayān] kurut |

59.07  maṇḍapamadhye sthaṇḍilamupalipyāstīrya sikatayātha kuśaiḥ|
59.07  bhadrāsanakṛtaśīrṣopadhānapādāṃ nyasetpratimām||

59.08  plakṣāśvatthodumbaraśirīṣavaṭasambhavaiḥ kaṣāyajalaiḥ|

59.08  maṅgalyasaṃjñitābhiḥ sarvāuṣadhibhiḥ kuśādyābhiḥ||

59.09  dvipavṛṣabhoddhata[K.uddhṛta]parvatavalmīkasaritsamāgamataṭeṣu|

59.09  padmasaraḥsu ca mṛdbhiḥ sapañcagavyaiśca tīrthajalaiḥ||

59.10  pūrvaśiraskāṃ snātāṃ suvarṇaratnāmbubhiśca sasugandhaiḥ|

59.10  nānātūryaninādaiḥ puṇyāhairvedanirghoṣaiḥ||

59.11  aindryāṃ diśīndraliṅgā mantrāḥ prāgdakṣiṇe +agniliṅgāśc

59.11  vaktavyā[K.japtavyā] dvijamukhyaiḥ pūjyāste dakṣiṇābhiśc |

59.12  yo devaḥ saṃsthāpyastanmantraiścānalaṃ dvijo juhuyāt |

59.12  agninimittāni mayā proktānīndradhvajotthāne[K.ucchrāye]||

59.13  dhūmākulo +apasavyo muhurmuhurviphuliṅgakṛnna śubhaḥ|

59.13  hotuḥ smṛtilopo vā prasarpaṇaṃ cāśubhaṃ proktam||

59.14  snātāmabhuktavastrāṃ svalaṅkṛtāṃ pūjitāṃ kusumagandhaiḥ|

59.14  pratimāṃ svāstīrṇāyāṃ śayyāyāṃ sthāpakaḥ kuryāt ||

59.15  suptāṃ *sagītanṛtyairjāgaraṇaiḥ[K.sunṛtyagītairjāgarakaiḥ] samyagevamadhivāsy

59.15  daivajñasampradiṣṭe kāle saṃsthāpanaṃ kuryāt ||

59.16  abhyarcya kusumavastrānulepanaiḥ śaṅkhatūryanirghoṣaiḥ|

59.16  prādakṣiṇyena nayedāyatanasya prayatnen |

59.17  kṛtvā baliṃ prabhūtaṃ sampūjya brāhmaṇāṃśca sabhyāṃśc

59.17  dattvā hiraṇyaśakalaṃ vinikṣipetpiṇḍikāśvabhr

59.18  sthāpakadaivajñadvijasabhyasthapatīn viśeṣato +abhyarcy

59.18  kalyāṇānāṃ bhāgī bhavatīha paratra ca svargī||

59.19  viṣṇorbhāgavatānmagāṃśca savituḥ śambhoḥ sabhasmadvijān

59.19 mātṝṇāmapi maṇḍalakramavido[K.mātṛmaṇḍalavido] viprān vidurbrahmaṇaḥ|

59.19 śākyān sarvahitasya śāntamanaso nagnān jinānāṃ vidur

59.19 ye yaṃ devamupāśritāḥ svavidhinā taistasya kāryā kriyā||

59.20  udagayane sitapakṣe śiśiragabhastau ca jīvavargasth

59.20  lagne sthire sthirāṃśe saumyairdhīdharmakendragataiḥ||

59.21  pāpairupacayasaṃsthairdhruvamṛduharitiṣyavāyudeveṣu|

59.21  vikuje dine +anukūle devānāṃ sthāpanaṃ śastam||

59.22  sāmānyamidaṃ samāsato lokānāṃ hitadaṃ mayā kṛtam|

59.22  adhivāsanasanniveśane sāvitre pṛthageva vistarāt ||



60 golakṣaṇādhyāyaḥ



60.01  parāśaraḥ prāha bṛhadrathāya

60.01 golakṣaṇaṃ yatkriyate tato +ayam|

60.01 sarvāstathā +apyāgamato +abhidhāsy |

60.02  sāsrāvilarūkṣākṣyo mūṣakanayanāśna śubhadā gāvaḥ|

60.02  pracalaccipiṭaviṣāṇāḥ karaṭāḥ kharasadṛśavarṇāś[K.varnāḥ] c |

60.03  daśasaptacaturdantyaḥ pralambamuṇḍānanā vinatapṛṣṭhyaḥ[K.pṛṣṭhāḥ ū.pṛṣṭhaḥ]|

60.03  hrasvasthūlagrīvā yavamadhyā dāritakhurāśc |

60.05  vṛṣabho +apyevaṃ sthūlātilambavṛṣaṇaḥ śirātatakroḍaḥ|

60.05  sthūlaśirācitagaṇḍastristhānaṃ mehate yaśc |

60.06  mārjārākṣaḥ kapilaḥ karaṭo vā na śubhado dvijasyaiva[K.dvijasyeṣṭaḥ]|

60.06  kṛṣṇauṣṭhatālujihvaḥ śvasano yūthasya ghātakaraḥ||

60.07  sthūlaśakṛnmaṇiśṛṅgaḥ sitodaraḥ kṛṣṇasāravarṇaśc

60.07  gṛhajāto +api tyājyo yūthavināśāvaho vṛṣabhaḥ||

60.08  śyāmakapuṣpacitāṅgo bhasmāruṇasannibho biḍālākṣaḥ|

60.08  viprāṇāmapi na śubhaṃ karoti vṛṣabhaḥ parigṛhītaḥ||

60.09  ye coddharanti pādān paṅkādiva yojitāḥ kṛśagrīvāḥ|

60.09  kātaranayanā hīnāśca pṛṣṭhataste na bhārasahāḥ||

60.10  mṛdusaṃhatatāmrauṣṭhāstanusphijastāmratālujihvāśc

60.10  hrasvatanu[K.tanuhrasvo]uccaśravaṇāḥ sukukṣayaḥ spṛṣṭa[K.spaṣṭa]jaṅghāśc |

60.11  ātāmrasaṃhatakhurā vyūḍhoraskā bṛhatkakudayuktāḥ|

60.11  snigdhaślakṣṇatanutvagromāṇastāmratanuśṛṅgāḥ||

60.12  tanubhūspṛgvāladhayo raktāntavilocanā mahocchvāsāḥ|

60.12  siṃhaskandhāstanvalpakambalāḥ pūjitāḥ sugamāḥ[K.ū.sugatāḥ]||

60.13  vāmāvartairvāme dakṣiṇapārśve ca dakṣiṇāvartaiḥ|

60.13  śubhadā bhavantyanaḍuho jaṅghābhiścaiṇakanibhābhiḥ||

60.14  vaidūrya[K.vaiḍūrya]mallikābudbudekṣaṇāḥ sthūlanetrapakṣmāṇaḥ[K.varmāṇaḥ]|

60.14  pārṣṇibhirasphuṭitābhiḥ śastāḥ sarve ca[K.api] bhārasahāḥ||

60.16  lambairvṛṣaṇairmeṣodaraśca saṃkṣiptavaṃkṣaṇa[K.kṣaṇā]kroḍaḥ|

60.19  miśraphalo +api grāhyo yadi naikāntapraśasto +asti||



61 śvalakṣaṇādhyāyaḥ



61.01  pādāḥ pañcanakhāstrayo +agracaraṇaḥ ṣaḍbhirnakhairdakṣiṇas

62 kukkuṭalakṣaṇādhyāyaḥ



62.01  kukkuṭastvṛjutanūruhāṅgulistāmravaktranakhacūlikaḥ sitaḥ|

62.01  rauti susvaramuṣātyaye ca yo vṛddhidaḥ sa nṛparāṣṭravājinām||

62.02  yavagrīvo yo vā badarasadṛśo vāpi vihago

62.02 bṛhanmūrdhā varṇairbhavati bahubhiśca ruciraḥ|

62.02 sa śastaḥ saṃgrāme madhumadhupavarṇaśca jayakṛd

62.02 na śasto yo +ato +anyaḥ kṛśatanuravaḥ khañjacaraṇaḥ||

62.03  kukkuṭī ca mṛducārubhāṣiṇī snigdhamūrtirucirānaneṣaṇā|

62.03  sā dadāti suciraṃ mahīkṣitāṃ śrīyaśovijayavīryasampadaḥ||



63 kūrmalakṣaṇādhyāyaḥ



63.01  sphuṭikarajatavarṇo nīlarājīvicitraḥ

63.01 aruṇasamavapurvā sarṣapākāracitraḥ

63.02  añjanabhṛṅgaśyāmatanurvā binduvicitro +avyaṅgaśarīraḥ|

63.03 gūḍhacchidraścoru[K.ū.cāru]vaṃśaśca śastaḥ|

63.03 krīḍāvāpyāṃ toyapūrṇe maṇau vā

63.03 kāryaḥ kūrmo maṅgalārthaṃ narendraiḥ||



64 chāgalakṣaṇādhyāyaḥ



64.01  chāgaśubhāśubhalakṣaṇamabhidhāsye navadaśāṣṭadantāst

64.01  dhanyāḥ sthāpyā veśmani santyājyāḥ saptadantā y |

64.02  ṛṣyanibhakṛṣṇalohitavarṇānāṃ śvetamati[K.api]śubhadam||

64.04  muṇḍāḥ sarve śubhadāḥ sarvasitāḥ sarvakṛṣṇadehāśc

64.04  ardhāsitāḥ sitārdhā dhanyāḥ kapilārdhakṛṣṇāśc |

64.05  sa śubhaḥ sitamūrdhā vā mūrdhani vā kṛttikā[K.ṭikkikā] yasy |

64.06  sapṛṣatakaṇṭhaśirā vā tilapiṣṭanibhaśca tāmradṛkśastaḥ|

64.07  yo vā carati saśabdaṃ mandaṃ ca sa śobhanaśchāgaḥ||

64.08  ṛṣyaśiroruhapādo yo vā prākpāṇḍuro +apare nīlaḥ|

64.08  sa bhavati śubhakṛcchāgaḥ ślokaścāpyatra gargoktaḥ||

64.09  kuṭṭakaḥ kuṭilaścaiva jaṭilo vāmanastathā|

64.10  athāpraśastāḥ kharatulyanādāḥ

64.10 pradīptapucchāḥ kunakhā vivarṇāḥ|

64.10 nikṛttakarṇā dvipamastakāśca

65 aśvalakṣaṇādhyāyaḥ



65.01  dīrghagrīvākṣikūṭastrikahṛdayapṛthustāmratālvoṣṭhajihvaḥ

65.05  ṣaṣbhirdantaiḥ sitābhairbhavati hayaśiśustaiḥ kaśāyairdvivarṣaiḥ

66 hastilakṣaṇādhyāyaḥ



66.01  madhvābhadantāḥ suvibhaktadehā

66.05 saṅkīrṇanāgasya mado vimiśraḥ||

66.06  tāmrauṣṭhatāluvadanāḥ kalaviṅkanetrāḥ

66.06 snigdhonnatāgradaśanāḥ pṛthulāyatāsyāḥ|
66.06 cāponnatāyatanigūḍhanimagnavaṃśās

66.07  vistīrṇakarṇahanunābhilalāṭaguhyāḥ

66.07 kūrmonnatadvinavaviṃśatibhirnakhaiśc

66.07 rekhātrayopacitavṛttakarāḥ suvālā

66.07 dhanyāḥ sugandhimadapuṣkaramārutāśc |

66.08  dīrghāṅguliraktapuṣkarāḥ sajalāmbhodaninādabṛṃhiṇaḥ|

66.08  bṛhadāyatavṛttakandharā dhanyā bhūmipatermataṅgajāḥ||

66.09  nimarda[K.ū.nirmadā]abhyadhikahīnanakhāṅgān

66.09 kubjavāmanakameṣaviṣāṇān|

66.09 śyāvanīlaśabalāsitatālūn||

66.10 garbhiṇī ca nṛpatiḥ paradeśaṃ

66.10 prāpayedativirūpaphalāst |



67  puruṣalakṣanādhyāyaḥ



67.01  unmānamānagatisaṃhatisāravarṇa

67.01 snehasvaraprakṛtisattvamanūkamādau|

67.01 kṣetraṃ mṛjāṃ ca vidhivatkuśalo +avalokya

67.01 sāmudravidvadati yātamanāgataṃ vā[K.ca]||

67.03  śūrpākāravirūkṣapāṇḍuranakhau vakrau śirāsantatau

67.03 mārgāyotkaṭakau kaṣāyasadṛśau vaṃśasya vicchedadau[K.vicchittidau]

67.03 brahmaghnau paripakvamṛddyutitalau pītāv*agamyāratau[K.agamyaratau]||

67.04  praviralatanuromavṛttajaṅghā

67.04 dviradakarapratimairvarorubhiśc

67.04 upacitasamajānavaśca bhūpā

67.04 dhanarahitāḥ śvaśṛgālatulyajaṅghāḥ||

67.05  romaikaikaṃ kūpake pārthivānāṃ

67.05 dve dve jñeye paṇḍitaśrotriyāṇām|

67.05 tryādyairniḥsvā mānavā duḥkhabhājaḥ

67.05 keśāścaivaṃ ninditāḥ pūjitāśc |

67.06  nirmāṃsajānurmriyate pravāse

67.06 saubhāgyamalpairvikaṭairdaridrāḥ|

67.06 strīnirjitāścaiva[K.cāpi] bhavanti nimnai

67.06 rājyaṃ samāṃsaiśca mahadbhirāyuḥ||

67.07  liṅge +alpe dhanavānapatyarahitaḥ sthūle *+api hīno[K.vihīno] dhanair

67.07 meḍhre vāmanate sutārtharahito vakre +anyathā putravān|

67.07 dāridryaṃ vinate tvadho +alpatanayo liṅge śirāsantate

67.07 sthūlagranthiyute sukhī mṛdu karotyantaṃ pramehādibhiḥ||

67.08  kośanigūḍhairbhūpā dīrghairbhagnaiśca vittaparihīnāḥ|

67.08  ṛjuvṛttaśephaso laghuśirālaśiśnāśca dhanavantaḥ||

67.09  jalamṛtyurekavṛṣaṇo viṣamaiḥ strīcañcalaḥ samaiḥ kṣitipaḥ|

67.09  hrasvāyuścodbaddhaiḥ pralambavṛṣaṇasya śatamāyuḥ||

67.10  raktairāḍhyā maṇibhirnirdravyāḥ pāṇḍuraiśca malinaiśc

67.10  sukhinaḥ saśabdamūtrā niḥsvā niḥśabdadhārāśc |

67.11  dvitricaturdhārābhiḥ pradakṣiṇāvartavalitamūtrābhiḥ|

67.11  pṛthivīpatayo jñeyā vikīrṇamūtrāśca dhanahīnāḥ||

67.12  ekaiva mūtradhārā valitā *rūpapradā na sutadātrī[K.rūpapradhānasutadātrī]|

67.12  snigdhonnatasamamaṇayo dhanavanitāratnabhoktāraḥ|

67.13  maṇibhiśca madhyanimnaiḥ kanyāpitaro bhavanti niḥsvāśc

67.13  bahupaśubhājo madhyonnataiśca nātyulbaṇairdhaninaḥ||

67.14  pariśuṣkabastiśīrṣairdhanarahitā durbhagāśca vijñeyāḥ|

67.14  kusumasamagandhaśukrā vijñātavyā mahīpālāḥ||

67.15  madhugandhe bahuvittā matsyasagandhe bahūnyapatyāni|

67.15  tanuśukraḥ strījanako māṃsasagandho mahābhogī||

67.16  madirāgandhe yajvā kṣārasagandhe ca retasi daridraḥ|

67.16  śīghraṃ maithunagāmī dīrghāyurato +anyathālpāyuḥ||

67.17  niḥsvo +atisthūlasphiksamāṃsalasphiksukhānvito bhavati|

67.17  vyāghrānto +adhyardhasphigmaṇḍūkasphignarādhipatiḥ||

67.18  siṃhakaṭirmanujendraḥ kapikarabhakaṭirdhanaiḥ parityaktaḥ|

67.18  samajaṭharā bhogayutā ghaṭapiṭharanibhodarā niḥsvāḥ||

67.19  avikalapārśvā dhanino nimnairvakraiśca bhogasantyaktāḥ|

67.19  samakukṣā bhogāḍhyā nimnābhirbhogaparihīnāḥ||

67.20  unnatakukṣāḥ kṣitipāḥ kuṭilāḥ syurmānavā viṣamakukṣāḥ|

67.20  sarpodarā daridrā bhavanti bahvāśinaścaiv |

67.21  parimaṇḍalonnatābhirvistīrṇābhiśca nābhibhiḥ sukhinaḥ|

67.21  alpā[K.svalpā] tvadṛśyanimnā nābhiḥ kleśāvahā bhavati||

67.22  valimadhyagatā viṣamā *śūlādbādhāṃ[K.śūlābādhaṃ] karoti naisvyaṃ[K.naiḥsvyaṃ] c

67.22  śāṭhyaṃ vāmāvartā karoti medhāṃ pradakṣiṇataḥ||

67.23  pārśvāyatā cirāyuṣamupariṣṭācceśvaraṃ gavāḍhyamadhaḥ|

67.23  śatapatrakarṇikābhā nābhirmanujeśvaraṃ kurut |

67.24  śastrāntaṃ strībhoginamācāryaṃ bahusutaṃ yathāsaṃkhyam|

67.24  ekadvitricaturbhirvalibhirvindyādnṛpaṃ tvavalim||

67.25  viṣamavalayo manuṣyā bhavantyagamyābhigāminaḥ pāpāḥ|

67.25  ṛjuvalayaḥ sukhabhājaḥ paradāradveṣiṇaścaiv |

67.26  māṃsalamṛdubhiḥ pārśvaiḥ pradakṣiṇāvartaromabhirbhūpāḥ|

67.26  viparītairnirdravyāḥ sukhaparihīnāḥ parapreṣyāḥ||

67.27  subhagā bhavantyanudvaddha[K.anubaddha]cūcukā nirdhanā viṣamadīrghaiḥ|

67.27  pīnopacitanimagnaiḥ kṣitipatayaścūcukaiḥ sukhinaḥ||

67.28  hṛdayaṃ samunnataṃ pṛthu na vepanaṃ māṃsalaṃ ca nṛpatīnām|

67.28  adhanānāṃ viparītaṃ khararomacitaṃ śirālaṃ c |

67.29  samavakṣaso +arthavantaḥ pīnaiḥ *śūrā hy[K.śūrāstv] akiñcanāstanubhiḥ|

67.29  viṣamaṃ vakṣo yeṣāṃ te niḥsvāḥ śastranidhanāśc |

67.30  viṣamairviṣamo jatrubhirarthavihīno +asthisandhipariṇaddhaiḥ|

67.30  unnatajatrurbhogī[K.bhāgī] nimnairniḥsvo +arthavān pīnaiḥ||

69 kanyālakṣaṇādhyāyaḥ



69.01  snigdhonnatāgratanutāmranakhau kumāryāḥ

69.01 pādau samopacitacārunigūḍhagulphau|

69.01 śliṣṭāṅgulī kamalakāntitalau ca yasyās

69.02 avasvedanau mṛdutalau caraṇau praśastau|

69.03 aśvatthapatrasadṛśaṃ vipulaṃ ca guhyam|

69.03 śroṇīlalāṭamurukūrmasamunnataṃ ca

69.06  bandhujīvakusumopamo +adharo

69.06 māṃsalo rucirabimbarūpabhṛt |

69.11  nigūḍhamaṇibandhanau taruṇapadmagarbhopamau

69.13 pradeśinīmadhyamikāntarālam|

69.18  hrasvayātiniḥsvatā dīrghayā kulakṣayaḥ|

70 vastracchedalakṣaṇādhyāyaḥ



70.01 b[K.71.6 b]| prabhūtavastradāśvinī bharaṇyathāpahāriṇī|

70.01 d[K.71.6 d]| pradahyate +agnidaivate prajesvare +arthasiddhayaḥ||

70.02 b[K.71.7 b]| mṛge tu mūṣakādbhayaṃ vyasutvameva śāṅkar

70.02 d[K.71.7 d]| punarvasau śubhāgamastadagrabhe dhanairyutiḥ||

70.03 b[K.71.8 b]| bhujaṅgabhe vilupyate maghāsu mṛtyumādiśet |

70.03 d[K.71.8 d]| bhagāhvaye nṛpādbhayaṃ dhanāgamāya cottarā||

70.04 b[K.71.9 b]| kareṇa karmasiddhayaḥ śubhāgamastu citrayā|

70.04 d[K.71.9 d]| śubhaṃ ca bhojyamānile dvidaivate janapriyaḥ||

70.05 b[K.71.10 b]| suhūdyutiś[K.ū.suhṛdyutiś] ca mitrabhe tadagrabhe[K.purandare] +ambarakṣayaḥ|

70.05 d[K.71.10 d]| jalaplutiśca nairṛte rujo jalādhidaivat |

70.06 b[K.71.11 b]| miṣṭamannamapi vaiśvadaivate vaiṣṇave bhavati netrarogatā|

70.06 d[K.71.11 d]| dhānyalabdhir[K.labdhim] api[K.atha] vāsave[K.viśvadaivate] vidurvāruṇe viṣakṛtaṃ mahadbhayam||

70.07 [K.71.12 ] bhadrapadāsu bhayaṃ salilotthaṃ

70.07 [K.71.12 ] tatparataśca bhavetsutalabdhiḥ|

70.07 [K.71.12 ] ratnayutiṃ kathayanti ca pauṣṇe

70.07 [K.71.12 ] yo +abhinavāmbaramicchati bhoktum||

[K.71.13  vipramatādatha bhūpatidattaṃ yacca vivāhavidhāvabhilabdham|

K.71.13  teṣu guṇai rahiteṣvapi bhoktuṃ nūtanamambaramiṣṭaphalaṃ syāt ||]

70.08 b[K.71.14 b]| bhoktuṃ navāmbaraṃ śastamṛkṣe +api guṇavarjit

70.08 d[K.71.14 d]| vivāhe rājasammāne brahmaṇāṇāṃ ca sammat |
70.09 b[K.71.1 b]| vastrasya koṇeṣu vasanti devā narāśca pāśāntadaśāntamadhy

70.09 d[K.71.1 d]| śeṣāstrayaścātra niśācarāṃśāstathaiva śayyāsanapādukāsu||

70.10 b[K.71.2 b]| lipte maṣīgomayakardamādyaiśchinne pradagdhe sphuṭite ca vindyāt |

70.10 d[K.71.2 d]| puṣṭaṃ nave +alpālpataraṃ ca bhukte pāpaṃ śubhaṃ cādhikamuttarīy |

70.11 b[K.71.3 b]| rugrākṣasāṃśesvatha vā +api mṛtyuḥ puñjanmatejaśca manuṣyabhāg

70.11 d[K.71.3 d]| bhāge +amarāṇāmatha bhogavṛddhiḥ prānteṣu sarvatra vadantyaniṣṭam||

70.12 b[K.71.4 b]| kaṅkaplavolūkakapotakākakravyādagomāyukharoṣṭrasarpaiḥ|

70.12 d[K.71.4 d]| chedākṛtirdaivatabhāgagāpi puṃsāṃ bhayaṃ mṛtyusamaṃ karoti||

70.13 b[K.71.5 b]| chatradhvajasvastikavardhamānaśrīvṛkṣakumbhāmbujatoraṇādyaiḥ|

70.13 d[K.71.5 d]| chedākṛtirnairṛtabhāgagāpi puṃsāṃ vidhatte na cireṇa lakṣmīm||



71 cāmaralakṣaṇādhyāyaḥ



71.01  devaiścamaryaḥ kila vālahetoḥ

71.01 sṛṣṭā himakṣmādharakandareṣu|

71.01 āpītavarṇāśca bhavanti tāsāṃ

71.01 kṛṣṇāśca lāṅgūlabhavāḥ sitāśc |

71.02 vaiśadyamalpāsthinibandhanatvam|

71.02 śauklyaṃ ca tāsāṃ[K.teṣāṃ] guṇasampaduktā

71.02 viddhālpaluptāni na śobhanāni||

71.03  adhyardhahastapramito +asya daṇḍo

71.03 hasto +atha vāratnisamo +atha vānyaḥ|

71.03 kāṣṭhātśubhātkāñcanarūpyaguptād

71.03 ratnaiś*ca sarvaiś[K.vicitraiś] ca hitāya rājñām||

71.04  yaṣṭyātapatrāṅkuśavetracāpavitānakuntadhvajacāmarāṇām|

71.05 rogamṛtyujananāśca parvabhiḥ|

71.06  yātrāprasiddhirdviṣatāṃ vināśo

71.06 lābhāḥ prabhūtā[K.prabhūto] vasudhāgamaśc

71.06 vṛddhiḥ paśūnāmabhivañchitāptis

71.06 tryādyeṣvayugmeṣu tadīśvarāṇām||



72 chatralakṣaṇādhyāyaḥ



72.01  nicitaṃ tu[K'str. nu] haṃsapakṣaiḥ kṛkavākumayūrasārasānāṃ vā|

72.01  daukūlyena[K.daukūlena] navena tu samantataśchāditaṃ śuklam||

72.02  muktāphalairupacitaṃ pralambamālāvilaṃ sphaṭikamūlam|

72.02  ṣaḍḍhastaśuddhahaimaṃ navaparvanagaikadaṇḍaṃ tu||

72.03  daṇḍārdhavistṛtaṃ tatsamāvṛtaṃ ratnabhūṣitam[K.ratnavibhūṣitam, K'str. ratnabhūṣitam] udagram|

72.03  nṛpatestadātapatraṃ kalyāṇaparaṃ vijayadaṃ c |

72.04  yuvarājanṛpatipatnyoḥ senāpatidaṇḍanāyakānāṃ c

72.04  daṇḍo +ardhapañcahastaḥ samapañca*kṛto +ardha[K.kṛtārdha]vistāraḥ||

72.05  anyeṣāmuṣṇaghnaṃ prasādapaṭṭairvibhūṣitaśiraskam|

72.05  vyālambiratnamālaṃ chatraṃ kāryaṃ tu[K.ca] māyūram||

72.06  anyeṣāṃ tu narāṇāṃ śītātapavāraṇaṃ tu caturasram[K.caturaśram]|

72.06  samavṛttadaṇḍayuktaṃ chatraṃ kāryaṃ tu viprāṇām||



73 strīpraśamsādhyāyaḥ



73.01  jaye dharitryāḥ purameva sāraṃ

73.01 pure gṛhaṃ sadmani caikadeśaḥ|

73.01 tatrāpi śayyā śayane varā strī

73.01 ratnojjvalā rājyasukhasya sāraḥ||

73.02  ratnāni vibhūṣayanti yoṣā bhūṣyante vanitā na ratnakāntyā|

73.02  ceto vinatā harantyaratnā no ratnāni vināṅganāṅgasaṅgam[K.saṅgāt]||

73.03  ākāraṃ vinigūhatāṃ ripubalaṃ jetuṃ samuttiṣṭhatāṃ

73.03 tantraṃ cintayatāṃ kṛtākṛtaśatavyāpāraśākhākulam|

73.03 mantriproktaniṣeviṇāṃ[K.niseviṇām, K'str. niṣevinām] kṣitibhujāmāśaṅkināṃ sarvato

73.03 dhuḥkhāmbhonidhivartināṃ sukhalavaḥ kāntāsamāliṅganam||

73.04  śrutaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ spṛṣṭaṃ smṛtamapi nṛṇāṃ hlādajananaṃ

73.04 na ratnaṃ strībhyo +anyatkva cidapi kṛtaṃ lokapatinā|

73.04 tadarthaṃ dharmārthau sutaviṣayasaukhyāni ca tato

73.04 gṛhe lakṣmyo mānyāḥ satatamabalā mānavibhavaiḥ||

73.05  ye +apyaṅganānāṃ pravadanti doṣān

73.05 vairāgyamārgeṇa guṇān vihāy

73.05 te durjanā me manaso vitarkaḥ

73.05 sadbhāvavākyāni na tāni teṣām||

73.06  prabrūta satyaṃ karato +aṅganānāṃ

73.06 doṣo +asti yo nācarito manuṣyaiḥ|

73.06 dhārṣṭyena pumbhiḥ pramadā nirastā

73.06 guṇādhikāstā manunātra coktam||

73.07  somastāsāmadātśaucaṃ gandharvaḥ[K.gandharvāḥ] śikṣitāṃ giram|

73.07  agniśca sarvabhakṣitvaṃ tasmānniṣkasamāḥ striyaḥ||

73.08  brāhmaṇāḥ pādato medhyā gāvo medhyāśca pṛṣṭhataḥ|

73.08  ajāśvā mukhato medhyāḥ striyo medhyāstu sarvataḥ||

73.09  striyaḥ pavitramatulaṃ naitā duṣyanti karhi cit |

73.09  māsi māsi rajo hyāsāṃ duṣkṛtānyapakarṣati||

73.10  jāmayo yāni gehāni śapantyapratipūjitāḥ|

73.10  tāni kṛtyāhatānīva vinaśyanti samantataḥ||

73.11  jāyā vā syājjanitrī vā sambhavaḥ strīkṛto nṛṇām|

73.11  he kṛtaghnāśtayornindāṃ kurvatāṃ vaḥ kutaḥ śubham||

73.12  dampatyorvyutkrame doṣaḥ samaḥ śāstre pratiṣṭhitaḥ|

73.12  narā na samavekṣante tenātra varamaṅganāḥ||

73.13  bahirlomnā tu ṣaṇmāsān veṣṭitaḥ kharacarmaṇā|

73.13  dārātikramaṇe bhikṣāṃ dehītyuktvā viśudhyati||

73.14  na śatenāpi varṣāṇāmapaiti madanāśayaḥ|

73.14  tatra aśaktyā nirvartante narā dhairyeṇa yoṣitaḥ||

73.15  aho dhārṣṭyamasādhūnāṃ nindatāmanaghāḥ striyaḥ|

73.15  muṣṇatāmiva caurāṇāṃ tiṣṭha caureti jalpatām||

73.16  puruṣaścaṭulāni[K.cātulāni, K'str. caṭulāni] kāminīnāṃ

73.16 kurute yāni raho na tāni paścāt |

73.16 sukṛtajñatayāṅganā gatāsūn

73.16 avagūhya praviśanti saptajihvam||

73.17  strīratnabhogo +asti narasya yasya

73.17 niḥsvo +api *sāmpratyavanīsvaro[K.svaṃ pratyavanīsvaro, K'str. māṃ pratyava] +asau|

73.17 rājyasya sāro +aśanamaṅganāśca

73.17 tṛṣṇānaloddīpanadāru śeṣam||

73.18  kāminīṃ prathamayauvanānvitāṃ

73.18 mandavalgumṛdupīḍitasvanām|

73.18 utstanīṃ samavalambya yā ratiḥ

73.18 sā na dhātṛbhavane +asti me matiḥ||

73.19  tatra devamunisiddhacāraṇair

73.19 mānyamānapitṛsevyasevanāt |

73.19 brūta dhātṛbhavane +asti kiṃ sukhaṃ

73.19 yadrahaḥ samavalambya na striyam||

73.20  ābrahmakīṭāntamidaṃ nibaddhaṃ

73.20 puṃstrīprayogeṇa jagatsamastam|

73.20 vrīḍātra kā yatra caturmukhatvam

73.20 īśo +api lobhādgamito yuvatyāḥ||



74 saubhāgyakaraṇādhyāyaḥ



74.01  jātyaṃ manobhavasukhaṃ subhagasya sarvam

74.01 ābhāsamātramitarasya manoviyogāt |

74.01 cittena bhāvayati dūragatāpi yaṃ strī

74.01 garbhaṃ bibharti sadṛśaṃ puruṣasya tasy |

74.02  bhaṅktvā kāṇḍaṃ pādapasyoptamurvyāṃ

74.02 bījaṃ vāsyāṃ nānyatāmeti yadvat |

74.02 evaṃ hyātmā jāyate strīṣu bhūyaḥ

74.02 kaścittasmin kṣetrayogādviśeṣaḥ||

74.03  ātmā sahaiti manasā mana indriyeṇa

74.03 svārthena cendriyamiti krama eṣa śīghraḥ|

74.03 yogo +ayameva manasaḥ kimagamyamasti

74.03 yasminmano vrajati tatra gato +ayamātmā||

74.04  ātmāyamātmani gato hṛdaye +atisūkṣmo

74.04 grāhyo +acalena manasā satatābhiyogāt |

74.04 yo yaṃ vicintayati yāti sa tanmayatvaṃ

74.04 yasmādataḥ subhagameva gatā yuvatyaḥ||

74.05  dākṣiṇyamekaṃ subhagatvaheturvidveṣaṇaṃ tadviparītaceṣṭā|

74.05  mantrauṣadhādyaiḥ kuhakaprayogairbhavanti doṣā bahavo na śarm |

74.06  vāllabhyamāyāti vihāya mānaṃ daurbhāgyamāpādayate +abhimānaḥ|

74.06  kṛcchreṇa saṃsādhayate +abhimānī kāryāṇyayatnena vadan priyāṇi||

74.07  tejo na tadyatpriyasāhasatvaṃ vākyaṃ na cāniṣṭamasatpraṇītam|

74.07  kāryasya gatva?antamanuddhatā ye tejasvinaste na vikatthanā y |

74.08  yaḥ sārvajanyaṃ subhagatvamicchedguṇān sa sarvasya vadetparokṣam[K.parokṣe]|

74.08  prāpnoti doṣānasato +apyanekān parasya yo doṣakathāṃ karoti||

74.09  sarvopakārānugatasya lokaḥ sarvopakārānugato narasy

74.09  kṛtvopakāraṃ dviṣatāṃ vipatsu yā kīrtiralpena na sā śubhen |

74.10  tṛṇairivāgniḥ sutarāṃ vivṛddhimāchādyamāno +api guṇo +abhyupaiti|

74.10  sa kevalaṃ durjanabhāvameti hantuṃ guṇān vāñchati yaḥ parasy |



75 kāndarpikādhyāyaḥ



75.01  rakte +adhike strī puruṣastu śukre napuṃsakaṃ śoṇitaśukrasāmy

75.01  yasmādataḥ śukravivṛddhidāni niṣevitavyāni rasāyanāni||

75.02  harmyapṛṣṭhamuḍunātharaśmayaḥ sotpalaṃ madhu madālasā priyā|

75.02  vallakī smarakathā rahaḥ srajo varga eṣa madanasya vāgurā||

75.03  mākṣīkadhātumadhupāradalohacūrṇa

75.03 pathyāśilājatu*ghṛtāni samāni[K.viḍaṅgaghṛtāni] yo +adyāt |

75.03 saikāni viṃśatirahāni jarānvito +api

75.03 so +aśītiko +api ramayatyabalāṃ yuvev |

75.04  kṣīraṃ śṛtaṃ yaḥ kapikacchumūlaiḥ

75.04 pibetkṣayaṃ strīṣu na so +abhyupaiti|

75.04 māṣān payaḥsarpiṣi vā vipakvān

75.04 ṣaḍgrāsamātrāṃśca payo +anupānam[K.anupānān]||

75.05  vidārikāyāḥ svarasena cūrṇaṃ muhurmuhurbhāvitaśoṣitaṃ c

75.05  śṛtena dugdhena saśarkareṇa pibetsa yasya pramadāḥ prabhūtāḥ||

75.06  dhātrīphalānāṃ svarasena cūrṇaṃ

75.06 subhāvitaṃ kṣaudrasitājyayuktam|

75.06 līḍhvānu pītvā ca payo +agniśaktyā

75.06 kāmaṃ nikāmaṃ puruṣo niṣevet ||

75.07  kṣīreṇa bastāṇḍa yujā śṛtena

75.07 samplāvya kāmī bahuśastilān yaḥ|

75.07 suśoṣitānatti *payaḥ pibec[K.pibetpayaś] ca

75.07 tasyāgrataḥ kiṃ caṭakaḥ karoti||

75.08  māṣasūpasahitena sarpiṣā ṣaṣṭikaudanamadanti ye narāḥ|

75.08  kṣīramapyanu pibanti tāsu te śarvarīṣu madanena[K.madane na] śerat |

75.09  tilāśvagandhākapikacchumūlair

75.09 vidārikāṣaṣṭikapiṣṭayogaḥ|

75.09 ājena piṣṭaḥ payasā ghṛtena

75.09 pakvaṃ[K.paktvā] bhavetśaṣkulikātivṛṣyā||

75.10  kṣīreṇa vā gokṣurakopayogaṃ

75.10 vidārikākandakabhakṣaṇaṃ vā|

75.10 kurvanna sīdedyadi jīryate +asya

75.10 mandāgnitā cedidamatra cūrṇam||

75.11  sājamodalavaṇā harītakī śṛṅgaverasahitā ca pippalī|

75.11  madyatakrataraloṣṇavāribhiścūrṇapānamudarāgnidīpanam||

75.12  atyamlatiktalavaṇāni kaṭūni vā+atti

75.12 *yaḥ kṣāraśākabahulāni[K.kṣāraśākabahulāni] ca bhojanāni|

75.12 dṛkśukravīryarahitaḥ sa karotyanekān
75.12 vyājān jaranniva yuvā +apyabalāmavāpy |



76 gandhayuktyadhyāyaḥ



76.01  sraggandhadhūpāmbarabhūṣaṇādyaṃ na śobhate śuklaśiroruhasy

76.01  yasmādato mūrdhajarāgasevāṃ kuryādyathaivāñjanabhūṣaṇānām||

76.02  lauhe pātre taṇḍulān kodravāṇāṃ

76.02 śukle pakvāṃllohacūrṇena sākam|

76.02 piṣṭān sūkṣmaṃ mūrdhni śuklāmlakeśe

76.02 datvā[K.dattvā] tiṣṭhedveṣṭayitvārdra[K.arka]patraiḥ||

76.03  yāte dvitīye prahare vihāya

76.03 dadyātśirasyāmalakapralepam|

76.03 saṃchādya patraiḥ praharadvayena

76.03 prakṣālitaṃ kārṣṇyamupaiti śīrṣam||

76.04  paścātśiraḥsnānasugandhatailair

76.04 lohāmlagandhaṃ śiraso +apanīy

76.04 hṛdyaiśca gandhairvividhaiśca dhūpair

76.04 antaḥpure rājyasukhaṃ niṣevet ||

76.05  tvakkuṣṭhareṇunalikāspṛkkārasatagarabālakaistulyaiḥ|

76.05  kesarapatravimiśrairnarapatiyogyaṃ śiraḥsnānam||

76.06  mañjiṣṭhayā vyāghranakhena śuktyā tvacā sakuṣṭhena rasena cūrṇaḥ|

76.06  tailena yukto +arkamayūkhataptaḥ karoti taccampakagandhi tailam||

76.07  tulyaiḥ patraturuṣkabālatagarairgandhaḥ smaroddīpanaḥ

76.07 savyāmo bakulo +ayameva kaṭukāhiṅgupradhūpānvitaḥ|

76.07 kuṣṭhenotpalagandhikaḥ samalayaḥ pūrvo bhaveccampako

76.07 jātītvaksahito +atimuktaka iti jñeyaḥ sakustumburuḥ||

76.08  śatapuṣpākundurukau pādenārdhena nakhaturuṣkau c

76.08  malayapriyaṅgu*bhāgau[K'str. bhāgo] gandho dhūpyo guḍanakhen |

76.09  guggulubālaka[K.vālaka]lākṣāmustānakhaśarkarāḥ kramāddhūpaḥ|

76.09  anyo māṃsībālaka[K.vālaka]turuṣkanakhacandanaiḥ piṇḍaḥ||

76.10  harītakīśaṅkhaghanadravāmbubhir

76.10 guḍotpalaiḥ śailakamustakānvitaiḥ|

76.10 navāntapādādivivardhitaiḥ kramād

76.10 bhavanti dhūpā bahavo manoharāḥ||

76.11  bhāgaiścaturbhiḥ sitaśaila[ū.śaileya]mustāḥ

76.11 śrīsarjabhāgau nakhaguggulū c

76.11 karpūrabodho madhupiṇḍito +ayaṃ

76.11 kopacchado nāma narendradhūpaḥ||

76.12  tvaguśīrapatrabhāgaiḥ sūkṣmailārdhena saṃyutaiścūrṇaḥ|

76.12  puṭavāsaḥ[K.paṭavāsaḥ] pravaro +ayaṃ mṛgakarpūraprabodhen |

76.13  ghanabālakaśaileyakakarpūrośīranāgapuṣpāṇi|

76.13  vyāghranakhaspṛkkāguru*damanaka[ū.madanaka]nakhatagaradhānyāni||

76.14  karpūra[k'str. karcūra]cola[K.cora]malayaiḥ svecchāparivartitaiścaturbhirataḥ|

76.14  ekadvitricaturbhirbhāgairgandhārṇavo bhavati||

76.15  atyulbaṇagandhatvādekāṃśo nityameva dhānyānām|

76.15  karpūrasya tadūno naitau dvitryādibhirdeyau||

76.16  śrīsarjaguḍanakhaiste dhūpayitavyāḥ kramānna piṇḍasthaiḥ|

76.16  bodhaḥ kastūrikayā deyaḥ karpūrasaṃyutayā||

76.17  atra sahasracatuṣṭayamanyāni ca saptatisahasrāṇi|

76.17  lakṣaṃ śatāni sapta viṃśatiyuktāni gandhānām||

76.18  ekaikamekabhāgaṃ dvitricaturbhāgikairyutaṃ dravyaiḥ|

76.18  ṣaḍgandhakaraṃ tadvaddvitricaturbhāgikaṃ kurut |

76.19  dravyacatuṣṭayayogādgandhacaturviṃśatiryathaikasy

76.19  evaṃ śeṣāṇāmapi ṣaṇṇavatiḥ sarvapiṇḍo +atr |

76.20  ṣoḍaśake dravyagaṇe caturvikalpena bhidyamānānām|

76.20  aṣṭādaśa jāyante śatāni sahitāni viṃśatyā||

76.21  ṣaṇṇavatibhedabhinnaścaturvikalpo gaṇo yatastasmāt |

76.21  ṣaṇṇanavatiguṇaḥ kāryaḥ sā saṅkhyā bhavati gandhānām||

76.22  pūrveṇa pūrveṇa gatena yuktaṃ

76.22 sthānaṃ vināntyaṃ pravadanti saṅkhyām|

76.22 icchāvikalpaiḥ kramaśo +abhinīya

76.22 nīte nivṛttiḥ punaranyanītiḥ||

76.23  dvitrīndriyāṣṭabhāgairaguruḥ patraṃ turuṣkaśaileyau

76.23  viṣayāṣṭapakṣadahanāḥ priyaṅgumustārasāḥ keśaḥ||

76.24  spṛkkātvaktagarāṇāṃ māṃsyāśca kṛtaikasaptaṣaḍbhāgāḥ|

76.24  saptartuvedacandrairmalayanakhaśrīkakundurukāḥ||

76.25  ṣoḍaśake kacchapuṭe yathā tathā *miśrite caturdravye[K.miśritaiścaturdravyaiḥ]|

76.25  ye +atrāṣṭādaśa bhāgāste +asmin gandhādayo yogāḥ||

76.26  nakhatagaraturuṣkayutā jātīkarpūramṛgakṛtobdodhāḥ[ū.udbodhāḥ]|

76.26  guḍanakhadhūpyā gandhāḥ kartavyāḥ sarvatobhadrāḥ||

76.27  jātīphalamṛgakarpūrabodhitaiḥ sasahakāramadhusiktaiḥ|

76.27  bahavo +atra pārijātāścaturbhiricchāparigṛhītaiḥ||

76.28  sarjarasaśrīvāsakasamanvitā ye +atra sarvadhūpās[K.sarvayogās] taiḥ|

76.28  śrīsarjarasaviyuktaiḥ snānāni sabālaka[K.savālaka]tvagbhiḥ||

76.29  rodhrośīranatāguru*mustāpatra[K.mustā]priyaṅguvanapathyāḥ|

76.29  navakoṣṭhātkacchapuṭāddravyatritayaṃ samuddhṛty |

76.30  candanaturuṣkabhāgau śuktyardhaṃ pādikā tu śatapuṣpā|

76.30  kaṭuhiṅgulaguḍadhūpyāḥ kesaragandhāścaturaśītiḥ||

76.31  saptāhaṃ gomūtre harītakīcūrṇasaṃyute kṣiptvā|

76.31  gandhodake ca bhūyo vinikṣipeddantakāṣṭhāni||

76.32  elātvakpatrāñjanamadhumaricairnāgapuṣpakuṣṭhaiśc

76.32  gandhāmbhaḥ kartavyaṃ kiñcitkālaṃ sthitānyasmin||

76.33  jātīphalapatrailākarpūraiḥ kṛtayamaikaśikhibhāgaiḥ|

76.33  avacūrnitāni bhānormarīcibhiḥ śoṣaṇīyāni||

76.34  varṇaprasādaṃ vadanasya kāntiṃ

76.34 vaiśadyamāsyasya sugandhitāṃ c

76.34 saṃsevituḥ śrotrasukhāṃ ca vācaṃ

76.34 kurvanti kāṣṭhānyasakṛdbhavānām||

76.35  kāmaṃ pradīpayati rūpamabhivyanakti

76.35 saubhāgyamāvahati vaktrasugandhitāṃ c

76.35 ūrjaṃ karoti kaphajāṃśca nihanti rogāṃs

76.35 tāmbūlamevamaparāṃśca guṇān karoti||

76.36  yuktena cūrṇena karoti rāgaṃ

76.36 rāgakṣayaṃ pūgaphalātiriktam|

76.36 cūrṇādhikaṃ vaktravigandhakāri

76.36 patrādhikaṃ sādhu karoti gandham||

76.37  patrādhikaṃ niśi hitaṃ saphalaṃ divā ca

76.37 proktānyathākaraṇamasya viḍambanaiv

76.37 kakkolapūgalavalīphalapārijātair

76.37 āmoditaṃ madamudā muditaṃ karoti||



77 strīpuṃsasamāyogādhyāyaḥ



77.01  śastrena veṇīvinigūhitena vidūrathaṃ svā mahiṣī jaghān

77.01  viṣapradigdhena ca nūpureṇa devī viraktā kila kāśirājam||

77.02  evaṃ viraktā janayanti doṣān

77.02 prāṇacchido +anyairanukīrtitaiḥ kim|

77.02 raktāviraktāḥ puruṣairato +arthāt

77.02 parīkṣitavyāḥ pramadāḥ prayatnāt ||

77.03  snehaṃ manobhavakṛtaṃ kathayanti bhāvā

77.03 nābhībhujastanavibhūṣaṇadarśanāni|

77.03 vastrābhisaṃyamanakeśavimokṣaṇāni

77.03 bhrūkṣepakampitakaṭākṣanirīkṣaṇāni||

77.04  uccaiḥ ṣṭhīvanamutkaṭaprahasitaṃ śayyāsanotsarpaṇaṃ

77.04 gātrāsphoṭanajṛmbhaṇāni sulabhadravyālpasamprārthanā|

77.04 bālāliṅganacumbanānyabhimukhe sakhyāḥ samālokanaṃ

77.04 dṛkpātaśca parāṅmukhe guṇakathā karṇasya kaṇḍūyanam||

77.05  imāṃ ca vindyādanuraktaceṣṭāṃ

77.05 priyāṇi vakti svadhanaṃ dadāti|

77.05 vilokya saṃhṛṣyati vītaroṣā

77.05 pramārṣṭi doṣān guṇakīrtanen |

77.06  tanmitrapūjā tadaridviṣatvaṃ

77.06 kṛtasmṛtiḥ proṣitadaurmanasyam|

77.06 stanauṣṭhadānānyupagūhanaṃ ca

77.06 svedo +atha cumbāprathamābhiyogaḥ||

77.07  viraktaceṣṭā bhrukuṭī[K.bhṛkuṭī]mukhatvaṃ

77.07 parāṅmukhatvaṃ kṛtavismṛtiśc

77.07 asambhramo duṣparitoṣatā ca

77.07 taddviṣṭamaitrī paruṣaṃ ca vākyam||

77.08  spṛṣṭvātha vālokya dhunoti gātraṃ

77.08 karoti garvaṃ na ruṇaddhi yāntam|

77.08 cumbāvirāme vadanaṃ pramārṣṭi

77.08 paścātsamuttiṣṭhati pūrvasuptā||

77.09  bhikṣuṇikā pravrajitā dāsī dhātrī kumārikā rajikā|

77.09  mālākārī duṣṭāṅganā sakhī nāpitī dūtyaḥ||

77.10  kulajanavināśaheturdūtyo yasmādataḥ prayatnen

77.10  tābhyaḥ striyo +abhirakṣyā vaṃśayaśomānavṛddhyartham||

77.11  rātrīvihārajāgararogavyapadeśaparagṛhekṣaṇikāḥ|

77.11  vyasanotsavāśca saṅketahetavasteṣu rakṣyāśc |

77.12  ādau necchati nojjhati smarakathāṃ vrīḍāvimiśrālasā

77.12 madhye hrīparivarjitābhyuparame lajjāvinamrānanā|

77.12 bhāvairnaikavidhaiḥ karotyabhinayaṃ bhūyaśca yā sādarā

77.12 buddhvā pumprakṛtiṃ ca yānucarati glānetaraiśceṣṭitaiḥ||

77.13  strīṇāṃ guṇā yauvanarūpaveṣa

77.13 dākṣiṇyavijñānavilāsapūrvāḥ|

77.13 strī ratnasaṃjñā ca guṇānvitāsu

77.13 strīvyādhayo +anyāścaturasya puṃsaḥ||

77.14  na grāmyavarṇairmaladigdhakāyā

77.14 nindyāṅgasaṃbandhikathāṃ ca kuryāt |

77.14 na cānyakāryasmaraṇaṃ rahaḥsthā

77.14 mano hi mūlaṃ haradagdhamūrteḥ||

77.15  śvāsaṃ manuṣyeṇa samaṃ tyajantī bāhūpadhānastanadānadakṣā|

77.15  sugandhakeśā susamīparāgā supte +anusuptā prathamaṃ vibuddhā||

77.16  duṣṭasvabhāvāḥ parivarjanīyā vimardakāleṣu ca na kṣamā yāḥ|

77.16  yāsāmasṛgvāsitanīlapītamātāmravarṇaṃ ca na tāḥ praśastāḥ||

77.17  yā svapnaśīlā bahuraktapittā pravāhinī vātakaphātiraktā[K.atiriktā]|

77.17  mahāśanā svedayutāṅgaduṣṭhā yā hrasvakeśī palitānvitā vā[K.ca]||

77.18  māṃsāni yasyāśca calanti nāryā

77.18 mahodarā khikkhiminī ca yā syāt ||

77.18 strīlakṣaṇe yāḥ kathitāśca pāpās

77.18 tābhirna kuryātsaha kāmadharmam||

77.19  śaśaśoṇitasaṅkāśaṃ lākṣārasasannikāśamathavā yat |

77.19  prakṣālitaṃ virajyati yaccāsṛktadbhavetśuddham||

77.20  yacchabdavedanāvarjitaṃ tryahātsannivartate raktam|

77.20  tatpuruṣasamprayogādavicāraṃ garbhatāṃ yāti||

77.21  na dinatrayaṃ niṣevyaṃ[K.niṣevet] snānaṃ mālyānulepanaṃ *strībhiḥ[K.ca strī]|

77.21  snāyāccaturthadivase śāstroktenopadeśen |

77.22  puṣyasnānauṣadhayo yāḥ kathitāstābhirambumiśrābhiḥ|

77.22  snāyāttathātra mantraḥ sa eva yastatra nirdiṣṭaḥ||

77.23  yugmāsu kila manuṣyā niśāsu nāryo bhavanti viṣamāsu|

77.23  dīrghāyuṣaḥ surūpāḥ sukhinaśca vikṛṣṭayugmāsu||

77.24  dakṣiṇapārśve puruṣo vāme nārī yamāvubhayasaṃsthau||

77.24  yadudaramadhyopagataṃ napuṃsakaṃ tanniboddhavyam||

77.25  kendratrikoṇeṣu śubhasthiteṣu

77.25 lagne śaśāṅke ca śubhaiḥ samet

77.25 pāpaistrilābhārigataiśca yāyāt

77.25 puṃjanmayogeṣu ca samprayogam||

77.26  na nakhadaśanavikṣatāni kuryād

77.26 ṛtusamaye puruṣaḥ striyāḥ kathañcit |

77.26 ṛturapi daśa ṣaṭca vāsarāṇi

77.26 prathamaniśātritayaṃ na tatra gamyam||



78  śayyāsanalakṣaṇādhyāyaḥ


78.01  sarvasya sarvakālaṃ yasmādupayogameti śāstramidam|

78.01  rājñāṃ viśeṣato +ataḥ śayanāsanalakṣaṇaṃ vakṣy |

78.02  asanaspandanacandanaharidrasuradārutindukīśālāḥ|

78.02  kāśmaryañjanapadmakaśākā vā śiṃśapā ca śubhāḥ||

78.03  aśanijalānilahastiprapātitā madhuvihaṅgakṛtanilayāḥ|

78.03  caityaśmaśānapathijordhvaśuṣkavallīnibaddhāśc |

78.04  kaṇṭakino *ye ca[K.vā ye] syurmahānadīsaṅgamodbhavā ye c

78.04  surabhavanajāśca na śubhā ye cāparayāmyadikpatitāḥ||

78.05  pratiṣiddhavṛkṣanirmitaśayanāsanasevanātkulavināśaḥ|

78.05  vyādhibhayavyayakalahā bhavantyanarthā[K.anarthāśca] anekavidhāḥ||

78.06  pūrvacchinnaṃ yadi vā dāru bhavettatparīkṣyamārambh

78.06  yadyārohettasmin kumārakaḥ putrapaśudaṃ tat ||

78.07  sitakusumamattavāraṇadadhyakṣatapūrṇakumbharatnāni|

78.07  maṅgalyānyanyāni ca dṛṣṭvārambhe śubhaṃ jñeyam||

78.08  karmāṅgulaṃ yavāṣṭakamudarāsaktaṃ tuṣaiḥ parityaktam|

78.08  aṅgulaśataṃ nṛpāṇāṃ mahatī śayyā jayāya kṛtā||

78.09  navatiḥ saiva ṣaḍūnā dvādaśahīnā triṣaṭkahīnā c

78.09  nṛpaputramantribalapatipurodhasāṃ syuryathāsaṅkhyam||

78.10  ardhamato +aṣṭāṃśonaṃ viṣkambho viśvakarmaṇā proktaḥ|

78.10  āyāmatryaṃśasamaḥ pādocchrāyaḥ sakukṣya[K.sakukṣi]śirāḥ||

78.11  yaḥ sarvaḥ śrīparṇyā paryaṅko nirmitaḥ sa dhanadātā|

78.11  asanakṛto rogaharastindukasāreṇa vittakaraḥ||

78.12  yaḥ kevalaśiṃśapayā vinirmito bahuvidhaṃ sa vṛddhikaraḥ|

78.12  candanamayo ripughno dharmayaśodīrghajīvitakṛt ||

78.13  yaḥ padmakaparyaṅkaḥ sa dīrghamāyuḥ śriyaṃ śrutaṃ vittam|

78.13  kurute śālena kṛtaḥ kalyāṇaṃ śākaracitaśc |

78.14  kevalacandanaracitaṃ kāñcanaguptaṃ vicitraratnayutam|

78.14  adhyāsan paryaṅkaṃ vibudhairapi pūjyate nṛpatiḥ||

78.15  anyena samāyuktā na tindukī śiṃśapā ca śubhaphaladā|

78.15  na *śrīparṇena[K.śrīparṇī na] ca devadāruvṛkṣo na cāpyasanaḥ||

78.16  śubhadau tu śālaśākau[K.śākaśālau] parasparaṃ saṃyutau pṛthakcaiv

78.16  tadvatpṛthakpraśastau sahitau ca haridrakakadambau||

78.17  sarvaḥ spandanaracito na śubhaḥ prāṇān hinasti cāmbakṛtaḥ|

78.17  asano +anyadārusahitaḥ kṣipraṃ doṣān karoti bahūn||

78.18  ambaspandanacandanavṛkṣāṇāṃ spandanātśubhāḥ pādāḥ|

78.18  phalataruṇā śayanāsanamiṣṭaphalaṃ bhavati sarveṇ |

78.19  gajadantaḥ sarveṣāṃ proktatarūṇāṃ praśasyate yog

78.19  kāryo +alaṅkāravidhirgajadantena praśasten |

78.20  dantasya mūlaparidhiṃ dvirāyataṃ prohya kalpayetśeṣam|

78.20  adhikamanūpacarāṇāṃ nyūnaṃ giricāriṇāṃ kiñcit ||

78.21  *śrīvṛkṣa[K.śrīvatsa]vardhamānacchatradhvajacāmarānurūpeṣu|

78.21  chede dṛṣṭeṣvārogya[K.arogya]vijayadhanavṛddhisaukhyāni||

78.22  praharaṇasadṛśeṣu jayo nandyāvarte pranaṣṭadeśāptiḥ|

78.22  loṣṭhe[K.ū.loṣṭe] tu labdhapūrvasya bhavati deśasya samprāptiḥ||

78.23  strīrūpe dhananāśo[K.svavināśo] bhṛṅgāre +abhyutthite sutotpattiḥ|

78.23  kumbhena nidhiprāptiryātrāvighnaṃ ca daṇḍen |

78.24  kṛkalāsakapibhujaṅgeṣvasubhikṣavyādhayo ripu*vaśitvam[K.vaśatvam]|

78.24  gṛdhrolūkadhvāṅkṣaśyenākāreṣu janamarakaḥ||

78.25  pāśe +atha vā kabandhe nṛpamṛtyurjanavipatsrute rakt

78.25  kṛṣṇe śyāve rūkṣe durgandhe cāśubhaṃ bhavati||

78.26  śuklaḥ samaḥ sugandhiḥ snigdhaśca śubhāvaho bhavecchedaḥ|

78.26  aśubhaśubhacchedā ye śayaneṣvapi te tathā phaladāḥ||

78.27  īṣāyoge dāru pradakṣiṇāgraṃ praśastamācāryaiḥ|

78.27  apasavyaikadigagre bhavati bhayaṃ bhūtasañjanitam||

78.28  ekenāvākśirasā[K.ekenāvākcchirasā] bhavati hi pādena pādavaikalyam|

78.28  dvābhyāṃ na jīryate +annaṃ tricaturbhiḥ kleśavadhabandhāḥ||

78.29  suṣire +atha vā vivarṇe granthau pādasya śīrṣage vyādhiḥ|

78.29  pāde kumbho yaśca granthau tasminnudararogaḥ||

78.30  kumbhādhastājjaṅghā tatra kṛto jaṅghayoḥ karoti bhayam|

78.30  tasyāścādharo +adhaḥ kṣayakṛddravyasya tatra kṛtaḥ||

78.31  khuradeśe yo granthiḥ khuriṇāṃ pīḍākaraḥ sa nirdiṣṭaḥ|

78.31  īṣāśīrṣaṇyośca tribhāgasaṃstho bhavenna śubhaḥ||

78.32  niṣkuṭamatha kolākṣaṃ sūkaranayanaṃ ca vatsanābhaṃ c

78.32  kālakamanyaddhundhukamiti kathitaśchidrasaṃkṣepaḥ||

78.33  ghaṭavatsuṣiraṃ madhye saṅkaṭamāsye ca niṣkuṭaṃ chidram|

78.33  niṣpāvamāṣamātraṃ nīlaṃ chidraṃ ca kolākṣam||

78.34  sūkaranayanaṃ viṣamaṃ vivarṇamadhyardhaparvadīrghaṃ c

78.34  vāmāvartaṃ bhinnaṃ parvamitaṃ vatsanābhākhyam||

78.35  kālakasaṃjñaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ dhundhukamiti yadbhavedvinirbhinnam|

78.35  dārusavarṇaṃ chidraṃ na tathā pāpaṃ samuddiṣṭam||

78.36  niṣkuṭasaṇjñe dravyakṣayastu kolekṣaṇe kuladhvaṃsaḥ|

78.36  śastrabhayaṃ sūkarake rogabhayaṃ vatsanābhākhy |

78.37  kālakadhundhukasaṃjñaṃ kīṭairviddhaṃ ca na śubhadaṃ chidram|

78.37  sarvaṃ granthipracuraṃ sarvatra na śobhanaṃ dāru||

78.38  ekadrumeṇa dhanyaṃ vṛkṣadvayanirmitaṃ ca dhanyataram|

78.38  tribhirātmajavṛddhikaraṃ caturbhirarthaṃ[K.artho] yaśaścāgryam||

78.39  pañcavanaspatiracite pañcatvaṃ yāti tatra yaḥ śet

78.39  ṣaṭsaptāṣṭatarūṇāṃ kāṣṭhairghaṭite kulavināśaḥ||



79 ratnaparīkṣādhyāyaḥ



79.01  ratnena śubhena śubhaṃ bhavati nṛpāṇāmaniṣṭamaśubhen

79.01  yasmādataḥ parīkṣyaṃ daivaṃ ratnāśritaṃ tajjñaiḥ||

79.02  dvipahayavanitādīnāṃ svaguṇaviśeṣeṇa ratnaśabdo +asti|

79.02  iha tūpalaratnānāmadhikāro vajrapūrvāṇām||

79.03  ratnāni balāddaityāddadhīcito +anye vadanti jātāni|

79.03  ke cidbhuvaḥ svabhāvādvaicitryaṃ prāhurupalānām||

79.04  vajrendranīlamarakatakarketarapadmarāgarudhirākhyāḥ|

79.04  vaidūrya[K.vaiḍūrya]pulakavimalakarājamaṇisphatikaśaśikāntāḥ||

79.05  saugandhikagomedakaśaṅkhamahānīlapuṣparāgākhyāḥ|

79.05  brahmamaṇijyotīrasasasyakamuktāpravālāni||

79.06  veṇātaṭe viśuddhaṃ śirīṣakusuma*prabhaṃ[K.upamaṃ] ca kauśalakam|

79.06  saurāṣṭrakamātāmraṃ kṛṣṇaṃ saurpārakaṃ vajram||

79.07  īṣattāmraṃ himavati mataṅgajaṃ vallapuṣpasaṅkāśam|

79.07  āpītaṃ ca kaliṅge śyāmaṃ pauṇḍreṣu sambhūtam||

79.08  aindraṃ ṣaḍaśri śuklaṃ yāmyaṃ sarpāsyarūpamasitaṃ c

79.08  kadalīkāṇḍanikāśaṃ vaiṣṇavamiti sarvasaṃsthānam||

79.09  vāruṇamabalāguhyopamaṃ bhavetkarṇikārapuṣpanibham|

79.09  śṛṅgāṭakasaṃsthānaṃ vyāghrākṣinibhaṃ ca hautabhujam||

79.10  vāyavyaṃ ca yavopamamaśokakusumaprabhaṃ samuddiṣṭam|

79.10  srotaḥ khaniḥ prakīrṇakamityākarasambhavastrividhaḥ||

79.11  raktaṃ pītaṃ ca śubhaṃ rājanyānāṃ sitaṃ dvijātīnām|

79.11  śairīṣaṃ vaiśyānāṃ śūdrāṇāṃ śasyate +asinibham||

79.12  sitasarṣapāṣṭakaṃ taṇḍulo bhavettaṇḍulaistu viṃśatyā|

79.12  tulitasya dve lakṣe mūlyaṃ dvidvyūnite caitat ||

79.13  pādatryaṃśārdhonaṃ tribhāgapañcāṃśaṣoḍaśāṃśāśc

79.13  bhāgaśca pañcaviṃśaḥ śatikassāhasrikaśceti||

79.14  sarvadravyābhedyaṃ laghvambhasi tarati raśmivatsnighdam|

79.14  taḍidanalaśakracāpopamaṃ ca vajraṃ hitāyoktam||

79.15  kākapadamakṣikākeśadhātuyuktāni śarkarair[K.śarkarā] viddham|

79.15  dviguṇāśri dagdha[K.digdha]kaluṣatrastaviśīrṇāni na śubhāni||

79.16  yāni ca budbudadalitāgracipiṭavāsīphalapradīrghāṇi|

79.16  sarveṣāṃ caiteṣāṃ mūlyādbhāgo +aṣṭamo hāniḥ||

79.17  vajraṃ na kiñcidapi dhārayitavyameke

79.17 putrārthinībhirabalābhiruśanti tajjñāḥ|

79.17 śṛṅgāṭakatripuṭadhānyakavatsthitaṃ yac

79.17 śroṇīnibhaṃ ca śubhadaṃ tanayārthinīnām||

79.18  svajanavibhavajīvitakṣayaṃ janayati vajramaniṣṭalakṣaṇam|

79.18  aśaniviṣabhayārināśanaṃ śubhamupabhogakaraṃ[K.urubhogakara] ca bhūbhṛtām||



80 muktālakṣaṇādhyāyaḥ



80.01  dvipabhujagaśuktiśaṅkhābhraveṇutimisūkaraprasūtāni|

80.01  muktāphalāni teṣāṃ bahusādhu ca śuktijaṃ bhavati||

80.02  siṃhalakapāralaukikasaurāṣṭrikatāmraparṇipāraśavāḥ|

80.02  kauberapāṇḍyavāṭakahaimā ityākarāstv[K.hy] aṣṭau||

80.03  bahusaṃsthānāḥ snigdhāḥ[K.snigdhā] haṃsābhāḥ siṃhalākarāḥ sthūlāḥ|

80.03  īṣattāmrāḥ śvetāstamoviyuktāśca tāmrākhyāḥ||

80.04  kṛṣṇāḥ śvetāḥ pītāḥ saśarkarāḥ pāralaukikā viṣamāḥ|

80.04  na sthūlā nātyalpā navanītanibhāśca saurāṣṭrāḥ||

80.05  jyotiṣmatyaḥ[K.mantaḥ] śubhrā guravo +atimahāguṇāśca pāraśavāḥ|

80.05  laghu jarjaraṃ dadhinibhaṃ *bṛhaddvisaṃsthānam[K.bṛhadvisaṃsthānam] api haimam||

80.06  viṣamaṃ kṛṣṇaśvetaṃ[K.kṛṣṇaṃ śvetam] laghu kauberaṃ pramāṇatejovat |

80.06  nimbaphalatripuṭadhānyakacūrṇāḥ syuḥ pāṇḍyavāṭabhavāḥ||

80.07  atasīkusumaśyāmaṃ vaiṣṇavamaindraṃ śaśāṅkasaṅkāśam|

80.07  haritālanibhaṃ vāruṇamasitaṃ yamadaivataṃ bhavati||

80.08  pariṇatadāḍimagulikāguñjātāmraṃ ca vāyudaivatyam|

80.08  nirdhūmānalakamalaprabhaṃ ca vijñeyamāgneyam||

80.09  māṣakacatuṣṭayadhṛtasyaikasya śatāhatā tripañcāśat |

80.09  kārṣāpaṇā nigaditā mūlyaṃ tejoguṇayutasy |

80.10  māṣakadalahānyāto dvātriṃśadviṃśatistrayodaśa c

80.10  aṣṭau ca śatāni śatatrayaṃ tripañcāśatā sahitam||

80.11  pañcatriṃśaṃ śatamiti catvāraḥ kṛṣṇalā navatimūlyāḥ|

80.11  sārdhāstisro guñjāḥ saptatimūlyaṃ dhṛtaṃ rūpam||

80.12  guñjātrayasya mūlyaṃ pañcāśadrūpakā guṇayutasy

80.12  rūpakapañcatriṃśattrayasya guñjārdhahīnasy |

80.13  paladaśabhāgo dharaṇaṃ tadyadi muktāstrayodaśa surūpāḥ|

80.13  *triśatī[ū.triṃśatī] sapañcaviṃśā rūpakasaṅkhyā kṛtaṃ mūlyam||

80.14  ṣoḍaśakasya dviśatī viṃśatirūpasya saptatiḥ saśatā|

80.14  yatpañcaviṃśatidhṛtaṃ tasya śataṃ triṃśatā sahitam||

80.15  triṃśatsaptatimūlyaṃ catvāriṃśacchatārdhamūlyaṃ[K.mūlyā] c

80.15  ṣaṣṭiḥ pañconā vā dharaṇaṃ pañcāṣṭakaṃ mūlyam||

80.16  muktāśītyā triṃśacchatasya sā pañcarūpakavihīnā|

80.16  dvitricatuḥpañcaśatā dvādaśaṣaṭpañcakatritayam||

80.17  pikkāpiccārghārdhā ravakaḥ sikthaṃ trayodaśādyānām|

80.17  saṃjṇāḥ parato nigarāścūrṇāścāśītipūrvāṇām||

80.18  etadguṇayuktānāṃ dharaṇadhṛtānāṃ prakīrtitaṃ[K.prakītitam] mūlyam|

80.18  parikalpyamantarāle hīnaguṇāṇāṃ kṣayaḥ kāryaḥ||

80.19  kṛṣṇaśvetakapītakatāmrāṇāmīṣadapi ca viṣamāṇām|

80.19  tryaṃśonaṃ viṣamakapītayośca ṣaḍbhāgadalahīnam||

80.20  airāvatakulajānāṃ puṣyaśravaṇendusūryadivaseṣu|

80.20  ye cottarāyaṇabhavā grahaṇe +arkendvośca bhadrebhāḥ||

80.21  teṣāṃ kila jāyante muktāḥ kumbheṣu saradakośeṣu|

80.21  bahavo bṛhatpramāṇā bahusaṃsthānāḥ prabhāyuktāḥ||

80.22  naiṣāmarghaḥ kāryo na ca vedho +atīva te prabhāyuktāḥ|

80.22  sutavijayārogyakarā mahāpavitrā dhṛtā rājñām||

80.23  daṃṣṭrāmūle śaśikāntisaprabhaṃ bahuguṇaṃ ca vārāham|

80.23  timijaṃ matsyāākṣībhaṃ bṛhatpavitraṃ bahuguṇaṃ c |

80.24  varṣopalavajjātaṃ vāyuskandhācca saptamādbhraṣṭam|

80.24  hriyate kila khāddivyaistaḍitprabhaṃ meghasambhūtam||

80.25  takṣakavāsukikulajāḥ kāmagamā ye ca pannagāsteṣām|

80.25  snigdhā nīladyutayo bhavanti muktāḥ phaṇasyānt |

80.26  śaste +avanipradeśe rajatamaye bhājane sthite ca yadi|

80.26  varṣati devo +akasmāttajjñeyaṃ nāgasambhūtam||

80.27  apaharati viṣamalakṣmīṃ kṣapayati śatrūn yaśo vikāśayati|

80.27  bhaujaṅgaṃ nṛpatīnāṃ dhṛtamakṛtārghaṃ vijayadaṃ c |

80.28  karpūrasphaṭikanibhaṃ cipiṭaṃ viṣamaṃ ca veṇujaṃ jñeyam|

80.28  śaṅkhodbhavaṃ śaśinibhaṃ vṛttaṃ bhrājiṣṇu ruciraṃ c |

80.29  śaṅkhatimiveṇuvāraṇavarāhabhujagābhrajāny*avaidyāni[K.avaidhyāni vavedyāni]|

80.29  amitaguṇatvāccaiṣāmarghaḥ śāstre na nirdiṣṭaḥ||

80.30  etāni sarvāṇi mahāguṇāni

80.30 sutārthasaubhāgyayaśaskarāṇi|

80.30 rukśokahantṝṇi ca pārthivānāṃ

80.30 muktāphalānīpsitakāmadāni||
80.31  surabhūṣaṇaṃ latānāṃ sahasramaṣṭottaraṃ caturhastam|

80.31  inducchando[K.indracchando] nāmnā vijayacchandastadardhen |

80.32  śatamaṣṭayutaṃ hāro devacchando hyaśītirekayutā|

80.32  aṣṭāṣṭako +ardhahāro raśmikalāpaśca navaṣaṭkaḥ||

80.33  dvātriṃśatā tu guccho viṃśatyā kīrtito +ardhagucchākhyaḥ|

80.33  ṣoḍaśabhirmāṇavako dvādaśabhiścārdhamāṇavakaḥ||

80.34  mandarasaṃjño +aṣṭābhiḥ pañcalatā hāraphalakamityuktam|

80.34  saptāviṃśatimuktā hasto nakṣatramāleti||

80.35  antaramaṇisaṃyuktā maṇisopānaṃ suvarṇagulikairvā|

80.35  taralakamaṇimadhyaṃ tadvijñeyaṃ cāṭukāramiti||

80.36  ekāvalī nāma yatheṣṭasaṅkhyā

80.36 hastapramāṇā maṇiviprayuktā|

80.36 saṃyojitā yā maṇinā tu madhye

80.36 yaṣṭīti sā bhūṣaṇavidbhiruktam||



81 padmarāgalakṣaṇādyāyaḥ



81.01  saugandhikakuruvindasphaṭikebhyaḥ padmarāgasambhūtiḥ[K'str. padmarāgo sambhūtiḥ]|

81.01  saugandhikajā bhramarāñjanābjajambūrasadyutayaḥ||

81.02  kuruvindabhavāḥ śabalā mandadyutayaśca dhātubhirviddhāḥ|

81.02  sphaṭikabhavā dyutimanto nānāvarṇā viśuddhāśc |

81.03  snigdhaḥ prabhānulepī svaccho +arciṣmān guruḥ susaṃsthānaḥ|

81.03  antaḥprabho +atirāgo[K.atirāgā] maṇiratnaguṇāḥ samastānām||

81.04  kaluṣā mandadyutayo lekhākīrṇāḥ sadhātavaḥ khaṇḍāḥ|

81.04  durviddhā na manojñāḥ saśarkarāśceti maṇidoṣāḥ||

81.05  bhramaraśikhikaṇṭhavarṇo dīpaśikhāsaprabho bhujaṅgānām|

81.05  bhavati maṇiḥ kika mūrdhani yo +anargheyaḥ sa vijñeyaḥ||

81.06  yastaṃ bibharti manujādhipatirna tasya

81.06 doṣā bhavanti viṣarogakṛtāḥ kadā cit |

81.06 rāṣṭre ca nityamabhivarṣati tasya devaḥ

81.06 śatrūṃśca nāśayati tasya maṇeḥ prabhāvāt ||

81.07  ṣaḍviṃśatiḥ sahasrāṇyekasya maṇeḥ palapramāṇasy

81.07  karṣatrayasya viṃśatirupadiṣṭā padmarāgasy |

81.08  ardhapalasya dvādaśa karṣasya ekasya ṣaṭsahasrāṇi|

81.08  yaccāṣṭamāṣakadhṛtaṃ tasya sahasratrayaṃ mūlyam||

81.09  māṣakacatuṣṭayaṃ daśaśatakrayaṃ dvau tu pañcaśatamūlyau|

81.09  parikalpyamantarāle mūlyaṃ hīnādhikaguṇānām||

81.10  varṇanyūnasyārdhaṃ tejohīnasya mūlyamaṣṭāṃśam|

81.10  alpaguṇo bahudoṣo mūlyātprāpnoti viṃśāṃśam||

81.11  ādhūmraṃ vraṇabahulaṃ svalpaguṇaṃ cāpnuyāddviśatabhāgam|

81.11  iti padmarāgamūlyaṃ pūrvācāryaiḥ samuddiṣṭam||



82 marakatalakṣaṇādhyāyaḥ|



82.01  śukavaṃśapatrakadalīśirīṣakusumaprabhaṃ guṇopetam|

82.01  surapitṛkārye marakatam[K.rakatam] atīva śubhadaṃ nṛṇāṃ vihitam[K.vidhṛtam]||



83 dīpalakṣaṇādhyāyaḥ



83.01  vāmāvarto malinakiraṇaḥ sasphuliṅgo +alpamūrtiḥ

83.01 kṣipraṃ nāśaṃ vrajati vimalasnehavartyanvito +api|

83.01 dīpaḥ pāpaṃ kathayati phalaṃ śabdavān vepanaśca

83.01 vyākīrṇārcirviśalabhamarudyaśca nāśaṃ prayāti||

83.02  dīpaḥ saṃhatamūrtirāyatatanurnirvepano dīptimān

83.02 niḥśabdo ruciraḥ pradakṣiṇagatirvaidūrya[K.vaiḍūrya]hemadyutiḥ|

83.02 lakṣmīṃ kṣipramabhivyanakti suciraṃ[K.ruciraṃ, K'str. suciram] yaścodyataṃ dīpyate

83.02 śeṣaṃ lakṣaṇamagnilakṣaṇasamaṃ yojyaṃ yathāyuktitaḥ||



84 dantakāṣṭhalakṣaṇādhyāyaḥ



84.01  vallīlatāgulmataruprabhedaiḥ

84.01 syurdantakāṣṭhāni sahasraśo yaiḥ|

84.01 phalāni vācyānyatha[K.ati] tatprasaṅgo

84.01 mā bhūdato vacmyatha kāmikāni||

84.02  ajñātapūrvāṇi na dantakāṣṭhāny

84.02 adyānna patraiśca samanvitāni|

84.02 na yugmaparvāṇi na pāṭitāni

84.02 na cordhvaśuṣkāṇi vinā tvacā c |

84.03  vaikaṅkataśrīphalakāśmarīṣu

84.03 brāhmī dyutiḥ kṣematarau sudārāḥ|

84.03 vṛddhirvaṭe +arke pracuraṃ ca tejaḥ

84.03 putrā madhūke saguṇāḥ[K.kakubhe] priyatvam||

84.04  lakṣmīḥ śirīṣe ca tathā karañje

84.04 plakṣe +arthasiddhiḥ samabhīpsitā syāt |

84.04 mānyatvamāyāti janasya jātyāṃ

84.04 prādhānyamaśvatthatarau vadanti||

84.05  ārogyamāyurbadarībṛhatyor

84.05 aiśvaryavṛddhiḥ khadire sabilv

84.05 dravyāṇi ceṣṭānyatimuktake syuḥ

84.05 prāpnoti tānyeva punaḥ kadamb |

84.06  nīpe[K.nimbe] +arthāptiḥ karavīre +annalabdhir

84.06 bhāṇḍīre syād*annamevaṃ[K.idameva] prabhūtam|

84.06 śamyāṃ śatrūnapahantyarjune ca

84.06 śyāmāyāṃ ca dviṣatāmeva nāśaḥ||

84.07  śāle +aśvakarṇe ca vadanti gauravaṃ

84.07 sabhadradārāvapi cāṭarūṣak

84.07 vāllabhyamāyāti janasya sarvataḥ

84.07 priyaṅgvapāmārgasajambudāḍimaiḥ||

84.08  udaṅmukhaḥ prāṅmukha eva vābdaṃ

84.08 kāmaṃ yatheṣṭhaṃ[ū.yatheṣṭaṃ] hṛdaye niveśy

84.08 adyādanindan[K.anindyaṃ] ca sukhopaviṣṭaḥ

84.08 prakṣālya jahyācca śucipradeś |

84.09  abhimukhapatitaṃ praśāntadiksthaṃ

84.09 śubhamatiśobhanamūrdhvasaṃsthitaṃ yat |

84.09 aśubhakaramato +anyathā pradiṣṭaṃ

84.09 sthitapatitaṃ ca karoti mṛṣṭamannam||



85 śākunādhyāyaḥ



85.01  yatśakraśukra[K.śukraśakra]vāgīśakapiṣṭhalagarutmatām|

85.01  matebhyaḥ prāha ṛṣabho bhāgurerdevalasya c |

85.02  bhāradvājamataṃ dṛṣṭvā yacca śrīdravyavardhanaḥ|

85.02  āvantikaḥ prāha nṛpo mahārājādhirājakaḥ||

85.03  saptarṣīṇāṃ mataṃ yacca saṃskṛtaṃ prākṛtaṃ ca yat |

85.03  yāni coktāni gargādyairyātrākāraiśca bhūribhiḥ||

85.04  tāni dṛṣṭvā cakāremaṃ sarvaśākunasaṃgraham|

85.04  varāhamihiraḥ prītyā śiṣyāṇāṃ jñānamuttamam||

85.05  anyajanmāntarakṛtaṃ karma puṃsāṃ śubhāśubham|

85.05  yattasya śakunaḥ pākaṃ nivedayati gacchatām||

85.06  grāmāraṇyāmbubhūvyomadyuniśobhayacāriṇaḥ|

85.06  rutayātekṣitokteṣu grāhyāḥ puṃstrīnapuṃsakāḥ[K.strīpunnapuṃsakāḥ]||

85.07  pṛthagjātyanavasthānādeṣāṃ vyaktirna lakṣyat

85.07  sāmānyalakṣaṇoddeśe ślokāvṛṣikṛtāvimau||

85.08  pīnonnatavikṛṣṭāṃsāḥ pṛthugrīvāḥ suvakṣasaḥ|

85.08  svalpagambhīravirutāḥ puṃāṃsaḥ sthiravikramāḥ||

85.09  tanūraskaśirogrīvāḥ sūkṣmāsyapadavikramāḥ|

85.09  prasaktamṛdubhāṣiṇyaḥ striyo +ato +anyannapuṃsakam||

85.10  grāmāraṇyapracārādyaṃ lokādevopalakṣayet |

85.10  saṃcikṣipsurahaṃ vacmi yātrāmātraprayojanam||

85.11  pathyātmānaṃ nṛpaṃ sainye pure coddiśya devatām|

85.11  sārthe pradhānaṃ sāmye[K.sāmyaṃ] syājjātividyāvayo +adhikam||

85.12  muktaprāptaiṣyadarkāsu phalaṃ dikṣu tathāvidham|

85.12  aṅgāra[K.aṅgāri]dīptadhūminyastāśca śāntāstato +aparāḥ[K.+aparā, K'str. aparāḥ]||

85.13  tatpañcamadiśāṃ tulyaṃ śubhaṃ traikālyamādiśet |

85.13  pariśeṣadiśor[K.pariśeṣayor] vācyaṃ yathāsannaṃ śubhāśubham||

85.14  śīghramāsannanimnasthaiścirādunnatadūragaiḥ|

85.14  sthānavṛddhyupaghātācca tadvadbrūyātphalaṃ punaḥ||

85.15  kṣaṇatithyuḍuvātārkairdevadīpto yathottaram|

85.15  kriyādīpto gatisthānabhāvasvaraviceṣṭitaiḥ||

85.16  daśadhaivaṃ praśānto +api saumyastṛṇaphalāśanaḥ|

85.16  māṃsāmedhyāśane raudro vimiśro +annāśanaḥ smṛtaḥ||

85.17  harmyaprāsādamaṅgalyamanojñasthānasaṃsthitāḥ|

85.17  śreṣṭhā madhurasakṣīraphalapuṣpadrumeṣu c |

85.18  svakāle giritoyasthā balino dyuniśācarāḥ|

85.18  klībastrī*puruṣā jñeyā[K.puruśāśca eṣāṃ] balinaḥ syuryathottaram||

85.19  javajātibalasthānaharṣasattvasvarānvitāḥ|

85.19  svabhūmāvanulomāśca tadūnāḥ syurvivarjitāḥ||

85.20  kukkuṭebhapirilyaśca śikhivañjulachikkarāḥ|

85.20  balinaḥ siṃhanādaśca kūṭapūrī ca pūrvataḥ||

85.21  kroṣṭukolūkahārītakākakokarkṣapiṅgalāḥ|

85.21  kapotaruditākrandakrūraśabdāśca yāmyataḥ||

85.22  gośaśakrauñcalomāśahaṃsotkrośakapiñjalāḥ|

85.22  viḍālotsavavāditragītahāsāśca vāruṇāḥ||

85.23  śatapatrakuraṅgākhumṛgaikaśaphakokilāḥ|

85.23  cāṣaśalyakapuṇyāhaghaṇṭāśaṅkharavā udak ||

85.24  na grāmyo +araṇyago grāhyo nāraṇyo grāmyasaṃsthitaḥ[K.grāmasaṃsthitaḥ]|

85.24  divācaro na śarvaryāṃ na ca naktaṃcaro divā||

85.25  dvandvarogārditatrastāḥ kalahāmiṣakāṅkṣiṇaḥ

85.25  āpagāntaritā mattā na grāhyāḥ śakunāḥ kva cit ||

85.26  rohitāśvāja*vāleyāḥ kuraṅga[K.vāleyakuraṅga]uṣṭramṛgāḥ śaśaḥ|

85.26  niṣphalāḥ śiśire jñeyā vasante kākakokilau||

85.27  na tu bhādrapade grāhyāḥ sūkaraśvavṛkādayaḥ|

85.27  śāradya[K.śaradya]abjādagokrauñcāḥ śrāvaṇe hasticātakau||

85.28  vyāghrarkṣavānaradvīpimahiṣāḥ sabileśayāḥ|

85.28  hemante niṣphalā jñeyā bālāḥ sarve vimānuṣāḥ||

85.29  aindrānaladiśormadhye tribhāgeṣu vyavasthitāḥ|

85.29  kośādhyakṣānalājīvitapoyuktāḥ pradakṣiṇam||

85.30  śilpī bhikṣurvivastrā strī yāmyānaladigantar

85.30  parataścāpi mātaṅgagopadharmasamāśrayāḥ||

85.31  nairṛtīvāruṇīmadhye pramadāsūtitaskarāḥ|

85.31  śauṇḍikaḥ śākunī hiṃsro vāyavyā[K.vāyavya]paścimāntar |

85.32  viṣaghātakagosvāmikuhakajñāstataḥ param|

85.32  dhanavānīkṣaṇīkaśca mālākāraḥ paraṃ tataḥ||

85.33  vaiṣṇavaścarakaścaiva vājināṃ rakṣaṇe rataḥ|

85.33  *dvātriṃśadevaṃ[K.evaṃ dvātriṃśato] bhedāḥ syuḥ pūrvadigbhiḥ sahoditāḥ||

85.34  rājā kumāro netā ca dūtaḥ śreṣṭhī caro dvijaḥ|

85.34  rājādhyakṣaś[K.ū.gajādhyakṣaś] ca pūrvādyāḥ kṣatriyādyāścaturdiśam||

85.35  gacchatastiṣṭhato vāpi diśi yasyāṃ vyavasthitaḥ|

85.35  virauti śakuno vācyastaddigjena samāgamaḥ||

85.36  bhinnabhairavadīnārtaparuṣakṣāmajarjarāḥ|

85.36  svanā[K.svarā] neṣṭāḥ śubhāḥ śānta[K.śāntā]hṛṣṭaprakṛtipūritāḥ||

85.37  śivā śyāmā ralā chucchuḥ piṅgalā gṛhagodhikā|

85.37  sūkarī parapuṣṭā ca punnāmānaśca vāmataḥ||

85.38  strīsaṃjñā bhāsabhaṣakakapiśrīkarṇa*dhikkarāḥ[K.chikkarāḥ]|

85.38  śikhiśrīkaṇṭhapippīkaruruśyenāśca dakṣiṇāḥ||

85.39  kṣveḍāsphoṭitapuṇyāhagītaśaṅkhāmbuniḥsvanāḥ|

85.39  satūryādhyayanāḥ puṃvatstrīvadanyā giraḥ śubhāḥ||

85.40  grāmau madhyamaṣaḍjau tu gāndhāraśceti śobhanāḥ|

85.40  ṣaḍja[K.ṣaḍjā]madhyamagāndhārā ṛṣabhaśca svarā hitāḥ||

85.41  rutakīrtanadṛṣṭeṣu bhāradvājājabarhiṇaḥ|

85.41  dhanyā nakulacāṣau ca saraṭaḥ pāpado +agrataḥ||

85.42  jāhakāhiśaśakroḍagodhānāṃ kīrtanaṃ śubham|

85.42  *rutaṃ sandarśanaṃ[K.ratasandarśanaṃ] neṣṭaṃ pratīpaṃ vānararkṣayoḥ||

85.43  ojāḥ pradakṣiṇaṃ śastā mṛgāḥ sanakulāṇḍajāḥ|
85.43  cāṣaḥ sanakulo vāmo bhṛgurāhāparāhṇataḥ||

85.44  chikkaraḥ kūṭapūrī ca pirilī cāhni dakṣiṇāḥ|

85.44  apasavyāḥ sadā śastā daṃṣṭriṇaḥ sabileśayāḥ||

85.45  śreṣṭhe hayasite prācyāṃ śavamāṃse ca dakṣiṇ

85.45  kanyakādadhinī paścādudaggoviprasādhavaḥ||

85.46  jālaśvacaraṇau neṣṭau prāgyāmyau śastraghātakau|

85.46  paścādāsavaṣaṇḍhau ca khalāsanahalānyudak ||

85.47  karmasaṅgamayuddheṣu praveśe naṣṭamārgaṇ

85.47  yānavyastagatā grāhyā viśeṣaścātra vakṣyat |

85.48  divā prasthānavadgrāhyāḥ kuraṅgaruruvānarāḥ|

85.48  ahnaśca prathame bhāge cāṣavañjulakukkuṭāḥ||

85.49  paścime śarvarībhāge naptṛkolūkapiṅgalāḥ|

85.49  sarva eva viparyastā grāhyāḥ sārtheṣu yoṣitām||

85.50  nṛpasandarśane grāhyaḥ praveśe +api prayāṇavat |

85.50  giryaraṇya*praveśeṣu[K.praveśe ca] nadīnāṃ cāvagāhan |

85.51  vāmadakṣiṇagau śastau yau tu tāvagrapṛṣṭhagau|

85.51  kriyādīptau vināśāya yātuḥ parighasaṃjñitau||

85.52  tāveva tu yathābhāgaṃ praśāntarutaceṣṭitau|

85.52  śakunau śakunadvārasaṃjñitāvarthasiddhay |

85.53  ke cittu śakunadvāramicchantyubhayataḥ sthitaiḥ|

85.53  śakunairekajātīyaiḥ śāntaceṣṭāvirāvibhiḥ||

85.54  visarjayati yadyeka ekaśca pratiṣedhati|

85.54  sa virodho +aśubho yāturgrāhyo yo[K.ū.vā] balavattaraḥ||

85.55  pūrvaṃ prāveśiko[K.prāveśeko] bhūtvā punaḥ prāsthāniko bhavet |

85.55  sukhena siddhimācaṣṭe praveśe tadviparyayāt[K.tadviparyayaḥ]||

85.56  visarjya śakunaḥ pūrvaṃ sa eva niruṇaddhi cet |

85.56  prāha yāturarermṛtyuṃ ḍamaraṃ rogameva vā||

85.57  apasavyāstu śakunā dīptā bhayanivedinaḥ|

85.57  ārambhe śakuno dīpto varṣāntastadbhayaṅkaraḥ||

85.58  tithivāyvarkabhasthānaceṣṭādīptā yathākramam|

85.58  dhanasainyabalāṅgeṣṭakarmaṇāṃ syurbhayaṅkarāḥ||

85.59  jīmūtadhvanidīpteṣu bhayaṃ bhavati mārutāt |

87.34  bhītā ruvanti kukukukviti tāmracūḍās

87.34 tyaktvā rutāni bhayadānyaparāṇi rātrau|

87.34 svasthaiḥ svabhāvavirutāni niśāvasāne

87.34 tārāṇi rāṣṭrapurapārthivavṛddhidāni||

87.35  nānāvidhāni virutāni hi chippikāyās

87.35 tasyāḥ śubhāḥ kulukulurna śubhāstu śeṣāḥ|

87.35 yāturbiḍālavirutaṃ na śubhaṃ sadaiva

87.35 gostu kṣutaṃ maraṇameva karoti yātuḥ||

87.36  huṃhuṃguglugiti priyāmabhilaṣan krośatyulūko mudā

87.36 pūrṇaḥ syādgurulu pradīptamapi ca jñeyaṃ sadā kiskisi|

87.36 vijñeyaḥ kalaho yadā balabalaṃ tasya[K.tasyāḥ] asakṛdvāśitaṃ

87.36 doṣāyaiva ṭaṭaṭṭaṭeti na śubhāḥ śeṣāstu dīpta[K.dīptāḥ]svarāḥ||

87.37  sārasakūjitamiṣṭaphalaṃ tadyadyugapadvirutaṃ mithunasy

87.37  ekarutaṃ na śubhaṃ yadi vā syādekarute pravirauti[K.pratirauti] cireṇ |

87.38  cirilvirilviti svaraiḥ śubhaṃ karoti piṅgalāḥ[K.piṅgalā]|

87.38  ato +apare tu ye svarāḥ pradīptasaṃjñitāstu t |

87.39  iśivirutaṃ gamanapratiṣedhi

87.39 kuśukuśu cetkalahaṃ prakaroti|

87.39 abhimatakāryagatiṃ ca yathā sā

87.39 kathayati taṃ ca vidhiṃ kathayāmi||

87.40  dināntasandhyāsamaye nivāsam

87.40 āgamya tasyāḥ prayataśca vṛkṣam|

87.40 devān samabhyarcya pitāmahādīn

87.40 navāmbarastaṃ ca taruṃ sugandhaiḥ||

87.41  eko niśīthe +analadiksthitaśca

87.41 divyetaraistāṃ śapathairniyojy

87.41 pṛcchedyathācintitamarthamevam

87.41 anena mantreṇa yathāśṛṇoti||

87.42  viddhi bhadre mayā yattvamimamarthaṃ pracoditā|

87.42  kalyāṇi sarvavacasāṃ veditrī tvaṃ prakīrtyas|

87.43  āpṛcche +adya gamiṣyāmi veditaśca punastvaham|

87.43  prātarāgamya pṛcche tvāmāgneyīṃ diśamāśritaḥ||

87.44  pracodayāmyahaṃ yattvāṃ tanme vyākhyātumarhasi|

87.44  svaceṣṭitena kalyāṇi yathā vedmi nirākulam||

87.45  ityevamukte tarumūrdhagāyāś

87.45 cirilvirilvīti rute +arthasiddhiḥ|

87.45 atyākulatvaṃ[ū.avyākulatvam] diśikāraśabde

87.45 kucākucetyevamudāhṛte vā||

87.46  avākpradāne *+api hita[K.vihita]arthasiddhiḥ

87.46 pūrvoktadikcakraphalairato +anyat |

87.46 vācyaṃ phalaṃ cottamamadhyanīca

87.46 śākhāsthitāyāṃ varamadhyanīcam||

87.47 chucchundarī cicciḍiti pradīptā

88 śvacakrādhyāyaḥ



88.01  nṛturagakarikumbhaparyāṇasakṣīravṛkṣeṣṭakāsañcayacchatraśayyāsanolūkhalāni dhvajaṃ cāmaraṃ śādvalaṃ puṣpitaṃ vā pradeśaṃ yadā śvāvamūtryāgrato yāti yātustadā kāryasiddhirbhavedārdrake gomaye miṣṭabhojyāgamaḥ śuṣkasammūtraṇe śuṣkamannaṃ guḍo modakāvāptirevātha vā

88.01 atha viṣatarukaṇṭhakīkāṣṭhapāṣāṇaśuṣkadrumāsthiśmaśānāni mūtryāvahatyātha vā yāyino +agresaro +aniṣṭamākhyāti śayyākulālādi bhāṇḍānyabhuktānyabhinnāni vā mūtrayan kanyakādoṣakṛdbhujyamānāni cedduṣṭatāṃ tadgṛhiṇyāstathā syādupānatphalaṃ gostu sammūtraṇe +avarṇajaḥ[K.varṇajaḥ] saṅkaraḥ|

88.01 gamanamukhamupānahaṃ sampragṛhyopatiṣṭhedyadā syādtadā siddhaye māṃsapūrṇānane +arthāptirādreṇa cāsthnā śubhaṃ sāgnyalātena śuṣkeṇa cāsthnā gṛhītena mṛtyuḥ praśāntolmukenābhighāto +atha puṃsaḥ śirohastapādādi vaktre bhuvo +abhyāgamo[K.hyāgamo] vastracīrādibhirvyāpadaḥ ke cidāhuḥ savastre śubham

88.01 praviśati tu gṛhaṃ saśuṣkāsthivaktre pradhānasya tasmin vadhaḥ śṛṅkhalāśīrṇavallīvaratrādi vā bandhanaṃ copagṛhyopatiṣṭhedyadā syāttadā bandhanaṃ leḍhi pādau vidhunvan svakarṇāvuparyākramaṃścāpi vighnāya yāturvirodhe virodhastathā svāṅgakaṇḍūyane syātsvapaṃścordhvapādaḥ sadā doṣakṛt ||

88.02  sūryodaye +arkābhimukho virauti

88.02 grāmasya madhye yadi sārameyaḥ|

88.02 eko yadā vā bahavaḥ sametāḥ

88.02 śaṃsanti deśādhipamanyamāśu||

88.03  sūryonmukhaḥ śvānaladiksthitaśca

88.03 caurānalatrāsakaro +acireṇ

88.03 madhyāhnakāle +analamṛtyuśaṃsī

88.03 saśoṇitaḥ syātkalaho +aparāhṇ |

88.04  ruvan dineśābhimukho +astakāle

88.04 kṛṣībalānāṃ bhayamāśu datte[K.dhatte]|

88.04 pradoṣakāle +aniladiṅmukhaśca[K.tu]

88.04 datte[K.dhatte] bhayaṃ mārutataskarottham||

88.05  udaṅmukhaścāpi niśārdhakāle

88.05 vipravyathāṃ goharaṇaṃ ca śāsti|

88.05 niśāvasāne śivadinmukhaśca

88.05 kanyābhidūṣānalagarbhapātān||

88.06  uccaiḥ svarāḥ syustṛṇakūṭasaṃsthāḥ

88.06 prāsādaveśmottamasaṃsthitā vā|

88.06 varṣāsu vṛṣṭiṃ kathayanti tīvrām

88.06 anyatra mṛtyuṃ dahanaṃ rujaśc |

88.07  prāvṛṭkāle +avagrahe +ambho +avagāhya

88.07 pratyāvartai[K.pratyāvṛttai] recakaiścāpyabhīkṣaṇam[K.abhīkṣṇam]|

88.07 ādhunvanto vā pibantaśca toyaṃ

88.07 vṛṣṭiṃ kurvantyantare dvādaśāhāt ||

88.08  dvāre śiro nyasya bahiḥ śarīraṃ

88.08 rorūyate śvā gṛhiṇīṃ viloky

88.08 rogapradaḥ syādatha mandirāntar

88.08 bahirmukho *vakti ca[K.śaṃsati] bandhakīṃ tām||

88.09  kuḍyamutkirati veśmano yadā tatra khānakabhayaṃ bhavettadā|

88.09  goṣṭhamutkirati gograhaṃ vadeddhānyalabdhimapi dhānyabhūmiṣu||

88.10  ekenākṣṇā sāśruṇā dīnadṛṣṭir

88.10 mandāhāro duḥkhakṛttadgṛhasy

88.10 kṣemārogyaṃ cābhidhatte mudaṃ c |

88.11  vāmaṃ jighrejjānu vittāgamāya

88.11 strībhiḥ sākaṃ vigraho dakṣiṇaṃ cet |

88.11 ūruṃ vāmaṃ cendriyārthopabhogaḥ[K.upabhogāḥ]

88.11 savyaṃ jighrediṣṭamitrairvirodhaḥ||
88.12  pādau jighredyāyinaścedayātrāṃ

88.12 prāhārthāptiṃ vāñchitāṃ niścalasy

88.12 sthānasthasyopānahau cedvijighret

88.12 kṣipraṃ yātrāṃ sārameyaḥ karoti||

88.13  ubhayorapi jighraṇe hi bāhvor

88.13 vijñeyo ripucaurasamprayogaḥ|

88.13 atha bhasmani gopayīta bhakṣān

88.13 māṃsāsthīni ca[K.vā ca] śīghramagnikopaḥ||

88.14  grāme bhaṣitvā ca bahiḥ śmaśāne

88.14 bhaṣanti ceduttamapuṃvināśaḥ|

88.14 yiyāsataścābhimukho virauti

88.14 yadā tadā śvā niruṇaddhi yātrām||

88.15  ukāra*varṇe virute[K.varṇena rute] +arthasiddhir

88.15 okāravarṇena ca vāmapārśv

88.15 vyākṣepamaukārarutena vindyān

88.15 niṣedhakṛtsarvarutaiśca paścāt ||

88.16  *khaṃkheti[K.saṅkheti] coccaiśca muhurmuhurye

88.16 ruvanti daṇḍairiva tāḍyamānāḥ|

88.16 śvāno +abhidhāvanti ca maṇḍalena

88.16 te śūnyatāṃ mṛtyubhayaṃ ca kuryuḥ||

88.17  prakāśya dantān yadi leḍhi sṛkviṇī

88.17 tadāśanaṃ mṛṣṭam[K.miṣṭam] uśanti tadvidaḥ|

88.17 yadānanaṃ *leḍhi punar[K.ca avalihen] na sṛkviṇī

88.17 pravṛttabhojye +api tadānnavighnakṛt ||

88.18  grāmasya madhye yadi vā purasya

88.18 bhaṣanti saṃhatya muhurmuhury

88.18 te kleśamākhyānti tadīśvarasya

88.18 śvāraṇyasaṃstho mṛgavadvicintyaḥ||

88.19  vṛkṣopage krośati toyapātaḥ

88.19 syādindrakīle sacivasya pīḍā|

88.19 vāyorgṛhe sasyabhayaṃ gṛhāntaḥ

88.19 pīḍā purasyaiva ca gopurasth |

88.20  bhayaṃ ca śayyāsu tadīśvarāṇāṃ

88.20 yāne bhaṣanto bhayadāśca paścāt |

88.20 athāpasavyā janasanniveśe

88.20 bhayaṃ bhaṣantaḥ kathayantyarīṇām||



89 śivārutādhyāyaḥ



89.01  śvabhiḥ śṛgālāḥ sadṛśāḥ phalena

89.01 viśeṣa eṣāṃ śiśire madāptiḥ|

89.01 hūhū rutānte parataśca ṭāṭā

89.01 pūrṇaḥ svaro +anye kathitāḥ pradīptāḥ||

89.02  lomāśikāyāḥ khalu kakkaśabdaḥ

89.02 pūrṇaḥ svabhāvaprabhavaḥ sa tasyāḥ|

89.02 ye +anye svarāste prakṛterapetāḥ

89.02 sarve ca dīptā iti sampradiṣṭāḥ||

89.03  pūrvodīcyoḥ śivā śastā śāntā sarvatra pūjitā|

89.03  dhūmitābhimukhī hanti svaradīptā digīśvarān||

89.4 b[K.omitted]| rājā kumāro netā ca dūtaḥ śreṣṭhī caro dvijaḥ|

89.4 d[K.omitted]| gajādhyakṣaśca pūrvādyāḥ kṣatriyādyāścaturdiśam||

89.05  sarvadikṣvaśubhā dīptā viśeṣeṇāhnyaśobhanā|

89.05  pure sainye +apasavyā ca kaṣṭā sūryonmukhī śivā||

89.06  yāhītyagnibhayaṃ śāsti ṭāṭeti mṛtavedikā|

89.06  dhigdhigduṣkṛtim[K.duṣkṛtam] ācaṣṭe sajvālā deśanāśinī||

89.07  naiva dāruṇatāmeke sajvālāyāḥ pracakṣat

89.07  arkādyanalavattasyā vaktraṃ lālāsvabhāvataḥ||

89.08  anyapratirutā yāmyā sodbandhamṛtaśaṃsinī|

89.08  vāruṇyanurutā saiva śaṃsate salile mṛtam||

89.09  akṣobhaḥ śravaṇaṃ ceṣṭaṃ dhanaprāptiḥ priyāgamaḥ|

89.09  kṣobhaḥ pradhānabhedaśca vāhanānāṃ ca sampadaḥ||

89.10  phalamā saptamādetadagrāhyaṃ parato rutam|

89.10  yāmyāyāṃ tadviparyastaṃ phalaṃ ṣaṭpañcamādṛt |

89.11  yā romāñcaṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ śakṛnmūtraṃ ca vājinām|

89.11  rāvāttrāsaṃ ca janayetsā śivā na śivapradā||

89.12  maunaṃ gatā pratirute naradvirada*vājibhiḥ[K.vājinām]|

89.12  yā śivā sā śivaṃ sainye pure vā samprayacchati||

89.13  bhebheti śivā bhayaṅkarī bhobho vyāpadamādiśecca sā|

89.13  mṛtibandhanivedinī phiphe[K.phipha] hūhū cātmahitā śivā svar |

89.14  śāntā tvavarṇāt*paramāruvantī[K.pavanau ruvantī]

89.14 ṭāṭāmudīrṇāmiti vāśyamānā|

89.14 ṭeṭe ca pūrvaṃ parataśca thethe

89.14 tasyāḥ svatuṣṭiprabhavaṃ rutaṃ tat ||

89.15  uccairghoraṃ varṇamuccārya pūrvaṃ

89.15 paścātkrośetkroṣṭukasyānurūpam|

89.15 yā sā kṣemaṃ prāha vittasya cāptiṃ

89.15 saṃyogaṃ vā proṣitena priyeṇ |



90 mṛgaceṣṭitādhyāyaḥ



90.01  sīmāgatā vanyamṛgā ruvantaḥ

90.01 sthitā vrajanto +atha samāpatantaḥ|

90.01 sampratyatītaiṣyabhayāni dīptāḥ

90.01 kurvanti śūnyaṃ parito bhramantaḥ||

90.02  te grāmyasattvairanuvāśyamānā

90.02 bhayāya rodhāya bhavanti vanyaiḥ|

90.02 dvābhyāmapi pratyanuvāśitāste

90.02 vandi*grahāyai ca[K.grahāyaiva] mṛgā ruvanti[K.bhavanti]||

90.03  vanye sattve dvārasaṃsthe purasya

90.03 rodho vācyaḥ sampraviṣṭe vināśaḥ|

90.03 sūte mṛtyuḥ syādbhayaṃ saṃsthite ca

90.03 gehaṃ yāte bandhanaṃ sampradiṣṭam||



91 gaveṅgitādhyāyaḥ



91.01  gāvo dīnāḥ pārthivasyāśivāya

91.01 pādairbhūmiṃ kuṭṭayantyaśca rogān|

91.01 mṛtyuṃ kurvantyaśrupūrṇāyatākṣyaḥ

91.01 patyurbhītāstaskarānāruvantyaḥ||

91.02  akāraṇe krośati cedanartho bhayāya rātrau vṛṣabhaḥ śivāy

91.02  bhṛśaṃ niruddhā yadi makṣikābhistadāśu vṛṣṭiṃ saramātmajairvā||

91.03  āgacchantyo veśma bambhāraveṇa

91.03 saṃsevantyo goṣṭhavṛddhyai gavāṃ gāḥ|

91.03 ārdrāṅgyo vā hṛṣṭaromṇyaḥ prahṛṣṭā

91.03 dhanyā gāvaḥ syurmahiṣyo +api caivam||



92 aśveṅgitādhyāyaḥ



92.01  utsargānna śubhadam *āsanātparasthaṃ[K.vāsanāparasthaṃ]

92.01 vāme ca jvalanamato +aparaṃ praśastam|

92.01 sarvāṅgajvalanamavṛddhidaṃ hayānāṃ

92.01 dve varṣe dahanakaṇāśca dhūpanaṃ vā||

92.02 pāyau ca pucche ca parājayaḥ syād

92.03 lalāṭavakṣo *+akṣibhuje ca[K.akṣibhujeṣu] dhūmaḥ

92.05 kampo vā vadanācca raktapatanaṃ dhūmasya vā sambhavaḥ|

92.05 asvapnaśca virodhināṃ[K.virodhitā] niśi divā nidrālasadhyānatā|

92.05 sādo +adhomukhatā viceṣṭitamidaṃ neṣṭaṃ smṛtaṃ vājinām||

92.06  ārohaṇamanyavājināṃ paryāṇādiyutasya vājinaḥ|

92.06  upavāhyaturaṅgamasya vā kalpasyaiva vipannaśobhanā||

92.07  krauñcavadripuvadhāya hreṣitaṃ[K.heṣitaṃ]

92.08  pūrṇapātradadhivipradevatā

92.08 dravyamiṣṭamatha vā paraṃ bhaved

92.11 nidrāratāśca pravadanti yātrām|

92.14  muhurmuhurmūtraśakṛtkaroti

92.14 na tāḍyamāno +apyanulomayāyī|

92.14 akāryabhīto +aśruvilocanaśca

92.14 śivaṃ[K.śubham] na bhartusturago +abhidhatt |

92.15  uktamidaṃ hayaceṣṭitamata ūrdhvaṃ dantināṃ pravakṣyāmi|

93 hasticeṣṭitādhyāyaḥ



93.01  dantasya mūlaparidhiṃ dvirāyataṃ prohya kalpayetśeṣam|

93.01  adhikamanūpacarāṇāṃ nyūnaṃ giricāriṇāṃ kiñcit ||

93.02  śrīvatsavardhamānacchatradhvajacāmarānurūpeṣu|

93.02  chede dṛṣṭeṣvārogyavijayadhanavṛddhisaukhyāni||

93.03  praharaṇasadṛśeṣu jayo nandyāvarte pranaṣṭadeśāptiḥ|

93.03  loṣṭe tu labdhapūrvasya bhavati deśasya samprāptiḥ||

93.04  strīrūpe +aśvavināśo[K.svavināśo] bhṛṅgāre +abhyutthite sutotpattiḥ|

93.04  kumbhena nidhiprāptiryātrāvighnaṃ ca daṇḍen |

93.05  kṛkalāsakapibhujaṅgeṣvasubhikṣavyādhayo ripu*vaśitvam[K.vaśatvam]|

93.05  gṛdhrolūkadhvāṅkṣaśyenākāreṣu janamarakaḥ||

93.06  pāśe +atha vā kabandhe nṛpamṛtyurjanavipatsrute rakt

93.06  kṛṣṇe śyāve rūkṣe durgandhe cāśubhaṃ bhavati||

93.07  śuklaḥ samaḥ sugandhiḥ snigdhaśca śubhāvaho bhavecchedaḥ|

93.07  galanamlānaphalāni ca dantasya samāni bhaṅgen |

93.08  mūlamadhyadaśanāgrasaṃsthitā

95.ṝ8  mātrādiyuksyādgrahayuktrikoṇe

95.ṝ8 dreṣkāṇaparyāyavadakṣareṣu|

95.ṝ8 nabhobaleṣūrdhvamadho +ambujeṣu

95.ṝ8 jñeyo *visargastu bala[ū.visargoastabala]anviteṣu||

95.ṝ9  śīrṣodayeṣūrdhvamuśanti mātrām

95.ṝ9 adhaśca pṛṣṭhodayaśabditeṣu|

95.ṝ9 tīryakca vindyādubhayodaye tāṃ

95.ṝ9 dīrgheṣu dīrghāmitareṣu cānyām||

95.ṝ10  prāglagnatoyāstanabhaḥsthiteṣu

95.ṝ10 bheṣvaṃśakebhyo +akṣarasaṃgrahaḥ syāt |

95.ṝ11  śubhagrahastūrjitavīryabhāgī

95.ṝ11 paśyan sthitaḥ kendratrikoṇayorvā

95.ṝ11 svocce +api varṇadvayamātmabhāg |

95.ṝ12  kṣetreśvare kṣīṇabale +aṃśake ca

95.ṝ12 mātrākṣaraṃ nāśamupaiti tajjam|

95.ṝ12 vargādyamuccāṃśayujīśadṛṣṭ |

95.ṝ13  kendre yathāsthānabalaprakarṣaṃ

95.ṝ13 kṣetrasya tatkṣetrapateśca buddhvā|

95.ṝ13 kāryo +akṣarāṇāmanupūrvayogo

95.ṝ14  tatrādirāśyādicaturvilagnam

95.ṝ14 ādyaṃśakādikramaparyāyeṇ

95.ṝ14 grahāṃśakebhyaḥ svagaṇākṣarāṇām

95.ṝ14 anvarthane prāptiriyaṃ vidhāryā||

95.ṝ15  meṣe kakāro hibuke yakāras

95.ṝ15 tule cakāro makare pakāraḥ|

95.ṝ15 meṣe chakāro hibuke +apyakāras

95.ṝ15 tule khakāro makare phakāraḥ||

95.ṝ16  meṣe ṭakāro hibuke ṭhakāras

95.ṝ16 tule takāro makare thakāraḥ|

95.ṝ16 meṣe tu rephā hibuke jakāras

95.ṝ16 tule bakāro makare gakāraḥ||

95.ṝ17  ākāramādye +ambugate ghakāram

95.ṝ17 aste bhakāraṃ makare jhakāram|

95.ṝ17 lagne ḍakāraṃ hibuke dakāram

95.ṝ17 aste dhakāraṃ makare ḍhakāram||

95.ṝ18  lagne ñakāro hibuke makāras

95.ṝ18 tule ṅakāro makare lakāraḥ|

95.ṝ18 lagne kakāro hibuke pakāras

95.ṝ18 tule cakāro makare ikāraḥ||

95.ṝ19  lagne nakāro hibuke takāras

95.ṝ19 tule ṇakāro makare ṭakāraḥ|

95.ṝ19 ityetaduktaṃ carasaṃjñakasya

95.ṝ19 vakṣye sthirākhyasya catuṣṭayasy |

95.ṝ20  vṛṣe phakāro hibuke khakāraḥ

95.ṝ20 kīṭe vakāro nṛghaṭe chakāraḥ|

95.ṝ20 ādyāṃśakebhyo matimān vidadhyād

95.ṝ20 anukrameṇa sthirasaṃjñakeṣu||

95.ṝ21  lagne bakāro hibuke jakāra

95.ṝ21 īkāramaste +ambarage gakāraḥ|

95.ṝ21 vṛṣe thakāro hibuke ṭakāraḥ

95.ṝ21 kīṭe ḍakāro nṛghaṭe dakāraḥ||

95.ṝ22 kīṭe jhakāro nṛghaṭe bhakāraḥ|

95.ṝ22 lagne jakāro hibuke ukāraḥ

95.ṝ22 kīṭe ṅakāro nṛghaṭe makāraḥ||

95.ṝ23  lagne ḍhakāro +atha jale ṇakāraś

95.ṝ23 cāste dhakāro +ambarage nakāraḥ|

95.ṝ23 vṛṣe ṣakāro hibuke cakāraḥ

95.ṝ23 kīṭe pakāro nṛghaṭe kakāraḥ||

95.ṝ24  ūkāramāhurvṛṣabhe jale kham

95.ṝ24 aste phakāro nṛghaṭe chakāraḥ|

95.ṝ24 antye vṛṣe ṭaṃ tamuśanti siṃhe

95.ṝ24 thaṃ saptage ṭhaṃ pravadanti kumbh |

95.ṝ25  dvimūrtisaṃjñe mithune jakārāḥ

95.ṝ25 ṣaṣṭhe bakāraḥ prathamāṃśake syāt |

95.ṝ25 dhanurdhare +astopagate gakāro

95.ṝ25 mīnadvaye cāmbarage sakāraḥ||

95.ṝ26  lagne ghakāro hibuke bhakāraś

95.ṝ26 cāste jhakāro +ambaramadhyage ī|

95.ṝ26 lagne dakāro hibuke dhakāram

95.ṝ26 aste ḍakāraṃ vidurambare ḍham||

95.ṝ27  lagne makāro hibuke ṅakāraś

95.ṝ27 aste hakāro +ambarage ñakāraḥ|

95.ṝ27 lagne pakāro jalage cakāra

95.ṝ27 aikāramaste +ambarage kakāraḥ||

95.ṝ28  prāglagne naṃ jalage ṇamāhur

95.ṝ28 astaṃ gate ṭaṃ nabhasi sthite tam|

95.ṝ28 prāglagnage khaṃ jalage yamāhur

95.ṝ28 astaṃ gate chaṃ nabhasi sthite pham||

95.ṝ29  lagne jamokāramathāmbusaṃsthe

95.ṝ29 gamastasaṃsthe vidurambare bam|

95.ṝ29 ṭhaṃ lagnage +antye hibukāśrite ḍaṃ

95.ṝ29 thamastage daṃ nabhasi sthite vai||

95.ṝ30  evaṃ vikalpo +akṣarasaṃgraho +ayaṃ

95.ṝ30 nāmnāṃ niruddiṣṭavidhāna uktaḥ|

95.ṝ30 sarveṣu lagneṣu ca ke cidevam

95.ṝ30 icchanti pūrvoktavidhānavattu||

95.ṝ31  kendrāṇi vā kendragatāṃśakaiḥ svaiḥ

95.ṝ31 pṛthakpṛthaksaṃguṇitāni kṛtvā|

95.ṝ31 trikṛdvibhaktaṃ vidurakṣaraṃ tat

95.ṝ31 kṣetreśvarasyāmśaparikramasvam||

95.ṝ32  saṃcintitaprārthitanirgateṣu

95.ṝ32 naṣṭakṣatastrīratibhojaneṣu|

95.ṝ32 svapnarkṣacintāpuruṣādivargeṣv

95.ṝ32 eteṣu nāmānyupalakṣayeta

95.14  dvyakṣaraṃ caragṛhāṃśakodaye

95.14 nāma cāsya caturakṣaraṃ sthir

95.14 nāmayugmamapi ca dvimūrtiṣu

95.14 tryakṣaraṃ bhavati cāsya pañcabhiḥ||

95.15  kādyāstu vargāḥ kujaśukrasaumya

95.15 jīvārkajānāṃ kramaśaḥ pradiṣṭāḥ|

95.15 varṇāṣṭakaṃ yādi ca śītaraśme

95.15 raverakārātkramaśaḥ svarāḥ syuḥ||

95.16  nāmāni cāgnyambukumāraviṣṇu

95.16 śakrendrapatnīcaturānanānām|

95.16 tulyāni sūryātkramaśo vicintya

95.16 dvitryādivarṇairghaṭayetsvabuddhyā||

95.17  vayāṃsi teṣāṃ stanapānabālya

95.17 vratasthitā yauvanamadhyavṛddhāḥ|

95.17 atīvavṛddhā iti candrabhauma

95.17 jñaśukrajīvārkaśanaiścarāṇām||



96 pākādhyāyaḥ



96.01  pakṣādbhānoḥ somasya māsiko +aṅgārakasya vaktroktaḥ|

96.01  ādarśanācca pāko budhasya jīvasya varṣeṇ |

96.02  ṣaḍbhiḥ sitasya māsairabdena śaneḥ suradviṣo +abdārdhāt |

96.02  varṣātsūryagrahaṇe sadyaḥ syāttvāṣṭrakīlakayoḥ||

96.03  tribhireva dhūmaketormāsaiḥ śvetasya saptarātrānt

96.03  saptāhātpariveṣendracāpasandhyābhrasūcīnām||

96.04  śītoṣṇaviparyāsaḥ phalapuṣpamakālajaṃ diśāṃ dāhaḥ|

96.04  sthiracarayoranyatvaṃ prasūtivikṛtiśca ṣaṇmāsāt ||

96.05  akriyamāṇakakaraṇaṃ bhūkampo +anutsavo duriṣṭaṃ c

96.05  śoṣaścāśoṣyāṇāṃ sroto +anyatvaṃ ca varṣārdhāt ||

96.06  stambhakusūlārcānāṃ jalpitaruditaprakampitasvedāḥ|

96.06  māsatrayeṇa kalahendracāpanirghātapākāśc |
96.07  kīṭākhumakṣikoragabāhulyaṃ mṛgavihaṅgavirutaṃ c

96.07  loṣṭasya cāpsu taraṇaṃ tribhireva vipacyate māsaiḥ||

96.08  prasavaḥ śunāmaraṇye vanyānāṃ grāmasampraveśaśc

96.08  madhunilayatoraṇendradhvajāśca varṣātsamadhikādvā||

96.09  gomāyugṛdhrasaṃghā daśāhikāḥ sadya eva tūryaravaḥ|

96.09  ākruṣṭaṃ pakṣaphalaṃ valmīko vidaraṇaṃ ca bhuvaḥ||

96.10  ahutāśaprajvalanaṃ ghṛtatailavasādivarṣaṇaṃ cāpi|

96.10  sadyaḥ paripacyante māse +adhyardhe ca janavādaḥ||

96.11  chatracitiyūpahutavahabījānāṃ saptabhirbhavati pakṣaiḥ|

96.11  chatrasya toraṇasya ca kecinmāsātphalaṃ prāhuḥ ||

96.12  atyantaviruddhānāṃ snehaḥ śabdaśca viyati bhūtānām|

96.12  mārjāranakulayormūṣakeṇa saṅgaśca māsen |
