96.15  pitryānmāsaḥ ṣaṭsaṭtrayo +ardhamaṣṭau ca triṣaḍekaikāḥ|

96.15  māsacatuṣke +aṣāḍhe sadyaḥ pākābhijittārā||

96.16  saptāṣṭavadhyardhaṃ trayastrayaḥ pañca caiva māsāḥ syuḥ|

96.16  śravaṇādīnāṃ pāko nakṣatrāṇāṃ yathāsaṅkhyam||

96.17 upaśamitaṃ vidhivaddvijaiśca śāntyā||



97 nakṣatrakarmaguṇādhyāyaḥ



97.01  śikhiguṇarasendriyānalaśaśiviṣayaguṇartupañcavasupakṣāḥ|

99 karaṇaguṇādhyāyaḥ



99.01  vavavālavakaulava[K.bavabālavakaulaba]taitilākhyagaravaṇijaviṣṭisaṃjñānām|

99.01  patayaḥ syurindrakamalajamitrāryamabhūśriyaḥ sayamāḥ||

99.02  kṛṣṇacaturdaśyardhāddhuvāṇi[K.ū.dhruvāṇi] śakuniścatuṣpadaṃ nāgam|

99.02  kiṃstughnamiti ca teṣāṃ kalivṛṣaphaṇimārutāḥ patayaḥ||

99.03 samprītimitravaraṇāni ca kaulave[K.kaulabe] syuḥ

100 nakṣatrajātakādhyāyaḥ



100.01  priyabhūṣaṇaḥ svarūpaḥ[K.surūpaḥ] subhago dakṣo +aśvinīṣu matimāṃśc

100.06  bahubhṛtyadhanī bhogī surapitṛbhakto mahodyamaḥ pitry

100.06  priyavāgdātā dyutimānaṭano nṛpasevako bhāgy |

100.08  dānto vaṇiktṛṣāluḥ[K.kṛpāluḥ] priyavāgdharmāśritaḥ svātau||

100.11  vaiśve vinītadhārmikabahumitrakṛtajñasubhagaśc |

100.12  śrīmān śravaṇe śrutavānudāradāro dhanānvitaḥ khyātaḥ|

100.12  dātāḍhyaśūragītapriyo dhaniṣṭhāsu dhanalubdhaḥ||

100.13  sphuṭavāgvyasanī ripuhā sāhasikaḥ śatabhiṣaksu durgrāhyaḥ|

100.13  bhadrapadāsūdvignaḥ strījitadhanapaṭuradātā c |

100.14  vaktā sukhī prajāvān jitaśatrurdhārmiko dvitīyāsu||

100.14  sampūrṇāṅgaḥ subhagaḥ śūraḥ śucirarthavān pauṣṇ |



101 rāśivibhāgādhyāyaḥ



101.01  aśvinyo +atha bharaṇyo bahulāpādaśca kīrtyate meṣaḥ|

101.01  vṛṣabho bahulāśeṣaṃ rohiṇyo +ardhaṃ ca mṛgaśirasaḥ||

101.02  mṛgaśiro +ardhaṃ raudraṃ punarvasor[K.punarvasorca] aṃśakatrayaṃ mithunaḥ[K.mithunam]|

101.02  pādaśca *punarvasutastiṣyaḥ śleṣā[K.punarvasoḥ satiṣyo +aśleṣā] ca karkaṭakaḥ||

101.03  siṃho +atha maghā pūrvā ca phalgunī pāda uttarāyāśc

101.03  tatpariśeṣaṃ hastaścitrādyardhaṃ ca kanyākhyaḥ||

101.04  taulini citrāntyārdhaṃ svātiḥ pādatrayaṃ viśākhāyāḥ|

101.04  alini viśākhāpādastathānurādhānvitā jyeṣṭhā||

101.05  mūlamaṣāḍhā pūrvā prathamaścāpyuttarāṃśako dhanvī|

101.05  makarastatpariṣeśaṃ śravaṇaḥ pūrvaṃ dhaniṣṭhārdham||

101.06  kumbho +antyadhaniṣṭhārdhaṃ śatabhiṣagaṃśatrayaṃ ca pūrvāyāḥ|

101.06  bhadrapadāyāḥ śeṣaṃ tathottarā revatī ca jhaṣaḥ||

101.07  aśvinīpitryamūlādyā meṣasiṃhahayādayaḥ|

101.07  viṣamarkṣānnivartante pādavṛddhyā yathottaram||



102  vivāhapaṭalādhyāyaḥ



102.01  mūrtau karoti dinakṛdvidhavāṃ kujaśca

102.01 rāhurvipannatanayāṃ ravijo daridrām|

102.01 śukraḥ śaśāṅkatanayaśca guruśca sādhvīm

102.01 āyuḥkṣayaṃ prakurute +atha vibhāvarīśaḥ||

102.02  kurvanti bhāskaraśanaiścararāhubhaumā

102.02 dāridryaduḥkhamatulaṃ niyataṃ dvitīy

102.02 vitteśvarīmavidhavāṃ guruśukrasaumyā

102.02 nārīṃ prabhūtatanayāṃ kurute śaśāṅkaḥ||

102.03  sūryendubhaumaguruśukrabudhāstṛtīye

102.03 kuryuḥ sadā bahusutāṃ dhanabhāginīṃ c

102.03 vyaktāṃ[K.vyaktaṃ] divākarasutaḥ subhagāṃ karoti

102.03 mṛtyuṃ dadāti niyamātkhalu saiṃhikeyaḥ||

102.04  svalpaṃ payaḥ sravati sūryasute caturthe

102.04 daurbhāgyamuṣṇakiraṇaḥ kurute śaśī c

102.04 rāhuḥ sapatnanam[K.sapatnyamū.sapatnam] api ca kṣitijo +alpavittaṃ[K.alpavittāṃ]

102.04 dadyādbhṛguḥ suraguruśca budhaśca saukhyam||

102.05  naṣṭātmajāṃ ravikujau khalu pañcamasthe[K.pañcamasthau]

102.05 candrātmajo bahusutā gurubhārgavau c

102.05 rāhurdadāti maraṇaṃ śanirugrarogaṃ

102.05 kanyā*vināśam[K.prasūtim] acirātkurute śaśāṅkaḥ||

102.06  ṣaṣṭhāśritāḥ śanidivākararāhujīvāḥ

102.06 kuryuḥ kujaśca subhagāṃ śvaśureṣu bhaktām|

102.06 candraḥ karoti vidhavāmuśanā daridrām

102.06 ṛddhāṃ śaśāṅkatanayaḥ kalahapriyāṃ c |
102.07  saurārajīvabudharāhuravīnduśukrāḥ

102.07 kuryuḥ prasahya khalu saptamarāśisaṃsthāḥ|

102.07 vaidhavyabandhanavadhakṣayamarthanāśa

102.07 vyādhipravāsamaraṇāni yathākrameṇ |

102.08  sthāne +aṣṭame gurubudhau niyataṃ viyogaṃ

102.08 mṛtyuṃ śaśī bhṛgusutaśca tathaiva rāhuḥ|

102.08 sūryaḥ karotyavidhavāṃ sarujāṃ[K.sarujaṃ] mahījaḥ

102.08 sūryātmajo dhanavatīṃ pativallabhāṃ c |

102.09  dharme sthitā bhṛgudivākarabhūmiputrā

102.09 jīvaśca dharmaniratāṃ śaśijastvarogām|

102.09 rāhuśca sūryatanayaśca karoti bandhyāṃ[K.vandhyāṃ]

102.09 kanyāprasūtimaṭanāṃ[K.aṭanaṃ] kurute śaśāṅkaḥ||

102.10  rāhurnabhaḥsthalagato[K.nabhasthalagato] vidhavāṃ karoti

102.10 pāpe ratāṃ dinakaraśca śanaiścaraśc

102.10 mṛtyuṃ kujo +artharahitāṃ kulaṭāṃ ca candraḥ

102.10 śeṣā grahā dhanavatīṃ subhagāṃ ca kuryuḥ||

102.11  āye ravirbahusutāṃ sadhanāṃ[K.dhaninīṃ] śaśāṅkaḥ

102.11 putrānvitāṃ kṣitisuto ravijo dhanāḍhyām|

102.11 āyuṣmatīṃ suraguruḥ śaśijaḥ samṛddhāṃ

102.11 rāhuḥ karotyavidhavāṃ bhṛgurarthayuktām||

102.12  ante gururdhanavatīṃ dinakṛddaridrāṃ

102.12 candro dhanavyayakarīṃ kulaṭāṃ ca rāhuḥ|

102.12 sādhvīṃ bhṛguḥ śaśisuto bahuputrapautrāṃ

102.12 pānaprasaktahṛdayāṃ ravijaḥ kujaśc |

102.13  gopairyaṣṭyāhatānāṃ khurapuṭadalitā yā tu dhūlirdinānte

102.13 sodvāhe sundarīṇāṃ vipuladhanasutārogyasaubhāgyakartrī|

102.13 tasmin kāle na carkṣaṃ na ca tithikaraṇaṃ naiva lagnaṃ na yogaḥ

102.13 khyātaḥ puṃsāṃ sukhārthaṃ śamayati duritānyutthitaṃ gorajastu||



103 grahagocarādhyāyaḥ



103.01  prāyeṇa sūtreṇa vinākṛtāni prakāśarandhrāṇi cirantanāni|

103.01  ratnāni śāstrāṇi ca yojitāni navairguṇairbhūṣayituṃ kṣamāṇi||

103.02  prāyeṇa gocaro vyavahāryo +atastatphalāni vakṣyāmi|

103.02  nānāvṛttairāryā[K.tanno] mukhacapalatvaṃ *kṣamadhvaṃ naḥ [K.kṣamantvāryāḥ]||

103.03  māṇḍavyagiraṃ śrutvā na madīyā rocate +atha vā naivam|

103.03  sādhvī tathā na puṃsāṃ priyā yathā syājjaghanacapalā||

103.04  sūryaḥ ṣaṭtridaśasthitastridaśaṣaṭsaptādyagaścandramā

103.04 jīvaḥ saptanavadvipañcamagato vakrārkajau ṣaṭtrigau|

103.04 saumyaḥ ṣaḍdvicaturdaśāṣṭamagataḥ sūrye[K.ū.sarve] +apyupānte śubhāḥ

103.04 śukraḥ saptamaṣaḍdaśarkṣasahitaḥ śārdūlavattrāsakṛt ||

103.05  janmanyāyāsado +arkaḥ kṣapayati vibhavān koṣṭharogādhvadātā

103.05 vittabhraṃśaṃ dvitīye diśati ca na sukhaṃ vañcanāṃ dṛgrujaṃ c

103.05 sthānaprāptiṃ tṛtīye *dhananicayamudā kalyakṛc[K.dhananicayamudākalyakṛc] cārihartā[K.hantā]

103.05 rogān datte[K.dhatte] caturthe janayati ca muhuḥ sragdharābhogavighnam||

103.06  pīḍāḥ syuḥ pañcamasthe savitari bahuśo rogārijanitāḥ

103.06 ṣaṣṭhe +arko hanti rogān kṣapayati ca ripūn śokāṃśca nudati|

103.06 adhvānaṃ saptamastho jaṭharagadabhayaṃ dainyaṃ ca kurut

103.06 ruktrāsau[K.rukkāsau] cāṣṭamasthe bhavati suvadanā na svāpi vanitā||

103.07  ravāvāpaddainyaṃ rugiti navame vitta[K.citta]ceṣṭāvirodho

103.07 jayaṃ prāpnotyugraṃ daśamagṛhage karmasiddhiṃ krameṇ

103.07 jayasthānaṃ[K.jayaṃ sthānaṃ] mānaṃ vibhavamapi caikādaśe roganāśaṃ

103.07 suvṛttānāṃ ceṣṭā bhavati saphalā dvādaśe netareṣām||

103.08  śaśī janmanyannapravaraśayanācchādanakaro

103.08 dvitīye mānārthān[K.mānārthau] glapayati savighnaśca bhavati|

103.08 tṛtīye vastrastrīdhanavijaya[K.nicaya]saukhyāni labhate

103.08 caturthe +aviśvāsaḥ śikhariṇi bhujaṅgena sadṛśaḥ||

103.09  dainyaṃ vyādhiṃ śucamapi śaśī pañcame mārgavighnaṃ

103.09 ṣaṣṭhe vittaṃ janayati sukhaṃ śatrurogakṣayaṃ c

103.09 yānaṃ mānaṃ śayanamaśanaṃ saptame vittalābhaṃ

103.09 mandākrānte phaṇini himagau ca aṣṭame bhīrna kasy |

103.10  navamagṛhago bandhodvegaśramodararogakṛd

103.10 daśamabhavane ca ājñākarmaprasiddhikaraḥ *śaśī[K.omitted]|

103.10 upacayasuhṛtsaṃyogārthapramodamupāntyago

103.10 vṛṣabhacaritān doṣānantye[K.ante] karoti ca[K.hi] savyayān||

103.11  kuje +abhighātaḥ prathame dvitīye narendrapīḍā kalahāridoṣaiḥ|

103.11  bhṛśaṃ ca pittānala*caurarogair[K.rogacaurair] upendravajrapratimo +api yaḥ syāt ||

103.12  tṛtīyagaścaurakumārakebhyo

103.12 bhaumaḥ sakāśātphalamādadhāti|

103.12 pradīptimājñāṃ dhanamaurṇikāni

103.12 dhātvākarākhyāni kila aparāṇi||

103.13  bhavati dharaṇije caturthage jvarajaṭharagadāsṛgudbhavaḥ|

103.13  kupuruṣajanitācca saṅgamātprasabhamapi karoti ca aśubham||

103.14  ripugadakopabhayāni pañcame

103.14 tanayakṛtāśca śuco mahīsut

103.14 dyutirapi na asya ciraṃ bhavetsthirā

103.14 śirasi kaperiva mālatī yathā[K.kṛtā]||

103.15  ripubhayakalahairvivarjitaḥ sakanakavidrumatāmra*kāmagaḥ[K.kāgamaḥ ū.cāgama]|

103.15  ripubhavanagate mahīsute kimaparavaktravikāramīkṣat |

103.16  kalatrakalahākṣirugjaṭhararogakṛtsaptame

103.16 kṣaratkṣatajarūkṣitaḥ kṣapita[K.kṣayita]vittamāno +aṣṭam

103.16 kuje navamasaṃsthite paribhavārthanāśādibhir

103.16 vilambitagatirbhavatyabaladehadhātuklamaiḥ||

103.17  daśamagṛhagate samaṃ mahīje vividhadhanāptirupāntyage jayaśc

103.17  janapadamupari sthitaśca bhuṅkte vanamiva ṣaṭcaraṇaḥ supuṣpitāgram||

103.18  nānāvyayairdvādaśage mahīsute

103.18 santāpyate +anarthaśataiśca mānavaḥ|

103.18 strīkopapittaiśca sanetravedanair

103.18 yo +api indravaṃśābhijanena garvitaḥ||

103.19  duṣṭavākyapiśunāhitabhedairbandhanaiḥ sakalahaiśca hṛtasvaḥ|

103.19  janmage śaśisute pathi gacchan svāgate +api kuśalaṃ na śṛṇoti||

103.20  paribhavo dhanagate dhanalabdhiḥ sahajage śaśisute hṛdayāptiḥ[K.ū.suhṛdāptiḥ]|

103.20  nṛpatiśatrubhayaśaṅkita*cito[K.citto] drutapadaṃ vrajati duścaritaiḥ svaiḥ||

103.21  caturthage svajanakuṭumbavṛddhayo

103.21 dhanāgamo bhavati ca śītaraśmij

103.21 sutasthite tanayakalatravigraho

103.21 niṣevate na ca rucirāmapi striyam||

103.22  saubhāgyaṃ vijayamatha unnatiṃ ca ṣaṣṭhe

103.22 vaivarṇyaṃ kalahamatīva saptame jñaḥ|

103.22 mṛtyusthe jayasuta[K.sutajaya]vastravittalābhā

103.22 naipuṇyaṃ bhavati matipraharṣaṇīyam||

103.23  vighnakaro navamaḥ śaśiputraḥ karmagato ripuhā dhanadaśc

103.23  sapramadaṃ śayanaṃ ca vidhatte tadgṛhado +atha *kathāṃ staraṇaṃ ca[K.kathāstaraṇaṃ]||

103.24  dhana*sutasukha[K.sukhasuta]yoṣinmitra*vāha[K.vāhya]āptituṣṭis

103.24 tuhinakiraṇaputre lābhage mṛṣṭavākyaḥ|

103.24 ripuparibhavarogaiḥ pīḍito dvādaśasthe

103.24 na sahati paribhoktuṃ mālinīyogasaukhyam||

103.25  jīve janmanyapagatadhanadhīḥ

103.25 sthānabhraṣṭo bahukalahayutaḥ|

103.25 prāpya arthe +arthān vyarirapi kurute

103.25 kāntāsyābje bhramaravilasitam||

103.26  sthānabhraṃśātkāryavighātācca tṛtīye

103.26 +anekaiḥ[K.naikaiḥ] kleśairbandhujanotthaiśca caturth

103.26 jīve śāntiṃ pīḍitacittaśca sa vinded[K.vinden]

103.26 naiva grāme nāpi vane mattamayūr |

103.27  janayati ca tanayabhavanamupagataḥ

103.27 parijanaśubhasutakarituragavṛṣān|

103.27 sakanakapuragṛhayuvativasanakṛn

103.27 maṇiguṇanikarakṛdapi vibudhaguruḥ||

103.28  na sakhīvadanaṃ tilakojjvalaṃ

103.28 na *ca vanaṃ[K.bhavanaṃ] śikhikokilanāditam|

103.28 hariṇaplutaśāvavicitritaṃ

103.28 ripugate manasaḥ sukhadaṃ gurau||

103.29  tridaśaguruḥ śayanaṃ ratibhogaṃ

103.29 dhanamaśanaṃ kusumānyupavāhyam|

103.29 janayati saptamarāśimupeto

103.29 lalitapadāṃ ca giraṃ dhiṣaṇāṃ c |

103.30  bandhaṃ vyādhiṃ cāṣṭame śokamugraṃ

103.30 mārga*kleśān[K.kleśaṃ] mṛtyutulyāṃśca rogān|

103.30 naipuṇyājñāputrakarmārthasiddhiṃ

103.30 dharme jīvaḥ śālinīnāṃ ca lābham||

103.31  sthānakalyadhanahā daśarkṣagas

103.31 tatprado bhavati lābhago guruḥ|

103.31 dvādaśe +adhvani vilomaduḥkhabhāg

103.31 yāti yadyapi naro rathoddhataḥ||

103.32  prathamagṛhopago bhṛgusutaḥ smaropakaraṇaiḥ

103.32 surabhimanojñagandhakusumāmbarairupacayam|

103.32 śayanagṛhāsanāśanayutasya ca anukurute

103.32 samadavilāsinīmukhasarojaṣaṭcaraṇatām||

103.33  śukre dvitīyagṛhage prasavārthadhānya

103.33 bhūpāla*sannata[K.sannati]kuṭumbahitānyavāpy

103.33 saṃsevate kusumaratnavibhūṣitaśca

103.33 kāmaṃ vasantatilakadyutimūrdhajo +api||

103.34  ājñārthamānāspadabhūtivastraśatrukṣayān daityagurustṛtīy

103.34  datte[K.dhatte] caturthaśca suhṛtsamājaṃ rudrendravajrapratimāṃ ca śaktim||

103.35  janayati śukraḥ pañcamasaṃstho guruparitoṣaṃ bandhujanāptim|

103.35  sutadhanalabdhiṃ mitrasahāyānanavasitatvaṃ ca aribaleṣu||

103.36  ṣaṣṭho bhṛguḥ paribhavarogatāpadaḥ

103.36 strīhetukaṃ janayati saptamo +aśubham|

103.36 yāto +aṣṭamaṃ bhavanaparicchadaprado

103.36 lakṣmīvatīmupanayati striyaṃ ca saḥ||

103.37  navame tu dharmavanitāsukhabhāg

103.37 bhṛguje +arthavastranicayaśca bhavet |

103.37 daśame +avamānakalahānniyamāt

103.37 pramitākṣarāṇyapi vadan labhat |

103.38  upāntyago bhṛgo sutaḥ suhṛddhanānnagandhadaḥ|

103.38  dhanāmbarāgamo +antyagaḥ sthirastu nāmbarāgamaḥ||

103.39  prathame ravije viṣavahnihataḥ svajanairviyutaḥ kṛtabandhuvadhaḥ|

103.39  paradeśamupaityasuhṛdbhavano vimukhārthasuto +aṭakadīnamukhaḥ||

103.40  cāravaśāddvitīyagṛhage dinakaratanaye

103.40 rūpasukhāpavarjitatanurvigatamadabalaḥ|

103.40 anyaguṇaiḥ kṛtaṃ vasucayaṃ tadapi khalu bhavaty

103.40 ambviva vaṃśapatrapatitaṃ na bahu na ca ciram||

103.41  sūryasute tṛtīyagṛhage dhanāni labhate

103.41 dāsaparicchadoṣṭramahiṣāśvakuñjarakharān|

103.41 sadmavibhūtisaukhyamamitaṃ gadavyuparamaṃ

103.41 bhīrurapi praśāstyadhiripūṃśca vīralalitaiḥ||

103.42  caturthaṃ gṛhaṃ sūryaputre +abhyupete

103.42 suhṛdvittabhāryādibhirviprayuktaḥ|

103.42 bhavatyasya sarvatra cāsādhu duṣṭaṃ

103.42 bhujaṅgaprayātānukāraṃ ca cittam||

103.43  sutadhanaparihīṇaḥ pañcamasthe

103.43 pracurakalahayuktaśca arkaputr

103.43 vinihataripurogaḥ ṣaṣṭhayāte

103.43 pibati ca vanitāsyaṃ śrīpuṭoṣṭham||

103.44  gacchatyadhvānaṃ saptame ca aṣṭame ca

103.44 hīnaḥ strīputraiḥ sūryaje dīnaceṣṭaḥ|

103.44 tadvaddharmasthe vairahṛdrogabandhair

103.44 dharmo +apyucchidyedvaiśvadevīkriyādyaḥ||

103.45  karmaprāptirdaśame +arthakṣayaśca

103.45 vidyākīrtyoḥ parihāniśca saur

103.45 taikṣṇyaṃ lābhe parayoṣārtha*lābhaś[K.lābhā]

103.45 ca antye[K.ante] prāpnotyapi śokormimālām||

103.46  api kālamapekṣya ca pātraṃ śubhakṛdvidadhātyanurūpam|
103.46  na madhau bahu kaṃ kuḍave vā[K.ca] visṛjatyapi meghavitānaḥ||

103.47  raktaiḥ puṣpairgandhaistāmraiḥ kanakavṛṣabakulakusumairdivākarabhūsutau

103.47 bhaktyā pūjyāvindurdhenvā sitakusumarajatamadhuraiḥ sitaśca madapradaiḥ|

103.47 kṛṣṇadravyaiḥ sauriḥ saumyo maṇirajatatilakakusumairguruḥ paripītakaiḥ

103.47 prītaiḥ pīḍā na syāduccādyadi patati viśati yadi vā bhujaṅgavijṛmbhitam||

103.48  śamayodgatāmaśubhadṛṣṭimapi vibudhaviprapūjayā|

103.48  śāntijapaniyamadānadamaiḥ sujanābhibhāṣaṇasamāgamaistathā||

103.49  ravibhaumau pūrvārdhe śaśisaurau kathayato +antyagau rāśeḥ|

103.49  sadasallakṣaṇamāryagītyupagītyoryathāsaṃkhyam||

103.50  ādau yādṛksaumyaḥ paścādapi tādṛśo bhavati|

103.50  upagītermātrāṇāṃ gaṇavatsatsamprayogo vā||

103.51  āryāṇāmapi kurute vināśamantargururviṣamasaṃsthaḥ|

103.51  gaṇa iva ṣaṣṭhe dṛṣṭaḥ sa[K.ca] sarvalaghutāṃ janaṃ[K.gato] nayati||

103.52  aśubhanirīkṣitaḥ śubhaphalo balinā balavān

103.52 aśubhaphalapradaśca śubhadṛgviṣayopagataḥ|

103.52 aśubhaśubhāvapi svaphalayorvrajataḥ samatām

103.52 idamapi gītakaṃ ca khalu narkuṭakaṃ ca yathā||

103.53  nīce +aribhe +aste *ca aridṛṣṭasya[K'str. aridṛṣṭasya] sarvaṃ vṛthā yat[K'str. yathā] parikīrtitam|

103.53  purato +andhasya iva kāminyāḥ savilāsakaṭākṣanirīkṣaṇam||

103.54  sūryasuto +arkaphalasamaścandrasutaśchandataḥ samanuyāti yathā|

103.54  skandhakamāryāgītirvaitālīyaṃ ca māgadhī gāthāryām||

103.55  sauro +arkaraśmi*yogāt[K.rāgāt] savikāro labdhavṛddhiradhikataram|

103.55  pittavadācarati nṛṇāṃ pathyakṛtāṃ na tu tathāryāṇām||

103.56  yādṛśena graheṇenduryuktastādṛgbhavetso +api|

103.56  manovṛttisamāyogādvikāra iva vaktrasy |

103.57  pañcamaṃ *laghu sarveṣu[K.sarvapādeṣu] saptamaṃ dvicaturthayoḥ|

103.57  yadvatślokākṣaraṃ tadvallaghutāṃ yāti duḥsthitaiḥ||

103.58  prakṛtyāpi laghuryaśca vṛttabāhye vyavasthitaḥ|

103.58  sa yāti gurutāṃ loke yadā syuḥ susthitā grahāḥ||

103.59  prārabdhamasusthitairgrahairyatkarmātmavivṛddhaye budhaiḥ[K.+abudhaiḥ]|

103.59  vinihanti tadeva karma tān vaitālīyamiva ayathākṛtam||

103.60  sausthityamavekṣya yo grahebhyaḥ kāle prakramaṇaṃ karoti rājā|

103.60  aṇunā api sa pauruṣeṇa vṛttasyāupacchandasikasya yāti pāram||

103.61  upacayabhavanopayātasya bhānordine kārayeddhematāmrāśvakāṣṭhāsthicarmāurṇikādridrumatvagnakhavyālacaurāyudhīyāṭavīkrūrarājopasevābhiṣekāuṣadhakṣaumapaṇyādigopālakāntāravaidyāśmakūṭāvadātābhivikhyātaśūrāhavaślāghya*yāyy[K.yājya]*agnikarmāṇi[K.agnikāryāṇi] siddhyanti lagnasthite vā ravau|

103.61 śiśirakiraṇavāsare tasya va apyudgame kendrasaṃsthe +atha vā bhūṣaṇaṃ śaṅkhamuktābjarūpyāmbuyajñekṣubhojyāṅganākṣīrasusnigdhavṛkṣakṣupānūpadhānyadravadravya*viprādhvagīta[K.viprāśvaśīta]kriyāśṛṅgikṛṣyādisenādhipākrandabhūpālasaubhāgyanaktañcaraślaiṣmikadravyamātulya[K.mātuṅga]puṣpāmbarārambhasiddhirbhavet ||

103.61 kṣititanayadine prasiddhyanti[K.prasidhyanti] dhātvākarādīni sarvāṇi kāryāṇi cāmīkarāgnipravālāyudhakrauryacauryābhighātāṭavīdurgasenādhikārāstathā raktapuṣpadrumā raktamanyacca tiktaṃ kaṭudravyakūṭāhipāśārjitasvāḥ kumārā bhiṣakśākyabhikṣukṣapāvṛttikośeśa[K.kauśeya]śāṭhyāni siddhyanti[K.sidhyanti] dambhāstathā||

103.61 harati[ū.harita, K.harita]maṇimahīsugandhīni vastrāṇi sādhāraṇaṃ nāṭakaṃ śāstravijñānakāvyāni sarvāḥ kalāyuktayo mantradhātukriyāvādanaipuṇyapuṇyavratāyogadūtāstathāyuṣyamāyānṛtasnānahrasvāṇi dīrghāṇi madhyāni ca chandataś[K.cchandanaś] caṇḍavṛṣṭiprayātānukārīṇi kāryāṇi siddhyanti[K.sidhyanti] saumyasya lagne +ahni vā||

103.62  suragurudivase kanakaṃ rajataṃ turagāḥ kariṇo vṛṣabhā bhiṣagauṣadhayaḥ[K.oṣadhayaḥ]

103.62 dvijapitṛsurakāryapuraḥsthitagharmanivāraṇacāmarabhūṣaṇabhūpatayaḥ|

103.62 vibudhabhavanadharmasamāśrayamaṅgalaśāstramanojñabalapradasatyagiraḥ

103.62 vratahavanadhanāni ca siddhikarāṇi tathā rucirāṇi ca varṇakadaṇḍakavat ||

103.63  bhṛgusutadivase ca citravastravṛṣyaveśyakāminīvilāsahāsayauvanopabhogaramyabhūmayaḥ

103.63 sphaṭikarajatamanmathopacāravāhanekṣuśāradaprakāragovaṇikkṛṣīvalāuṣadhāmbujāni c

103.63 savitṛsutadine ca kārayenmahiṣyajoṣṭrakṛṣṇalohadāsavṛddhanīcakarmapakṣicaurapāśikān

103.63 cyutavinayaviśīrṇabhāṇḍahastyapekṣavighnakāraṇāni ca anyathā na sādhayetsamudrago +apyapāṃ kaṇam||

103.64  vipulāmapi buddhvā chandovicitiṃ bhavati kāryametāvat |

103.64  śrutisukhadavṛttasaṃgrahamimamāha varāhamihiro +ataḥ||



104 rūpasatrādhyāyaḥ



104.01  pādau mūlaṃ jaṅghe ca rohiṇī jānunī[K.omitted] tathāśvinyaḥ|

104.01  ūrū ca āṣāḍhadvayamatha guhyaṃ phalgunī*dvitayam[K.yugmam]||

104.02  kaṭirapi ca kṛttikā pārśvayośca yamalā bhavanti bhadrapadāḥ|

104.02  kukṣisthā revatyo vijñeyamuro +anurādhā c |

104.03  pṛṣṭhaṃ viddhi dhaniṣṭhāṃ[K.dhaniṣṭhā] bhujau viśākhā[K.viśākhāṃ] smṛtau karau hastaḥ||

104.03  aṅgulyaśca punarvasurāśleṣāsaṃjñitāśca nakhāḥ||

104.04  grīvā jyeṣṭhā *śravaṇaṃ śravaṇau[K.śravaṇau śravanaḥ] puṣyo mukhaṃ dvijāḥ svātiḥ|

104.04  hasitaṃ śatabhiṣagatha nāsikā maghā mṛgaśiro netr |

104.05  citrā lalāṭasaṃsthā śiro bharaṇyaḥ śiroruhāścārdrā|

104.05  nakṣatrapuruṣako +ayaṃ kartavyo rūpamicchadbhiḥ||

104.06  caitrasya bahulapakṣe hyaṣṭamyāṃ mūlasaṃyute candr

104.06  hy[K.omitted] upavāsaḥ kartavyo viṣṇuṃ sampūjya dhiṣṇyaṃ c |

104.07  dadyādvrate samāpte ghṛtapūrṇaṃ bhājanaṃ suvarṇayutam|

104.07  viprāya kālaviduṣe saratnavastraṃ svaśaktyā c |

104.08  annaiḥ kṣīraghṛtotkaṭaiḥ saha guḍairviprān samabhyarcayed

104.08 dadyātteṣu suvarṇa[K.tathaiva]vastrarajataṃ lāvaṇyamicchannaraḥ|

104.08 pādarkṣātprabhṛti kramādupavasannaṅgarkṣanāmasvapi

104.08 kuryātkeśavapūjanaṃ svavidhinā dhiṣṇyasya pūjāṃ tathā||

104.09  pralambabāhuḥ pṛthupīnavakṣāḥ kṣapākarāsyaḥ sitacārudantaḥ|

104.09  gajendragāmī kamalāyatākṣaḥ strīcittahārī smaratulyamūrtiḥ||

104.10  śaradamalapūrṇacandradyutisadṛśamukhī sarojadalanetrā|

104.10  ruciradaśanā sukarṇā bhramarodarasannibhaiḥ keśaiḥ||

104.11  puṃskokilasamavāṇī tāmroṣṭī[K.ū.tāmroṣṭhī] padmapatrakaracaraṇā|

104.11  stanabhārānatamadhyā pradakṣiṇāvartayā nābhyā||

104.12  kadalīkāṇḍanibhoruḥ[K.ūrūḥ] suśroṇī varakukundarā subhagā|

104.12  suśliṣṭāṅgulipādā bhavati pramadā *manuṣyaśca[K.manuṣyo vā]||

104.13  yāvannakṣatramālā vicarati gagane bhūṣayantī iha bhāsā

104.13 tāvannakṣatrabhūto vicarati saha tairbrahmaṇo +ahno +avaśeṣam|

104.13 kalpādau cakravartī bhavati hi matimāṃstatkṣayāccāpi bhūyaḥ

104.13 saṃsāre jāyamāno bhavati narapatirbrāhmaṇo vā dhanāḍhyaḥ||

104.14  mṛgaśīrṣādyāḥ keśavanārāyaṇamādhavāḥ sagovindāḥ|

104.14  viṣṇumadhusūdanākhyau trivikramo vāmanaścaiv |

104.15  śrīdharanāmā tasmātsahṛṣīkeśaśca padmanābhaśc

104.15 ddāmodara ityete māsāḥ proktā yathāsaṃkhyaṃ||

104.16  māsanāma samupoṣito naro dvādaśīṣu vidhivatprakīrtayan|

104.16  keśavaṃ samabhipūjya tatpadaṃ yāti yatra nahi janmajaṃ bhayam||



105 upasaṃhārādhyāyaḥ



105.01  jyotiḥśāstrasamudraṃ pramathya matimandarādriṇā +atha mayā|

105.01  lokasya ālokakaraḥ śāstraśaśāṅkaḥ samutkṣiptaḥ||

105.02  pūrvācāryagranthā notsṛṣṭāḥ kurvatā mayā śāstram|

105.02  tānavalokyedaṃ ca prayatadhvaṃ kāmataḥ sujanāḥ||

105.03  atha vā kṛśam[K.bhṛśam] api sujanaḥ prathayati doṣārṇavādguṇaṃ dṛṣṭvā|

105.03  nīcastadviparītaḥ prakṛtiriyaṃ sādhvasādhūnām||

105.04  durjanahutāśataptaṃ kāvyasuvarṇaṃ viśuddhimāyāti|

105.04  śrāvayitavyaṃ tasmādduṣṭajanasya prayatnen |

105.05  granthasya yatpracarato +asya vināśameti

105.05 lekhyādbahuśrutamukhādhigamakrameṇ

105.05 yadvā mayā kukṛtamalpamihākṛtaṃ vā

105.05 kāryaṃ tadatra viduṣā parihṛtya rāgam||

105.06  dinakaramunigurucaraṇapraṇipātakṛtaprasādamatinedam|

105.06  śāstramupasaṅgṛhītaṃ namo +astu pūrvapraṇetṛbhyaḥ||



106 śāstrānukramaṇī



106.01  śāstropanayaḥ pūrvaṃ sāṃvatsarasūtramarkacāraśc

106.01  śaśirāhubhaumabudhagurusitamandaśikhigrahāṇāṃ c |

106.02  cāraścāgastyamuneḥ saptarṣīṇāṃ ca kūrmayogaśc

106.02  nakṣatrāṇāṃ vyūho grahabhaktirgrahavimardaśc |

106.03  grahaśaśiyogaḥ samyaggrahavarṣaphalaṃ grahāṇāṃ c

106.03  śṛṅgāṭasaṃsthitānāṃ meghānāṃ garbhalakṣaṇaṃ caiv |

106.04  dhāraṇavarṣaṇarohiṇivāyavyāṣāḍhabhadrapada[K.bhādrapada]yogāḥ|

106.04  kṣaṇavṛṣṭiḥ kusumalatāḥ sandhyācihnaṃ diśāṃ dāhaḥ||

106.05  bhūkampolkāpariveṣalakṣaṇaṃ śakracāpakhapuraṃ c

106.05  pratisūryo nirghātaḥ sasyadravyārghakāṇḍaṃ c |

106.06  indradhvajanīrājanakhañjanakotpātavarhi[K.barhi]citraṃ c

106.06  puṣyābhiṣekapaṭṭapramāṇamasilakṣaṇaṃ vāstu||

106.07  udak[K.udag]ārgalamārāmikamamarālayalakṣaṇaṃ kuliśalepaḥ|

106.07  pratimā vanapraveśaḥ surabhavanānāṃ pratiṣṭhā c |

106.08  cihnaṃ gavāmatha śunāṃ kukkuṭakūrmājapuruṣacihnaṃ c

106.08  pañcamanuṣyavibhāgaḥ strīcihnaṃ vastravicchedaḥ||

106.09  cāmaradaṇḍaparīkṣā strīstotraṃ cāpi subhagakaraṇaṃ c

106.09  kāndarpikānulepanapuṃstrikādhyāyaśayanavidhiḥ||

106.10  vajraparīkṣā mauktikalakṣaṇamatha padmarāgamarakatayoḥ|

106.10  dīpasya lakṣaṇaṃ dantadhāvanaṃ śākunaṃ miśram||

106.11  antaracakraṃ virutaṃ śvaceṣṭitaṃ virutamatha śivāyāśc |

106.11  caritaṃ mṛgāśvakariṇāṃ vāyasavidyottaraṃ ca tataḥ||

106.12  pāko nakṣatraguṇāstithikaraṇaguṇāḥ sadhiṣṇyajanmaguṇāḥ|

106.12  *gocaramatha[K.gocarastathā] grahāṇāṃ kathito nakṣatrapuruṣaśc |

106.13  śatamidamadhyāyānāmanuparipāṭikramādanukrāntam|

106.13  atra ślokasahasrāṇyābaddhānyūnacatvāri||
